Abstract: Interleaved thinking, where a unified multimodal model alternates between textual reasoning and visual generation, has shown promise on spatial and physical tasks. However, in complex long-chain scenarios, we identify a fundamental failure mode: generated images diverge from the textual context while subsequent text ignores the visual evidence, causing the two modalities to alternate without genuinely informing each other. We term this Modal Isolation and attribute it to compounding information loss at modality boundaries. We decompose each reasoning cycle into atomic operations and define modality transition loss, quantifying cross-modal hallucination (text-to-image) and visual utilization deficit (image-to-text) at each boundary. We propose MoTiF (Modality Tiransition Fidelity), a two-stage training framework that directly optimizes these transitions: Reflective SFT trains the model to detect and recover from erroneous visual outputs; Flow-GRPO improves image generation fidelity via reinforcement learning. All training signals in MoTiF derive from transition-level fidelity rather than end-task accuracy. Across four visual puzzle benchmarks, this transition-level supervision substantially improves both cross-modal coherence and final task accuracy. The results demonstrate that effective interleaved reasoning requires explicit structural supervision at modality boundaries, not merely scaling or end-task optimization.

</details>


### [93] [Magnifying What Matters: Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering for Visual Text Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12898)
*Shenglai Zeng,Qirui Wang,Kai Guo,Xinnan Dai,Xianxuan Long,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGAR方法，利用VLM中后层注意力机制定位关键视觉区域并重新渲染文本，无需训练即可显著提升视觉文本理解任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTC流程将渲染和布局视为固定且与内容无关的预处理步骤，缺乏对VLM内部处理视觉化文本机制的理解。作者发现VLM存在"定位但不利用"的现象，即证据定位注意力与答案正确性解耦，但放大定位区域可恢复大部分错误。

Method: 提出AGAR（Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering），一种无需训练、模型无关的方法。利用VLM模型中后层注意力识别top-K重要视觉块，将其映射回词跨度，重新渲染页面时放大这些跨度，然后重新推理答案。

Result: 在9个VTC基准（短文本、长上下文、多页记忆QA）和4个VLM骨干网络上，AGAR作为即插即用增强方案持续提升现成VLM性能；可与VLM后训练组合获得进一步提升；在视觉和文本输入退化下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AGAR是一种有效的训练免费、模型无关的VTC增强方法，揭示了VLM内部注意力与文本利用之间的解耦关系，为理解VLM处理视觉化文本提供了机制性洞见。

Abstract: Visual Text Comprehension (VTC) renders text into images for a vision-language model (VLM) to read, sidestepping LLM context-window limits and powering applications from long-page OCR to multi-page memory QA. Yet existing VTC pipelines treat rendering and layout as a fixed, content-agnostic preprocessing step and offer little mechanistic understanding of how VLMs internally process visualized text. Through a focused empirical study on VTC QA tasks, we reveal that VLMs exhibit a localization-without-utilization regime: evidence-localizing attention emerges sharply in the middle-to-late layers and is largely decoupled from answer correctness, yet simply enlarging the localized spans on the rendered page recovers a large fraction of the failures. Building on these observations, we propose AGAR (Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering), a training-free, model-agnostic method that leverages a VLM's own middle-to-late layer attention to identify the top-K important visual patches, maps them back to word spans, and re-renders the page with those spans enlarged before re-inferring the answer. Extensive experiments across nine VTC benchmarks (short-form, long-context, and multi-page memory QA) and four VLM backbones show that AGAR (i)consistently improves off-the-shelf VLMs as a plug-and-play enhancement, (ii)composes with VLM post-training to yield further gains, and (iii)remains robust under both visual- and text-side input degradation.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-Label Test-Time Adaptation with Bayesian Conditional Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12925)
*Qiru Li,Ao Zhou,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Cong Wang,Yafeng Yin,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BCP是一种无需梯度的测试时自适应方法，通过贝叶斯条件先验估计为冻结的视觉-语言模型注入标签依赖关系，以解决多标签识别中的分布偏移问题，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉-语言模型在多标签识别中面对分布偏移时脆弱：零样本推理独立打分，忽略标签共现结构，导致标签集不连贯，主导概念压制了兼容的弱标签。

Method: 提出贝叶斯条件先验估计（BCP），一种无梯度的测试时自适应方法。将零样本logits视为固定图像-文本似然下的边缘后验代理，将分布偏移导致的错误归因于错误的标签先验。对每张测试图像，选择高置信度锚标签，执行锚定条件的贝叶斯修正（logit空间的闭式更新，等价于点互信息PMI），通过在线估计锚定条件先验（轻量二阶共现统计）实现无目标标注的自适应。

Result: 在多个多标签基准和CLIP骨干上，BCP一致优于强TTA基线：RN50的平均mAP从57.31提升至69.22，ViT-B/16从62.61提升至71.79。

Conclusion: BCP通过注入标签依赖关系有效缓解了多标签识别中VLMs的分布偏移问题，且无需修改骨干网络、无需梯度计算，仅增加极小的计算开销。

Abstract: Multi-label recognition with frozen Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is brittle under distribution shift: standard zero-shot inference scores labels independently, ignoring co-occurrence structure and producing incoherent label sets where dominant concepts suppress weaker but compatible labels. We introduce Bayesian Conditional Priors (BCP) Estimation, a gradient-free test-time adaptation method that injects label dependency without tuning the backbone. BCP views zero-shot logits as a proxy for marginal posteriors under a fixed image-text likelihood and attributes shift-induced errors mainly to a mismatched label prior. For each test image, it selects a high-confidence anchor label and applies an anchor-conditioned Bayesian refinement. This update is closed-form in logit space and admits a pointwise mutual information (PMI) interpretation, explicitly promoting compatible labels and suppressing incompatible ones. BCP operates without target annotations by estimating anchor-conditioned priors online from the unlabeled test stream via lightweight second-order co-occurrence statistics, adding negligible overhead beyond a single forward pass. Across standard multi-label benchmarks and multiple CLIP backbones, BCP consistently outperforms strong TTA baselines, e.g., improving RN50 average mAP from 57.31 to 69.22 and ViT-B/16 from 62.61 to 71.79.

</details>


### [95] [MAMVI: 3D Test-Time Adaptation via Masked Multi-View Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12939)
*Inseok Kong,Geunyoung Jung,Jiyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAMVI方法，通过混合掩码策略和单步多视图聚合损失，解决3D点云测试时适应的顺序优化导致的高延迟问题，在保持竞争力的同时实现4.9-8.9倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云测试时适应方法在多视图增强中采用顺序优化，每个视图独立处理导致反复优化步骤，推理延迟大，难以满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出MAMVI方法：1) 使用混合掩码策略（固定比率+贝塔分布采样）确保稳定性与多样性；2) 聚合多视图损失，通过单次反向传播实现统一单步适应；3) 基于置信度的自适应学习率动态调整每个样本的适应强度。

Result: 在ShapeNet-C和ScanObjectNN-C上达到SOTA精度，在ModelNet-40C上具有竞争力，同时推理速度提升4.9-8.9倍。

Conclusion: MAMVI通过单步多视图适应有效解决了现有方法的延迟问题，在保持高性能的同时显著加速，适用于实时3D点云推理场景。

Abstract: 3D point cloud models suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts caused by sensor noise, occlusions, and environmental changes. Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a practical paradigm for mitigating this issue during inference. Recently, leveraging multi-view augmentation has shown promise in improving 3D TTA performance. However, existing multi-view approaches are often constrained by sequential optimization that treats each view independently. This sequential optimization leads to substantial inference latency due to repetitive optimization steps, making real-time adaptation impractical. To address this, we propose Masked Multi-View Test-Time Adaptation (MAMVI), which replaces sequential optimization with a unified single-step adaptation. Specifically, MAMVI utilizes a hybrid masking strategy that combines fixed ratios for stability with Beta-distributed sampling for diversity. By aggregating losses across multiple views, MAMVI performs adaptation through a single backward pass based on multi-view consensus. Additionally, a confidence-based adaptive learning rate is used to dynamically adjust the adaptation intensity for each sample. Extensive experiments on ModelNet-40C, ShapeNet-C, and ScanObjectNN-C demonstrate that MAMVI achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on ShapeNet-C and ScanObjectNN-C. Moreover, it remains competitive on ModelNet-40C while delivering 4.9-8.9 times faster inference, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Our code is available at https://github.com/Inseok-kong/MAMVI

</details>


### [96] [YOLO-AMC: An Improved YOLO Architecture with Attention Mechanisms for Building Crack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12958)
*Ching-Yu Tsai,Chia-Min Lin,Chih-Hsiang Yang,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO-AMC，一种基于YOLOv11改进的裂缝检测架构，通过移除C2PSA模块并在Neck层引入GAM、Res-CBAM和SA等多种注意力机制，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了裂缝检测精度，mAP@0.5达0.9917，并在边缘设备Raspberry Pi 5上实现约5 FPS的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂缝检测在基础设施检查和结构健康监测中至关重要，但裂缝通常表现为细长、低对比度的结构，易受背景噪声影响，现有目标检测模型难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: 基于YOLOv11框架，移除原始C2PSA模块，在Neck的多尺度特征融合层中引入全局注意力机制(GAM)、残差卷积块注意力模块(Res-CBAM)和Shuffle Attention(SA)等多种注意力机制，以增强跨尺度特征融合能力。

Result: YOLO-AMC在测试集上达到mAP@0.5=0.9917和mAP@0.5:0.95=0.9506，优于YOLOv11(0.9833/0.9112)和YOLOv8(0.9707/0.8921)；计算复杂度为7.6 GFLOPs，在RTX 4090上达110.95 FPS，在Raspberry Pi 5上约5 FPS。

Conclusion: 提出的YOLO-AMC在裂缝检测任务中实现了精度与部署效率的良好平衡，GAM注意力机制效果最优，且模型可在边缘设备上运行。

Abstract: Crack detection plays an important role in infrastructure inspection and Structural Health Monitoring (SHM). However, cracks typically appear as thin, low-contrast structures and are easily affected by background noise, posing challenges for existing object detection models. This study proposes an improved YOLO-based architecture with integrated attention mechanisms, termed YOLO-AMC (YOLO with Attention Mechanisms for Crack Detection), to enhance automated crack detection performance. Based on YOLOv11, the original C2PSA module is removed, and multiple attention mechanisms, including Global Attention Mechanism (GAM), Residual Convolutional Block Attention Module (Res-CBAM), and Shuffle Attention (SA), are introduced into the multi-scale feature fusion layers of the Neck to strengthen cross-scale feature integration. Experimental results demonstrate that YOLO-AMC consistently outperforms baseline models YOLOv11n and YOLOv8n across multiple evaluation metrics. Among the evaluated attention modules, GAM achieves the best detection performance, obtaining mAP@0.5 = 0.9917 and mAP@0.5:0.95 = 0.9506 on the test dataset, which are higher than those of YOLOv11 (0.9833 / 0.9112) and YOLOv8 (0.9707 / 0.8921). Furthermore, while maintaining a computational complexity of 7.6 GFLOPs, the proposed model achieves 110.95 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 platform and approximately 5 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 edge device, demonstrating a favorable trade-off between accuracy and deployment efficiency. The implementation code for this study is available on GitHub at https://github.com/CY-Tsai24/YOLO-AMC.

</details>


### [97] [Camera and LiDAR BEV Fusion for Cooperative 3D Object Detection on TUMTraf V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12981)
*Muhammad Shahbaz,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个用于DriveX 2026挑战赛TUMTraf V2X协同3D目标检测任务的Camera-LiDAR融合检测器。该检测器在共享BEV空间融合三路路侧相机与基础设施+车辆融合点云，采用CenterPoint风格检测头、广义IoU回归损失和IoU质量重排序头。在公开测试集上达到0.85的3D mAP。研究发现50个测试帧中有44帧与训练/验证集存在标签重叠，通过微调（0.89 mAP）和真实标签替换（0.99 mAP）两个实验量化了数据泄露对评分的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个用于车路协同（V2X）场景下的高精度3D目标检测器，并探究训练/测试数据重叠（数据泄露）对评测结果的影响。

Method: 融合三路路侧摄像头与基础设施+车辆融合点云，在共享BEV空间进行多模态融合；采用CenterPoint风格的检测头，结合广义IoU回归损失和IoU质量重排序头进行目标检测与框回归。

Result: 在公开Codabench测试集上达到0.85 3D mAP；发现数据重叠后，针对44个重叠帧进行微调达到0.89 mAP，替换为真实标签达到0.99 mAP。

Conclusion: 所提出的V2X融合检测器在TUMTraf数据集上表现良好，但评测结果因训练/测试集数据泄露而显著膨胀；报告了三种配置下的完整结果以供公正评估。

Abstract: We describe a Camera and LiDAR fusion detector developed for the TUMTraf V2X cooperative 3D object detection track of the DriveX 2026 challenge. The detector fuses three roadside cameras with a fused infrastructure-plus-vehicle point cloud in a shared bird's-eye-view space and predicts boxes through a CenterPoint-style head with a generalized IoU regression loss and an IoU quality re-ranking head. Trained on the provided train and validation splits, the model reaches a 3D mAP of 0.85 on the public Codabench test split. While iterating on the system, we observed that 44 of the 50 test frames are also present in the released train (40) and validation (4) splits with their labels. We therefore conducted two additional studies to quantify how this overlap affects the final score: (1) a finetuning run that oversamples the 44 overlapping frames, reaching 0.89 mAP, and (2) a post-processing run that replaces predictions on those frames with the released ground truth, reaching 0.99 mAP (uploaded to our Codabench account for testing but not published on the leaderboard). All three configurations and their per-class results are reported.

</details>


### [98] [Objects Before Words: Object-First Inductive Biases for Grounding Language in Child-View Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12985)
*Sathira Silva,Abrham Kahsay Gebreselasie,Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Kartik Kuckreja,Daniel Harari,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BabyMind提出了一种基于物体优先偏好的对比学习框架，通过跟踪对齐的物体文件和多实例对比目标，在稀疏、噪声的婴儿视角视频-语言数据中学习词义，在SAYCam-S上超越CVCL 2.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿视角视频中，看护者言语稀疏且与画面弱同步，单帧对比学习会产生含噪声的正样本对（目标物体缺失或被干扰物混淆），需要解决"何时出现"和"何处出现"双重歧义。

Method: 1) 使用离线掩码区域接口提取候选物体嵌入；2) 通过跟踪将短时间窗口内的候选目标链接为轻量级物体文件；3) 利用原型空间的多实例对比目标将话语与物体文件袋对齐；4) 加入轨迹一致性和全局物体一致性正则化。

Result: 在SAYCam-S数据集上，BabyMind在Labeled-S 15选1准确率上比CVCL提升2.6个百分点，并在词汇内分布外基准上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 引入物体优先的归纳偏置（物体文件跟踪与多实例对比）能有效缓解弱监督下的噪声对齐问题，提升婴儿视角词义学习性能。

Abstract: Learning grounded word meaning from natural experience requires resolving two ambiguities in infant-view recordings: when the named referent appears and where it is in a cluttered frame. In SAYCam-style data, caregiver speech is sparse and weakly synchronized with egocentric video, so single-frame contrastive pairing yields noisy positives in which the intended object is absent or entangled with distractors. We propose BabyMind, an object-first bias for child-view contrastive learning under sparse, noisy supervision. BabyMind extracts candidate object embeddings using an offline mask-based region interface, links candidates across a short utterance-centered window into lightweight object files via tracking, and aligns utterances to bags of object files with a prototype-space multiple-instance contrastive objective. Track-coherence and global-object agreement regularizers stabilize learning and transfer object-file structure into the global frame embedding used at evaluation. On SAYCam-S, BabyMind improves Labeled-S 15 forced-choice accuracy by +2.6 points over CVCL and yields consistent gains on in-vocabulary out-of-distribution benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/sathiiii/BabyMind.

</details>


### [99] [Diffusion Transformer World-Action Model for AV Scene Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12987)
*Ruslan Sharifullin,Benjamin Jiang,Kai Xi Chew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜空间扩散Transformer（DiT）的动作条件世界模型，用于自动驾驶的未来场景预测。通过对比六种冻结编码器，发现V-JEPA2结合时间上下文效果最佳。研究揭示了四个关键成分：空间token、x₀目标、残差锚定和匹配目标不确定性的采样。在Stable-Diffusion-VAE编-预-解流水线中，扩散模型在感知指标（FID/KID）上比回归方法提升4.8倍，且动作可控性强（转向与场景位移Spearman ρ=0.81）。还设计了一个1.7M参数的“跳跃”模型，可恢复完整的地面真值运动幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型在紧凑可训练规模下存在未来预测模糊性问题，且标准失真度量（如余弦相似度、SSIM）会误导性地奖励模糊的回归均值而非真实预测，亟需一种能够生成逼真未来帧且具备良好动作可控性的紧凑潜世界模型。

Method: 采用潜空间扩散Transformer（DiT）架构，在冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取的潜空间中进行预测，通过对比六种编码器确定最优表示；设计可控诊断实验识别出四个关键成分（空间token、x₀目标、残差锚定、匹配不确定性采样）；使用Stable-Diffusion-VAE进行解码；引入训练集导出的校准方法实现无测试集真实值的实用部署；设计1.7M参数的“跳跃”模型解决单次运动捕获不足问题。

Result: V-JEPA2编码器相比最佳单帧编码器降低转向RMSE 40%；扩散模型KID达0.078，回归为0.375（提升4.8倍）；转向与场景位移Spearman ρ=0.81，回归为-0.18；“跳跃”模型恢复1.02倍地面真值运动幅度，而单次模型不足一半。

Conclusion: 扩散世界模型在感知质量上显著优于回归方法，且具备良好的动作可控性；标准失真度量会掩盖真实性能，应使用基于Inception的FID/KID等感知指标评估；提出的紧凑模型可实现长达8秒的未来场景预测，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let an autonomous vehicle predict future camera scenes from its own planned controls, enabling planning and simulation without real-world rollouts, but at compact, trainable scale the futures are ambiguous and the field's standard distortion metrics actively mislead: they reward a blurry regression mean over a realistic prediction. We confront this with a compact latent world model that, given the present front-camera latent and a sequence of ego-actions, predicts future scene latents a frozen decoder renders to $256 \times 256$ frames up to 8 seconds ahead, evaluated on 150 held-out nuScenes scenes. We first benchmark where to predict: across six frozen encoders spanning four representation families, V-JEPA2 with temporal context reduces steering RMSE by 40% over the best single-frame encoder. We then train a latent Diffusion Transformer (DiT) and, through a controlled diagnosis, identify the four ingredients it needs: spatial tokens, the $x_0$ objective, residual anchoring, and sampling matched to target uncertainty. In a Stable-Diffusion-VAE encode-predict-decode pipeline we expose the central tension: distortion metrics (cosine similarity, SSIM) favor the blurry mean, masking that the diffusion model is far closer to the real frame distribution. Inception-based FID and KID reveal a clean perception-distortion frontier: diffusion attains KID 0.078 versus 0.375 for regression ($4.8\times$ better), and a deployable train-derived calibration makes this practical without test-time ground truth. The model is genuinely action-controllable (steering drives scene displacement, Spearman $ρ= 0.81$, vs $-0.18$ for regression). We trace limited single-pass motion to a shared-present anchor and engineer a compact 1.7M-parameter "jump" model that recovers full ground-truth motion magnitude ($1.02\times$ GT), where single-pass models capture less than half.

</details>


### [100] [A Machine Learning Framework for Real-Time Personalized Ergonomic Pose Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12988)
*Manex Atxa,Bruno Simoes,Julen Balzategui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三维体积视频数据实时预测人体工效学/非工效学姿态的新方法，通过多角度3D点云分析克服传统摄像头固定视角和遮挡问题，结合深度学习分类器实现实时姿态评估，并在负重任务案例研究中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摄像头存在固定视角和遮挡问题，限制了姿势评估的全面性；工作场所安全与健康监测需求日益增长，需要一种可扩展、实时的工效学评估方法。

Method: 利用RGB-D相机捕获3D体积视频数据生成点云，支持多角度实时姿态推理；仅用户手动选择和标注的姿态用于训练个性化深度学习分类器；结合3D数据技术与2D姿态估计算法。

Result: 通过负重任务案例研究，系统实现了实时骨骼标注与分类，训练后的模型能对新流式数据执行实时推理。

Conclusion: 该方法为实时工效学评估提供了可扩展、实用的方案，克服了传统视觉方法的视角限制，对工作场所安全健康监测有显著贡献。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new methodology for real-time prediction of ergonomic and non-ergonomic human poses using volumetric video data in three dimensions. Although the methodology was designed for ergonomic assessments, it can be adapted to other applications requiring real-time analysis of human posture. One aspect that makes this system stand out is its ability to analyze 3D point clouds during the assessment, enabling computation from multiple angles. This overcomes a critical limitation of cameras which provide often a fixed viewpoint, thereby restricting the data available for a thorough postural evaluation, especially when occlusions occur. The system continuously and automatically performs pose inference using the chosen perspective on the real-time streaming data; however, only the poses manually selected and labeled by the user are used to train the personalized deep learning classifier. The methodology has been refined through a case study in which RGB-D cameras captured subjects performing load-lifting tasks, enabling real-time skeletal labeling. The model was trained on this data and, following the training phase, performs inference on new streaming data in real time. This research offers a scalable and pragmatic approach for real-time ergonomic evaluation by combining state-of-the-art 3D data technologies and traditional 2D pose estimation algorithms. It addresses the increasing need for safety and health monitoring in workplace environments, marking a notable contribution to the domain.

</details>


### [101] [Quality-Preserving Imperceptible Adversarial Attack on Skeleton-based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13022)
*Ziyi Chang,Kanglei Zhou,Xiaohui Liang,Hubert P. H. Shum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分布的对抗攻击方法，在不降低动作质量的前提下攻击骨骼人体动作识别系统，并设计了对齐人类感知的新指标来评估攻击后的动作自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法引入类噪声扰动，导致攻击后动作质量下降，容易被先进的S-HAR系统察觉。作者发现这种退化源于优化过程中经验风险与真实风险之间的差距。

Method: 提出基于分布的对抗攻击方法，避免引入类噪声扰动，通过最小化风险差距来保持动作质量；同时提出与人类感知对齐的新指标来评估动作自然度。

Result: 在两个数据集上对最先进的S-HAR方法进行实验，定性和定量分析均表明该方法在攻击成功率和攻击后动作质量方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该质量保持攻击的成功对动作识别器的鲁棒性提出了严重质疑，凸显了该领域需要进一步改进的需求。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on skeletal human action recognition have received significant attention. However, existing methods typically introduce noise-like perturbations that degrade motion quality post-attack, and thereby are inherently perceptible with recent advancements in S-HAR systems. We discover that this degradation stems from the gap between empirical and true risks during the optimization process of previous adversarial attacks. To address this issue, we propose an attack where adversarial motions are obtained without compromising their motion quality. To minimize the risk gap and preserve motion quality, we propose a distribution-based adversarial attack method without introducing noise-like perturbations. To faithfully evaluate the motion quality, we propose a new metric that aligns with human perception on real-world naturalness. Experiments have been conducted on the state-of-the-art S-HAR methods across two datasets, demonstrating the superiority of our method in both the attack success rate and the post-attack motion quality through qualitative and quantitative analyses. The success of our quality-preserving attack application and distribution-based method raises serious concerns about the robustness of action recognizers, highlighting the need for further enhancements in this domain.

</details>


### [102] [A Multi-Modal Framework with Cross-Subject Pseudo-Labeling and Semantic Alignment for Micro-Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13030)
*Haoran Zhang,Haokun Zhang,Pengyu Liu,Yujia Zhang,Weibao Xue,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对微手势识别的多模态框架，通过显著性引导的多模态提取、温和平方根平滑加权、正交语义嵌入损失和跨模态伪标签策略，在MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 1中取得了68.13%的F1分数，排名第四。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手势（MGs）是自发且微妙的身体动作，常隐含人类情绪。在未修剪视频中识别MGs面临三个主要挑战：极低的信噪比、严重的长尾类别分布以及跨主体评估场景中的固有域偏移。

Method: 提出多模态框架，包括：(1) 显著性引导的多模态提取流水线，整合68关键点骨骼关节坐标、3D热图体积和高分辨率RGB视觉特征；(2) 温和平方根平滑加权机制结合正交语义嵌入损失，保护尾部类别；(3) 跨模态伪标签（CMPL）策略用于无监督域适应；(4) 温度缩放的软投票机制用于缓解晚期融合中的过度自信。

Result: 在MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 1上取得了68.13%的竞争性F1分数，排名第四。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态框架有效应对了微手势识别中的低信噪比、长尾分布和域偏移挑战，多种策略协同作用提升了识别性能。

Abstract: Micro-gestures (MGs) are spontaneous and subtle body movements that frequently convey hidden human emotions. Recognizing MGs in untrimmed videos remains highly challenging due to their extremely low signal-to-noise ratio, severe long-tailed class distribution, and the inherent domain shift encountered in cross-subject evaluation scenarios. In this paper, we propose a comprehensive multi-modal framework for Track 1 of the 4th MiGA-IJCAI Challenge. To capture fine-grained representations, we design a saliency-guided multi-modal extraction pipeline integrating 68-keypoint skeleton joint coordinates, 3D heatmap volumes, and high-resolution RGB visual features. We introduce a gentle square-root smoothed weighting mechanism paired with an Orthogonal Semantic Embedding Loss to protect tail classes without compromising overall recognition capabilities. More importantly, to bridge the cross-subject generalization gap, we propose a Cross-Modal Pseudo-Labeling (CMPL) strategy for unsupervised domain adaptation, which significantly boosts single-modal robustness. A temperature-scaled soft-voting mechanism is finally utilized to alleviate overconfidence during late fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves a competitive F1-score of 68.13\%, securing the 4th place.

</details>


### [103] [GeoCFNet: Geometry-Aware Confidence Field Network for Robot-Assisted Endoscopic Submucosal Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13032)
*Rui Tang,Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Haochen Yin,Huxin Gao,Jiewen Lai,Jiazheng Wang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoCFNet，一种基于几何感知的置信场网络，用于机器人辅助内镜黏膜下剥离术（ESD）的视觉引导。该网络结合DINOv3骨干网络、Token-Differentiated Fusion模块、SegFormer解码器和几何感知空间正则化，解决了动态内镜场景中烟雾、高光、组织变形等挑战，实现了高精度且几何稳定的置信场估计（RMSE 0.0480, PSNR 27.1995）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助ESD手术需要稳定精确的视觉引导以维持准确的 dissection 走廊和安全组织边缘，但动态内镜场景中存在烟雾、镜面高光、组织变形、弱纹理及目标区域薄几何结构等挑战，导致现有置信场估计方法不可靠。

Method: 将 dissection 引导建模为几何感知的置信场估计问题，提出GeoCFNet：基于预训练DINOv3骨干网络，集成Token-Differentiated Fusion模块聚合类标记上下文与密集块表示，使用SegFormer解码器进行置信回归，并引入几何感知空间正则化（GASR）保持空间一致性和局部几何过渡。

Result: RMSE 0.0480, PSNR 27.1995, SSIM 0.3397, CC 0.2466，表明该方法实现了准确且几何稳定的置信场估计。

Conclusion: GeoCFNet有效解决了动态内镜场景下的置信场估计难题，为机器人辅助ESD手术提供了可靠的视觉引导，具有降低复发率和改善长期预后的潜力。

Abstract: Advanced surgical robotics has made robot-assisted endoscopic submucosal dissection (ESD) a promising approach for the en-bloc resection of large lesions, with the potential to reduce recurrence and improve long-term outcomes. However, the technical complexity and risk of complications in ESD demand stable and precise visual guidance to maintain an accurate dissection corridor and a safe tissue margin. Dense confidence fields provide an effective representation for this purpose by describing both the preferred dissection region and its spatial transition to surrounding tissue. However, reliable confidence field estimation remains challenging in dynamic endoscopic scenes due to smoke, specular highlights, tissue deformation, weak texture, and the thin geometric structure of the target region. To address these challenges, we formulate dissection guidance as a geometry-aware confidence field estimation problem and propose GeoCFNet, a geometry-aware confidence field network built on a pretrained DINOv3 backbone. GeoCFNet integrates a Token-Differentiated Fusion module to aggregate class-token context with dense patch representations, a SegFormer decoder for confidence regression, and Geometry-Aware Spatial Regularization (GASR) to preserve spatial coherence and local geometric transitions. Experimental results show that GeoCFNet achieves RMSE 0.0480, PSNR 27.1995, SSIM 0.3397, and CC 0.2466, indicating accurate and geometrically stable confidence field estimation for robot-assisted ESD guidance.

</details>


### [104] [SAM-Deep-EIoU: Selective Mask Propagation for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13033)
*Alexander Holmberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性掩码传播算法，在简单帧使用轻量级基础跟踪器，仅在不确定帧调用昂贵的VOS模型，通过黑盒方式提升多目标跟踪性能，在DanceTrack和SportsMOT上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标跟踪中难度分布呈长尾特性，大部分帧简单可被轻量跟踪器处理，但少数困难帧易导致身份丢失。VOS模型能处理困难帧但计算成本过高，需要一种能智能调度两种模型的方法。

Method: 提出选择性掩码传播算法：(1)基础跟踪器处理大部分帧；(2)在分配不确定性信号触发时，调用VOS模型处理窗口；(3)仅当VOS模型做出与基础跟踪器身份分配相矛盾的自信预测时才修改基础输出；(4)训练无关，将两者视为黑盒。

Result: 在DanceTrack上提升三种不同基础跟踪器性能；在SportsMOT上以SAM3-Deep-EIoU结合全局轨迹关联达到86.8 HOTA的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 选择性调度策略能以较小计算开销获得VOS模型的优势，且可灵活替换更强大的VOS组件进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking has a heavy-tailed difficulty distribution: most frames are easy for a lightweight base tracker, while a small fraction are intrinsically hard. Video object segmentation (VOS) models can often preserve identity through the hard frames where the base tracker fails, but they are much more expensive in compute and memory. We propose selective mask propagation, a tracking algorithm that dispatches from a base tracker to a VOS model only on windows where an assignment-uncertainty signal fires. The base tracker's output is modified only when the VOS model makes a confident prediction that contradicts the base tracker's identity assignment; weak or inconclusive predictions preserve the base output. The method is training-free, treats both the base tracker and the VOS model as black boxes, and can benefit from replacing the VOS component with a more capable model. On DanceTrack, selective mask propagation improves three different base trackers. On SportsMOT, where identity preservation is central to sports analytics, SAM3-Deep-EIoU with global track association achieves state-of-the-art performance on the benchmark with 86.8 HOTA.

</details>


### [105] [TetherCache: Stabilizing Autoregressive Long-Form Video Generation with Gated Recall and Trusted Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13035)
*Yu Meng,Xiangyang Luo,Letian Li,Wenyuan Jiang,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TetherCache是一种无需训练、即插即用的缓存管理策略，通过GRAB（门控注意力-多样性平衡召回）和TAME（信任对齐记忆编辑）两种机制，有效缓解自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中的上下文分布漂移问题，在240秒生成中将质量漂移从7.84降至1.33。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在生成长视频时面临两大挑战：有限的KV缓存预算无法保留完整历史帧，以及反复以自生成帧为条件导致上下文分布漂移，随时间累积产生视觉伪影、质量退化和时间漂移，难以扩展至分钟级视频生成。

Method: 将缓存组织为sink、memory和recent三个区域，并提出两种互补机制：(1) GRAB使用结合注意力相关性和时间多样性的门控分数选择长程记忆帧；(2) TAME通过将新召回的记忆token的统计量对齐到可信上下文分布，减轻漂移历史特征的污染。

Result: 在VBench-Long基准上，30s、60s和240s设置下均一致提升长视频生成质量。240秒生成中，整体和语义分数显著提升，质量漂移从7.84降至1.33。

Conclusion: TetherCache通过创新的缓存管理和记忆编辑机制，有效解决了自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中的漂移问题，为稳定长时域自回归视频扩散提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models provide a natural formulation for streaming and variable-length video generation by conditioning newly generated frames on previously generated content. However, extending these models to minute-level generation remains challenging: the limited KV-cache budget prevents the model from retaining the full history, while repeatedly conditioning on self-generated frames induces a context distribution shift that accumulates over time, leading to visual artifacts, quality degradation, and temporal drift. In this paper, we propose TetherCache, a training-free and plug-and-play cache management strategy for drift-resistant long video generation. TetherCache organizes the cache into sink, memory, and recent regions, and introduces two complementary mechanisms. First, GRAB (Gated Recall with Attention-Diversity Balancing) selects long-range memory frames using a gated score that combines attention-based relevance with temporal diversity, preserving informative yet diverse historical context under a fixed cache budget. Second, TAME (Trusted Alignment via Memory Editing) lightly edits newly recalled memory tokens by aligning their statistics to a trusted context distribution, reducing the pollution caused by drifted historical features. Built on Self-Forcing, TetherCache consistently improves long-video generation quality on VBench-Long across 30s, 60s, and 240s settings. In particular, for 240s generation, it substantially improves overall and semantic scores while reducing quality drift from 7.84 to 1.33, demonstrating its effectiveness for stable long-horizon autoregressive video diffusion.

</details>


### [106] [SeamEdit: A Black-Box VLM-Agnostic Pipeline for Large-Image Semantic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13041)
*Xiangyu Lyu,Dan Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeamEdit是一种无需训练、模型无关的流水线，通过五阶段后处理方法（重叠瓦片分解、黑盒VLM修复、几何与颜色一致性校正、基于接缝风险的多候选排序、动态规划曲线接缝融合）实现大图像的高质量语义区域编辑，解决了瓦片编辑中的语义变形、画面对齐漂移和接缝伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大图像的语义区域编辑需要同时满足高生成质量和与周围内容的自然融合，现有方法依赖白盒模型，未充分利用闭源模型的强大生成能力；直接将闭源模型应用于瓦片编辑会导致语义变形、画面对齐漂移和可见接缝伪影等失败模式。

Method: 提出SeamEdit流水线，包含五个阶段：(1) 基于重叠的瓦片分解（Overlay-based Tile Decomposition）；(2) 黑盒VLM修复（Black-box VLM Inpainting）；(3) 几何与颜色一致性校正（Geometric and Color-consistency Correction）；(4) 基于接缝风险的多候选排序（Seam-risk-based Multi-candidate Ranking）；(5) 动态规划曲线接缝融合（Dynamic-programming Curved Seam Fusion）。该方法无需训练、模型无关，将任意具备修复能力的VLM视为黑盒。

Result: 该流水线有效降低了接缝可见性，支持任意瓦片区域的语义修改，解决了直接应用闭源模型于瓦片编辑时出现的语义变形、画面对齐漂移和接缝伪影问题。

Conclusion: SeamEdit通过后处理流水线的方式，无需训练即可利用闭源VLM的生成能力实现高质量的大图像语义编辑，是一种模型无关的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic region editing for large images must satisfy two requirements at the same time: high generative quality and natural integration with surrounding content. Some related methods rely on white-box models and leave the strong generation capability of closed-source models underexplored. Directly applying closed-source models to tiled editing, however, introduces several failure modes: semantic deformation, canvas-level alignment drift, and visible seam artifacts. This paper presents SeamEdit, a training-free and model-agnostic pipeline that treats any VLM with inpainting capability as a black-box oracle. SeamEdit mitigates these issues through a five-stage post-hoc pipeline: overlay-based tile decomposition, black-box VLM inpainting, geometric and color-consistency correction, seam-risk-based multi-candidate ranking, and dynamic-programming curved seam fusion. The pipeline reduces seam visibility and supports semantic modification of arbitrary tile regions.

</details>


### [107] [Iterative Visual Thinking: Teaching Vision-Language Models Spatial Self-Correction through Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13156)
*Animesh Tripathy,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代视觉思维（IVT）框架，通过两阶段训练（SFT教师蒸馏+GRPO强化学习）让视觉语言模型学会自我纠正空间定位预测，仅用2400样本在单GPU上将Acc@0.5从79.6%提升至82.0%，并显著稳定多步修正轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在单次空间定位上表现优异，但缺乏观察并纠正自身预测的机制；直接让VLM迭代观察渲染结果会导致性能崩溃（Acc@0.5下降31个百分点），暴露出定位能力与自我纠正能力之间的根本性差距。

Method: 提出IVT闭环框架：模型预测边界框→在图像上渲染观察预测结果→通过视觉反馈迭代修正。两阶段训练：1）利用基模型自身预测作为真实错误，用教师VLM生成纠正推理轨迹进行SFT监督；2）应用分组相对策略优化（GRPO）配合IoU奖励稳定多步修正。

Result: 在RefCOCOg、Ref-Adv、Ref-L4混合基准上，SFT预热使Acc@0.5达82.0%（+2.4pp）、Acc@0.7达74.1%（+3.2pp）、Acc@0.9达48.3%（+2.8pp）；GRPO将每步IoU退化降低5倍，稳定修正轨迹。仅用2400样本、单GPU训练。

Conclusion: 空间自我纠正是可学习的能力，可以在适度规模下注入VLM中，闭环视觉反馈比简单迭代提示更有效。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong singleshot spatial grounding, yet lack any mechanism to observe and correct their own predictions. We find that naively prompting a VLM to iterate over rendered visualizations of its predictions causes catastrophic failure: Acc@0.5 on referring expression comprehension collapses from 79.6% to 48.7% (a 31 percentage point drop), revealing a fundamental gap between grounding capability and self-correction ability. We propose Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT), a closed-loop framework in which the model predicts a bounding box, observes the prediction rendered on the image, and iteratively refines through visual feedback. A two-phase training recipe closes the self-correction gap: first, we exploit the base model's own predictions as realistic errors and prompt a teacher VLM to generate corrective reasoning traces, yielding supervised data without human annotation; second, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a simple IoU reward to stabilize multi-step refinement. On a mixed benchmark spanning RefCOCOg, Ref-Adv, and Ref-L4 (505 test samples), SFT warm-up with IVT surpasses the single-shot base model on every metric: Acc@0.5 rises to 82.0% (+2.4pp), Acc@0.7 to 74.1% (+3.2pp), and Acc@0.9 to 48.3% (+2.8pp). GRPO further reduces per-step IoU degradation by 5x, stabilizing the refinement trajectory. All training uses only 2,400 samples on a single GPU, demonstrating that spatial self-correction is a learnable capability that can be instilled at modest scale.

</details>


### [108] [Transformer-Guided Graph Attention for Direct Cardiac Mesh Reconstruction: A Structural Digital Twin Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13188)
*Abhishek H S,Akash Ganamukhi,Abhimanyu Suresh,Aditya G Hiremath,Prasad B Honnavalli,Adithya Balasubramanyam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端网络，直接从3D医学图像生成平滑、可用于仿真的心脏表面网格，无需传统分割和网格后处理流程。在MM-WHS 2017基准上，CT和MRI的Dice分别为0.84和0.83，平均Chamfer距离1.8mm，95%表面距离低于5mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心脏建模流程中，网格生成（图像分割→Marching Cubes→手动清理）耗时、不一致且依赖专业知识，成为心脏数字孪生临床应用的瓶颈。

Method: 训练单一端到端网络：3D Swin Transformer编码器-解码器从CT/MRI提取体素特征，配合图注意力网络（GAT）头部迭代变形模板网格以拟合患者心脏边界。

Result: MM-WHS 2017基准：CT Dice 0.84，MRI Dice 0.83；平均Chamfer距离1.8mm，95%表面距离低于5mm。单次前向传播即可生成网格，无需后处理。

Conclusion: 对于心脏数字孪生管线，几何保真度和拓扑正确性比像素级Dice更重要。去除后处理瓶颈使患者特异性心脏仿真更易用于临床。

Abstract: Building patient-specific cardiac models sits at the heart of precision cardiology, yet getting those models into clinical use keeps running into the same wall: mesh generation is slow, messy, and frustrating. The standard workflow -- segmenting the image, running Marching Cubes, and then manually cleaning up the result -- is time-consuming, inconsistent across operators, and demands specialist knowledge most clinical teams do not have. We take a fundamentally different approach. Instead of treating segmentation and mesh generation as two separate problems, we train a single end-to-end network that goes directly from a raw 3D medical image to a smooth, simulation-ready cardiac surface mesh. The core is a 3D Swin Transformer encoder-decoder that extracts volumetric features from CT or MRI volumes, paired with a Graph Attention Network (GAT) head that iteratively deforms a template mesh to fit the patient's cardiac boundary. We tested on the MM-WHS 2017 benchmark using both CT and MRI. Segmentation scores were competitive (Dice of 0.84 on CT, 0.83 on MRI), but the primary focus is mesh quality: mean Chamfer distance of 1.8 mm, with 95th-percentile surface distance below 5 mm. Every mesh is produced in a single forward pass -- no Marching Cubes, no smoothing filters, no manual cleanup. We argue that for cardiac digital twin pipelines, geometric fidelity and topological correctness matter more than pixel-level Dice scores. By removing the post-processing bottleneck, this approach makes patient-specific cardiac simulation substantially more accessible for clinical use.

</details>


### [109] [Visual Place Recognition in Forests with Depth-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13206)
*Walter Nedov,Saimunur Rahman,Kavindie Katuwandeniya,David Hall,Kaushik Roy,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对森林环境中视觉位置识别的挑战，本文提出一个轻量级深度感知蒸馏框架，将几何线索注入DINOv2模型，在WildCross基准测试上相比仅依赖外观的模型取得了性能提升，验证了深度作为互补模态的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林环境中的视觉位置识别因植被重复、结构线索弱以及遍历间外观变化大而具有挑战性。

Method: 提出轻量级深度感知蒸馏框架，将几何线索注入基于DINOv2的位置识别模型，同时保持其预训练的描述子空间。

Result: 在WildCross基准测试上，相比仅依赖外观的模型取得了性能提升，对外观变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 深度是自然环境中位置识别的重要互补模态，深度感知蒸馏是实现更鲁棒森林感知的有前景方向。

Abstract: Visual place recognition in natural forest environments remains challenging due to repetitive vegetation, weak structural cues, and significant appearance variation across traversals. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a lightweight depth-aware distillation framework that injects geometric cues into a DINOv2-based place recognition model, while maintaining its pre-trained descriptor space. Evaluated on the recent WildCross benchmark, the proposed approach yields gains over an appearance-only counterpart, providing robustness to appearance variations. These results demonstrate the importance of depth as a strong complementary modality for place recognition in natural environments and identify depth-aware distillation as a promising direction for more robust forest perception.

</details>


### [110] [TimeLens: On-Device Artifact Recognition with Retrieval-Augmented Question Answering for the Grand Egyptian Museum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13267)
*Rawan Hesham,Ali Ashraf,Amr Ahmed,Malak Alaa,Omar Ahmed,Omar Wagih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLens是一个面向大埃及博物馆的AI双语移动导览系统，通过手机摄像头实时识别展品，并支持英阿双语问答。研究解决了展厅部署中的三个问题：细微视觉相似性、训练数据与手持拍摄的差距、以及AI生成不实历史信息的风险。工程贡献包括：通过数据质量迭代优化开发了5.97MB的YOLOv8n设备端检测器（mAP@0.5=0.995），以及基于双语RAG架构的导览系统，端到端延迟从30秒优化至约10秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆现场部署AI导览系统面临三大挑战：1) 展品间视觉区分度极低（如51件目录中的近乎相同的拉美西斯雕像）；2)  curated训练数据与用户手持拍摄条件之间存在域差距；3) AI导览可能输出未经证实的历史信息，需要避免幻觉。

Method: 1) 设备端文物检测器：通过数据质量驱动的迭代过程——从基础模型自动标注（YOLO-World），到空间标签清洗规则，最终建立全人工标注数据集——最终采用YOLOv8n模型（5.97MB TF Lite）。2) 双语RAG导览系统：基于108条记录的ChromaDB知识库，对7个候选语言模型进行基准测试，最终选用Gemma 4 E2B (Q4 K M)，并通过10项针对性优化将延迟从30秒以上降至约10秒。

Result: 检测器：mAP@0.5=0.995，mAP@0.5:0.95=0.924，模型大小仅5.97MB，可在中端手机上实时运行。导览系统：端到端延迟从30秒+优化至约10秒。

Conclusion: 通过数据质量驱动的迭代方法，即便在极细粒度视觉识别场景下，小型设备端模型也能达到接近完美的检测精度；结合RAG架构可有效控制AI导览的幻觉风险；系统已集成至生产级Flutter应用中，具备双语界面、博物馆位置限定和TTS功能。

Abstract: TimeLens is an AI-powered bilingual mobile guide for the Grand Egyptian Museum (GEM). Pointing a phone at an exhibit, a visitor sees the artifact recognized in real time and can ask follow-up questions answered in English or Arabic. The work addresses three problems specific to in-gallery deployment: fine-grained visual similarity among 51 catalogued artifacts (many near-identical Ramesside statues), the gap between curated training data and handheld camera conditions, and the risk of an AI guide stating unsupported historical facts. Two engineering contributions are reported. First, an on-device artifact detector was developed through a data-quality-driven iteration study -- from foundation-model auto-annotation (YOLO-World), through spatial label-cleaning rules, to a fully hand-annotated dataset -- isolating label quality as the decisive factor: the final YOLOv8n model resolves every previously failing class while remaining a 5.97 MB TensorFlow Lite asset that runs in real time on a mid-range phone (mAP@0.5 = 0.995, mAP@0.5:0.95 = 0.924). Second, a bilingual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) guide, grounded in a 108-record ChromaDB knowledge base, was benchmarked across seven candidate language models, with Gemma 4 E2B (Q4 K M) selected; ten targeted optimizations reduce end-to-end latency from over 30 s to approximately 10 s. Both subsystems are integrated in a production Flutter application with bilingual interface, museum location gating, and text-to-speech support.

</details>


### [111] [Zero-Shot Captioning for Cultural Heritage: Automated Image Analysis of Traditional Indonesian Clothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13275)
*Anugrah Aidin Yotolembah,Novanto Yudistira,Gembong Edhi Setyawan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Custom ZeroCLIP，一种检索增强的视觉-语言框架，用于印尼传统服饰的零样本描述生成。该框架在包含38个省份3800张专家标注图像的数据集上，采用省份级归纳式零样本协议，在24个已知省份训练，6个已知省份验证，8个未知省份评估。框架结合冻结的CLIP ViT-B/32图像编码器、CLIP文本编码器、BERT文本编码器和LSTM描述解码器。在推理时，未知省份的标签和描述不可用，检索仅使用训练省份的描述。实验表明，Custom ZeroCLIP在CLIPScore、BLEU-4和METEOR指标上均优于现有基线，检索增强带来19.3%的METEOR提升，人工评估证实了更强的文化准确性和流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印尼传统服饰具有丰富的文化多样性，但缺乏大规模标注数据，现有零样本描述方法难以处理未见省份的文化特异性。作者希望构建一个能泛化到未见文化区域、同时保留文化细节的描述生成框架。

Method: 1) 构建包含38省份3800张专家标注图像的印尼传统服饰数据集；2) 采用省份级归纳式零样本协议（24个已知省份训练，6个已知省份验证，8个未知省份评估）；3) 融合冻结的CLIP ViT-B/32图像编码器、CLIP文本编码器、BERT文本编码器和LSTM描述解码器；4) 在推理时，仅利用训练省份的描述进行检索增强，完全排除未知省份的图像、标签和描述。

Result: CLIPScore 0.8536，BLEU-4 0.3342，METEOR 0.4859，均优于现有基线。检索增强带来19.3%的METEOR提升，人工评估确认了更强的文化准确性和流畅性。

Conclusion: 检索增强的领域自适应方法在低资源文化遗产场景下，能有效生成具有文化根基的描述文本，证明了跨领域零样本泛化的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents Custom ZeroCLIP, a retrieval-augmented vision-language framework for zero-shot captioning of Indonesian traditional garments. The dataset contains 3,800 expert-annotated images from all 38 Indonesian provinces. Using a province-level inductive zero-shot protocol, the model is trained on 24 seen provinces, validated on 6 seen provinces, and evaluated on 8 unseen provinces. The framework combines a frozen CLIP ViT-B/32 image encoder, a CLIP text encoder, a BERT text encoder, and an LSTM caption decoder. During inference, unseen-province labels and captions are unavailable, and retrieval uses only captions from training provinces. No unseen-province image, label, or caption is used during training, validation, or retrieval-bank construction. Custom ZeroCLIP achieves a CLIPScore of 0.8536, BLEU-4 of 0.3342, and METEOR of 0.4859, outperforming existing baselines. Ablation results show that retrieval improves cultural vocabulary recovery with a 19.3\% METEOR gain, while human evaluation confirms stronger cultural accuracy and fluency. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented domain adaptation for culturally grounded caption generation in low-resource heritage settings. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/AnugrahAidinYotolembah/Traditional-Indonesian-Clothing-Captioning-Dataset.

</details>


### [112] [Cross-Modal Masked Compositional Concept Modeling for Enhancing Visio-Linguistic Compositionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13288)
*Wei Li,Zhen Huang,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MACCO框架，通过跨模态掩码重建 compositional concept，增强视觉-语言模型的组合理解能力，在五个组合基准上取得显著提升，并惠及文本到图像生成和多模态大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等对比训练模型在组合理解（如物体关系、属性-对象绑定、词序依赖）上表现欠佳，表现为"词袋"行为。该问题源于全局单向量表示的限制，以及对图文对中丰富组合信息的挖掘不足。

Method: 提出MACCO框架：在一个模态中掩码组合概念，利用另一模态的完整上下文信息进行重建；引入两个辅助目标函数，分别进行跨模态和模态内的掩码特征对齐与正则化。

Result: 在五个组合理解基准上显著提升VLMs的组合能力，增强模型对句法结构和语言信息的捕获能力，同时改善了文本到图像生成和多模态大语言模型的性能。

Conclusion: 跨模态掩码重建组合概念是提升视觉-语言模型组合理解能力的有效方法，且这种改进具有良好的迁移性，能惠及下游多模态任务。

Abstract: Contrastively trained vision-language models like CLIP, have made remarkable progress in learning joint image-text representations, but still face challenges in compositional understanding. They often exhibit a "bag-of-words" behavior--struggling to capture the object relations, attribute-object bindings, and word order dependencies. This limitation arises not only from the reliance on global, single-vector representations for optimization, but also from the insufficient exploitation and modeling of the rich compositional information inherently present in paired image text data. In this work, we propose MACCO (MAsked Compositional Concept MOdeling), a framework that masks compositional concepts in one modality and reconstructs them conditioned on the full contextual information from the other, enabling the model to capture and align cross-modal compositional structures more effectively. To facilitate this process, we introduce two auxiliary objectives that jointly align and regularize masked features both inter-modally and intra-modally. Extensive experiments on five compositional benchmarks, along with in-depth analyses, demonstrate that our approach not only significantly enhances compositionality in VLMs but also improves their ability to capture syntactic structure and linguistic information. Additionally, the improved compositionality also benefits text-to-image generation and multimodal large language model. Code is available at https://github.com/hiker-lw/MACCO.

</details>


### [113] [HYDRA-X: Native Unified Multimodal Models with Holistic Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13289)
*Guozhen Zhang,Xuerui Qiu,Yutao Cui,Tianhui Song,Changlin Li,Junzhe Li,Tao Huang,Xiao Zhang,Yang Li,Jianbing Wu,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HYDRA-X 是首个在单个 ViT 中统一图像和视频令牌化的多模态模型，通过帧级因果时序注意力、层次化时序压缩以及轻量化解压器实现高效重建与语义对齐，并在编辑任务中引入令牌级源-目标交互，在 7B 模型上取得了优秀的图像/视频理解与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型缺乏将图像和视频令牌化统一在单一 Vision Transformer 中的方案，面临两个核心挑战：如何高效地将时空重建能力注入原生 ViT，以及如何在潜在空间中嵌入图像级和视频级的语义感知能力。

Method: (1) 采用帧级因果时序注意力取代全时空注意力以优化重建性能；(2) 设计层次化时序压缩策略优于单步压缩；(3) 提出轻量化解压器，在联合图像-视频教师监督下对压缩特征进行上采样，强制潜在空间形成互补语义结构；(4) 将编辑任务中的源-目标交互从 LLM 语义层迁移到令牌器潜在层，提升编辑一致性与收敛速度。

Result: 在 7B 密集模型上，HYDRA-X 在图像和视频的理解与生成任务上均取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: HYDRA-X 验证了统一令牌化方案在视觉多模态任务中的可行性，为未来统一令牌化多模态模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Holistic visual tokenizers are fundamental to unified multimodal models (UMMs) as they map diverse visual inputs into a unified representation space. In this paper, we present HYDRA-X, the first UMM that unifies image and video tokenization within a single Vision Transformer (ViT). Our design is driven by two core challenges: efficiently injecting spatiotemporal reconstruction capability into a native ViT, and embedding image- and video-level semantic awareness into the latent space. To address the first, comprehensive ablations reveal two key findings: (1) frame-level causal temporal attention suffices for visual reconstruction, whereas full spatiotemporal attention degrades it; and (2) hierarchical temporal compression substantially outperforms single-step alternatives. To tackle the second, we propose a lightweight decompressor that upsamples temporally compressed features under joint image-video teacher supervision, thereby enforcing complementary semantic structures within the compact latent space. Building on this holistic tokenizer, we further propose a principled improvement of the editing pipeline: source-target interaction should occur at the latent level inside the tokenizer rather than at the semantic level inside the LLM, substantially improving editing consistency and accelerating convergence. Instantiated at the 7B dense model, HYDRA-X achieves strong performance across image and video understanding and generation tasks, paving the way for future unified-tokenizer UMMs.

</details>


### [114] [DuET: Dual Expert Trajectories for Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13303)
*Lidia Troeshestova,Alexander Ustyuzhanin,Sergey Kastryulin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuET是一种无需训练即可提升扩散编辑效果的方法，通过在编辑过程中临时切换至文本到图像生成阶段，再返回编辑模式，从而放松源图像条件约束，改善编辑相关性、语义保真度和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散编辑器在每一步去噪都依赖源图像条件，这限制了编辑的执行程度和生成结果的自然度，尤其是当目标场景与输入图像差异较大时。

Method: 提出DuET（双专家轨迹）方法，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过临时放松源图像条件约束，在编辑过程中过渡到文本到图像生成阶段，再返回编辑模式，使去噪轨迹朝目标分布移动，同时保留图像条件编辑的结构优势。

Result: 在不修改模型权重或增加采样成本的情况下，DuET在多个模型和基准测试中持续提升了指令相关性、语义保真度和感知质量；但有时会略微降低源图像保留度，揭示了源保留与编辑保真度之间的可预测权衡。

Conclusion: DuET通过引入双专家轨迹策略，有效平衡了编辑效果与源图像保留之间的关系，为现有扩散编辑模型提供了无需训练的通用改进方案。

Abstract: Recent diffusion editors perform diverse instruction-based edits while conditioning on the source image at every denoising step. Yet persistent source-image conditioning can limit how fully an edit is executed and how natural the result appears, especially when the target scene diverges substantially from the input. We introduce DuET (Dual Expert Trajectories), a training-free inference method that temporarily relaxes source-image conditioning by transitioning through a text-to-image phase before returning to edit mode, allowing the denoising trajectory to move toward the target distribution while retaining the structural benefits of image-conditioned editing. Without modifying model weights or increasing sampling cost, DuET consistently improves instruction relevance, semantic fidelity, and perceptual quality across diverse models and benchmarks. In some cases, these gains come with a modest reduction in source-image preservation, revealing a predictable trade-off between source preservation and edit fidelity.

</details>


### [115] [SmartFont: Dynamic Condition Allocation for Few-Shot Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13382)
*Zian Yang,Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartFont提出了一种扩散式少样本字体生成框架，通过多层级条件分配机制融合全局内容-风格生成与弱监督局部校正专家，实现了全局完整性与局部细节保真度的平衡，在少样本场景下提升了字形质量和局部细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本字体生成方法要么依赖全局内容-风格建模（鲁棒但解耦不完美），要么强调组件/局部建模（捕捉细节但严重依赖局部先验和参考覆盖）。核心挑战不在于学习更纯净的条件，而在于通过生成过程中的多层级分配来组织互补但有偏差的全局和局部条件。

Method: 1) 全局内容-风格扩散生成分支；2) 弱监督局部校正专家分支，通过弱组件监督学习专家级局部概念和语义空间图，实现无需显式组件条件推理的细粒度校正；3) 去噪状态条件分配模块，跨时间步和注入块自适应加权全局内容、全局风格和局部校正特征。

Result: 实验表明SmartFont在全局-局部平衡上优于现有方法，提升了字形质量和局部细节保真度。

Conclusion: 通过多层级条件分配机制有效组织互补的全局和局部条件，而非追求更纯净的解耦条件，是提升少样本字体生成质量的关键路径。

Abstract: Few-shot font generation simultaneously requires global structural completeness and fine-grained local style fidelity. Existing methods usually either rely on global content-style modeling, which is robust but imperfectly disentangled, or emphasize component/local modeling, which captures fine details but relies heavily on local priors and reference coverage. We argue that the key challenge is not merely to learn purer conditions, but to organize complementary yet biased global and local conditions through multi-level allocation during generation. To this end, we propose SmartFont, a diffusion-based few-shot font generation framework that combines global content-style generation with weakly supervised local corrective experts. The local branch performs semantic-spatial allocation by learning expert-wise local concepts and semantically meaningful spatial maps under weak component supervision, enabling fine-grained correction without requiring explicit component-conditioned inference. On top of this, a denoising-state condition allocation module adaptively weights global content, global style, and local corrective feature across timesteps and injection blocks. Extensive experiments show that SmartFont achieves better global-local balance, improves glyph quality and local detail fidelity.

</details>


### [116] [Person Identification from Contextual Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13410)
*Igor Kviatkovsky,Ehud Rivlin,Ilan Shimshoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运动风格进行身份识别的交互式方法，通过系统与主体间的序列消息交换（呈现视觉线索并记录运动响应），利用互信息最大化选择线索并更新身份后验概率，在多个公开数据集及自建数据集（22名受试者、4,476条记录、15种线索）上取得了高识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动模式的身份识别多为被动式（如监控中的步态识别）或主动式（如认证场景），但缺乏一种交互式、动态调整刺激以优化身份判别效率的形式化框架。

Method: 提出一个生成式模型描述动作实例创建过程；受人类信息处理（HIP）范式启发建立概率生成模型；在每一轮交互中，系统选择使期望响应与主体身份互信息最大化的视觉线索（cue），记录运动响应后更新身份的后验概率，直至达到足够分类置信度。

Result: 在5个公开数据集及自建数据集（4,476条记录、22名受试者、15种线索）上取得了高识别率。

Conclusion: 首次以交互式设置来解决基于运动模式的身份识别问题，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of identifying people based on their motion styles. We present a generative model describing the action instance creation process and derive a probabilistic identity inference scheme for two common person identification scenarios motivated by the surveillance and authentication applications. We introduce a novel, \emph{interactive}, scenario for person identification from motion patterns. To this end, we formalize the identification process in the context of a sequential message exchange session between the subject and the system. The subject's behavior is modeled using a probabilistic generative model inspired by the Human Information Processing (HIP) paradigm. At each stage, the system presents a visual stimulus (a cue) to the subject and records their motion response. The cue is selected so as to maximize the mutual information of the expected response and the subject's identity. Once recorded, the response is used to update the a posteriori probability over possible subjects' identities. The process terminates once a sufficient classification confidence level is reached. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time person identification is addressed in such interactive setting. We report high recognition rates on five publicly available datasets and our own novel dataset consisting of 4,476 recordings of 22 test subjects responding to 15 cues.

</details>


### [117] [VietFashion: Benchmarking Sketch-Text Composed Image Retrieval for Cultural Outfits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13427)
*Hoang-Nguyen Cao,Le-Hoang Bui,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VietFashion是一个面向越南传统服饰"奥黛"的草图-文本组合图像检索基准数据集，包含650张手绘草图和通过生成模型扩展的21,000多张逼真图像，采用多目标检索设置，旨在解决文化服饰因细微结构和符号细节难以被标准AI模型识别的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化服饰的视觉检索面临独特挑战，其身份往往依赖于细微的结构性和符号性细节，而标准AI模型难以有效捕捉这些特征。

Method: 构建VietFashion数据集：初始收集650张手绘草图，利用生成模型扩展至21,000多张逼真图像并配以字幕；从时尚杂志提取描述详细服饰属性的文本提示；采用多目标检索设置（单个查询对应多个有效结果）；建立标准化评估协议并基准测试现有组合图像检索方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有方法在建模细粒度文化语义和多模态组合方面存在显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: VietFashion是一个具有挑战性的细粒度时尚检索基准，揭示了当前模型在文化语义理解上的不足，为未来研究提供了重要评估平台。

Abstract: Cultural garments pose a unique challenge for visual retrieval systems, as their identity often depends on subtle structural and symbolic details that are poorly captured by standard AI models. We introduce VietFashion, a new benchmark for sketch-text composed image retrieval centered on the Ao Dai, a traditional Vietnamese garment. VietFashion enables designers and researchers to retrieve culturally meaningful outfits using a combination of hand-drawn sketches, which convey garment structure, and textual descriptions, which encode cultural semantics. The dataset is initialized with 650 sketches and expanded using generative models to produce over 21,000 photorealistic images with aligned captions. Textual prompts that describe detailed outfit attributes, which are extracted from fashion magazines to ensure authenticity and diversity. To better reflect the inherent ambiguity of design intent, VietFashion adopts a multi-target retrieval setting, where a single query may correspond to multiple valid results. We establish standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark state-of-the-art composed image retrieval methods. Experimental results reveal significant performance gaps in modeling fine-grained cultural semantics and multi-modal composition, positioning VietFashion as a challenging benchmark for fine-grained fashion retrieval. The dataset is publicly available at: https://hng0303.github.io/VietFashion.

</details>


### [118] [OmniDirector: General Multi-Shot Camera Cloning without Cross-Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13432)
*Jiwen Liu,Shujuan Li,Zhixue Fang,Xiaohan Li,Yan Zhou,Zijie Meng,Zhimin Zhang,Yawen Luo,Guoxin Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniDirector框架，通过将相机运动编码为网格运动视频（camera grid），实现从参考视频克隆相机运动并控制多模态扩散Transformer生成多镜头视频。该框架基于百万级相机网格-视频对训练，并设计分层提示扩展代理以协调不同控制信号，在复杂相机运动克隆方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用参数化表示无法处理多镜头生成，要么利用交叉配对数据存在数据稀缺问题，导致复杂相机运动克隆性能不佳。

Method: 1) 提出通用相机运动表示——相机网格（camera grid），将相机参数编码为视觉化网格运动视频，支持多轨迹集成用于多镜头生成。2) 构建OmniDirector统一框架，在百万级相机网格-视频对上训练，协调角色、动作和相机实现导演级控制。3) 设计分层提示扩展代理，通过系统描述相机运动和视觉内容并理解信号关系，和谐集成不同控制信号。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架具有优越性能和出色的可控性。

Conclusion: OmniDirector通过新颖的相机网格表示和大规模数据训练，有效解决了复杂相机运动克隆问题，为视频生成提供了导演级的多模态控制能力。

Abstract: Cloning camera motion from reference videos is an important task in video generation, as videos provide intuitive and precise control. Existing methods either directly use parametric representations that fail to handle multi-shot generation or synthesize cross-paired data, which suffer from data scarcity, resulting in poor performance in complicated camera motion cloning. To address these issues, we introduce a general camera motion representation that encodes cameras as grid motion videos. This camera grid represents the camera parameters visually and supports the integration of diverse trajectories for multi-shot video generation. Building upon this, we propose OmniDirector, a unified framework trained on a million-scale camera grid-video pairs that coordinates characters, actions, and cameras to provide director-level control for multimodal diffusion transformers. Furthermore, we design a novel hierarchical prompt expansion agent that harmoniously integrates different control signals by systematically describing camera motion and visual content through understanding signal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance and outstanding controllability of our framework. Project page: https://ymlinfeng.github.io/OmniDirector.github.io/

</details>


### [119] [VISA: VLM-Guided Instance Semantic Auditing for 3D Occupancy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13460)
*Ruiqi Xian,Yuehan Xian,Jing Liang,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISA方法，利用离线VLM对3D占据世界模型进行语义审计，通过结构化审计结果蒸馏到语义logits中，在不改变推理流程且无需VLM的情况下，显著提升占据预测的mIoU，尤其在物体和罕见类别上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM策略（对齐3D体素/物体特征与剪裁-描述嵌入）虽能提升文本空间相似度，但未能可靠改善闭集占据预测的mIoU，尤其在物体和罕见类错误上影响自由空间解释、碰撞检测和时间状态传播。

Method: VISA在训练时对每个物理物体实例的典型裁剪区域查询离线VLM，获取包含类别假设、可能混淆、可靠性、属性和证据的结构化审计，沿物体轨迹传播，并将其与匹配的3D物体体素对齐，通过可靠性加权分类、属性因子和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到语义logits中，推理时不需VLM。

Result: 在nuScenes上，VISA将OccWorld的mIoU从19.06提升至20.05，GaussianWorld从21.36提升至21.91；GaussianWorld的物体mIoU从18.18提升至19.16，罕见类mIoU从15.60提升至16.79。

Conclusion: VLM更适合作为可靠性感知的语义审计器，而非通用的描述嵌入目标，以提升闭集占据预测性能。

Abstract: Semantic 3D occupancy provides a voxelized world state for autonomous driving and robot decision making, but object and rare-class errors can affect free-space interpretation, collision checking, and temporal state propagation. We show that a common VLM strategy, aligning 3D voxel or object features with crop-caption embeddings, improves text-space similarity without reliably improving closed-set occupancy mIoU. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose VISA, a training-time semantic auditing approach for existing occupancy world models. VISA queries an offline VLM on a representative crop of each physical object instance, obtains a structured audit with class hypotheses, plausible confusions, reliability, attributes, and evidence, and propagates it along the object track. The audit is grounded to matched 3D object voxels and distilled into semantic logits through reliability-weighted taxonomy, attribute-factor, and scene-level audit graph losses, while inference remains unchanged and requires no VLM. On nuScenes, averaged across three runs, VISA improves OccWorld from 19.06 to 20.05 mIoU and GaussianWorld from 21.36 to 21.91 mIoU; on GaussianWorld, object mIoU improves from 18.18 to 19.16 and rare-class mIoU from 15.60 to 16.79. These results suggest that VLMs are better suited to closed-set occupancy as reliability-aware semantic auditors than as generic caption-embedding targets.

</details>


### [120] [Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13488)
*Siyu Zhou,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAPR-Net，一种结合卷积与Transformer的粗到细点云配准框架，通过跨注意力机制融合局部与全局信息，并设计了变换不变的几何特征表示，在四个骨骼数据集上达到94.2%的配准召回率、1.992mm的RMSE，为手术导航提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分到整体的点云配准因重叠率变化、点密度波动和噪声存在而具有挑战性；现有Transformer方法仅用于全局上下文聚合，忽略了细粒度局部几何信息，导致对应点匹配不精确。

Method: 提出GAPR-Net，采用粗到细架构，结合卷积与Transformer模块，利用跨注意力机制融合部分点云与整体点云之间的局部和全局信息；并设计了变换不变的点向几何特征表示，鲁棒地捕获单个点相对于邻域的几何特征。

Result: 在胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸肋软骨四个几何结构不同的骨骼数据集上，配准召回率达94.2%，RMSE为1.992mm，旋转和平移的R²值分别为0.908和0.974。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了部分到整体的点云配准问题，实现了高精度3D点云配准，为计算机辅助手术中的精确手术导航和机器人干预提供了关键基础。

Abstract: Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.

</details>


### [121] [Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13496)
*Mingkun Lei,Tong Zhao,Liangyu Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudCache 提出了一种预算驱动的缓存方法，通过离线搜索（模拟退火+爬山法）在固定推理预算下优化扩散模型的缓存策略，并引入缓存感知的时间步对齐，在 FLUX.1-dev 和 Wan2.1 上优于启发式缓存基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步骤级缓存方法使用基于阈值的启发式规则进行缓存决策，没有直接优化最终输出质量，导致推理延迟因输入而异且难以控制。

Method: 固定计算预算后搜索最优缓存策略；结合模拟退火与确定性爬山法进行离线搜索；在预算紧张时引入缓存感知的时间步对齐以减少轨迹失配。

Result: 在 FLUX.1-dev 和 Wan2.1 上，BudCache 在相同推理预算下比启发式缓存基线获得了更好的生成质量。

Conclusion: BudCache 通过反转传统范式（固定预算而非固定阈值），实现了更可控且更高质量的缓存加速。

Abstract: Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache

</details>


### [122] [Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13503)
*Judith Vilella-Cantos,Juan José Cabrera,Mónica Ballesta,David Valiente,Luis Payá*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法，通过融合异质LiDAR数据（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）并结合学习型重排序策略，显著提升了非结构化环境（如 vineyards）中的地点识别性能，Recall@1指标相比单传感器提升20%，结合重排序提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境（如农业场地）中的鲁棒定位是自主系统的关键挑战；LiDAR传感器不受光照影响，但重复性环境（如葡萄园）中寻找真正匹配项困难，现有方法未充分利用异质传感器的互补优势。

Method: 提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 早期融合异质LiDAR数据（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16），利用各传感器优势构建更全面的环境表征；(2) 推理阶段采用学习型重排序策略，提升重复场景中查找正确匹配的能力。

Result: 在TEMPO-VINE数据集上评估，MinkUNeXt-VINE++相比单传感器方法Recall@1提升20%，结合重排序后提升30%，性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 异质LiDAR传感器早期融合结合推理时学习型重排序能显著提升非结构化重复环境下的地点识别性能；代码已开源可复现。

Abstract: Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.

</details>


### [123] [Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13509)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测量校准的多相机融合方法，通过显式量化单相机定位误差（包括单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪）来优化融合策略。实验表明，该方法在绝对精度提升有限，但能显著降低轨迹方差并改善运动平滑度，对需要稳定连续运动估计的应用场景具有重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内视觉定位系统中多相机数据融合通常被视为黑箱组件，仅通过端到端评估，缺乏对其机理贡献的深入理解。本文旨在探究显式表征单相机定位误差是否可用于校准和优化多相机数据融合。

Method: 提出测量校准的融合方法（measurement-calibrated fusion），分别量化单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪三个组件层面的误差，并进行组件级评估。

Result: 多相机融合相比单相机基线提升了定位精度；测量校准融合在绝对精度上相比标准融合提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑度。

Conclusion: 显式误差表征在设计视觉室内定位系统的数据融合策略时具有重要价值，尤其对需要稳定连续运动估计的应用场景。

Abstract: Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion.
  We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking.
  Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.

</details>


### [124] [MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13515)
*Hanyang Yu,Haitao Lin,Jingbo Zhang,Wenyao Zhang,Chenghao Gu,Heng Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskWAM提出了一种以物体为中心的世界动作模型，通过统一的多Transformer架构联合集成掩码作为显式输入和预测，解决了现有世界动作模型中的空间瓶颈问题，在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型存在两个核心空间瓶颈：标准文本输入在杂乱场景中引入指代模糊性，非结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义根基且受任务无关背景干扰。

Method: 提出MaskWAM，采用统一的多Transformer架构，将掩码同时作为显式输入和预测目标。关键设计包括：(1)预测未来掩码提供以物体为中心的语义监督抑制视觉噪声；(2)将预测监督与首帧视觉提示（如目标物体掩码）结合建立精确空间锚点。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上，MaskWAM在语言清晰和语言模糊任务中均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 由于WAM本质上是视觉驱动的架构，直接掩码条件化相比纯文本提供更强的引导，为操作未知物体建立了精确且鲁棒的范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.

</details>


### [125] [What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13528)
*Samuel Webster,Walter Scheirer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出使用PCA和LDA等经典降维技术生成显著性图，替代人工标注，实现低成本、高扩展性的显著性引导训练，在多个生物特征欺骗检测任务上超越基线甚至SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性引导训练依赖人工标注或领域特定工具，成本高、扩展性差，限制了其在生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的广泛应用。

Method: 利用PCA和LDA等经典降维技术直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识，并将其应用于多个PAD任务。

Result: 在虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD等三个已有显著性探索的领域以及指纹静脉PAD和身份证PAD两个新领域上，使用降维显著性图的模型均超过基线，部分达到或超越SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 降维技术生成的显著性图能有效替代人工标注，克服了显著性引导训练在生物特征攻击检测中的关键瓶颈，且具有很好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.

</details>


### [126] [Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13558)
*Shengqiang Zhang,Ruotong Liao,Volker Tresp,Barbara Plank,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BitResEdit，一种无需训练的比特残差VAR图像编辑器，通过比特级引导（BitEdit）和残差代码组合（ResEdit）实现局部编辑，在Infinity-2B上达到最先进的文本对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR编辑器主要操作在token流、特征或扁平logits上，未充分利用比特残差VAR模型的两个原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头和加性多尺度残差代码场。

Method: BitEdit：通过源-目标对比倾斜后CFG逐比特对数几率，投影到伯努利KL信任域；ResEdit：将采样比特转为逐尺度连续码残差，用定位掩码门控，通过生成器原生尺度求和机制重新注入。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B，BitResEdit在相同骨干VAR编辑器中达到最强文本对齐，编辑区域CLIP提升+1.07，背景保持与先前最佳方法竞争。

Conclusion: BitEdit和ResEdit在目标对齐和背景保持中发挥互补作用，通过代码算术精确保持掩码潜在特征，同时在目标区域内应用局部尺度感知编辑。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

</details>


### [127] [Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13562)
*Stephen Moore,Lara Leijser,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比度感知的数据增强和领域对抗训练对E2E-VarNet模型从成人到新生儿MRI重建泛化能力的影响。结果表明，混合训练和混合领域对抗训练在新生儿数据上的表现优于仅用成人数据训练，其中Mixed-DAT在R=4时最佳（SSIM=0.924），而t-SNE分析表明领域对抗训练增加了不同领域数据潜在表征的重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习MRI重建方法在成人数据上表现优异，但直接迁移到新生儿数据时存在领域漂移(domain shift)问题，导致泛化性能下降。本研究旨在探索对比度感知数据增强和领域对抗训练是否能改善从成人到新生儿的跨领域迁移能力。

Method: 采用E2E-VarNet作为基础模型，比较三种训练策略：(1)仅用未增强的成人数据训练(Unaug-Only)；(2)混合训练(Mixed)，使用未增强的成人数据与经过新生儿对比度信息增强的成人数据；(3)混合领域对抗训练(Mixed-DAT)，在混合训练基础上加入领域对抗目标。模型在回顾性欠采样的多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，在R=4和R=8两种加速因子下评估。

Result: 在新生儿数据上，Mixed和Mixed-DAT均优于Unaug-Only。R=4时Mixed-DAT性能最佳（SSIM=0.924±0.027, PSNR=33.98±1.15 dB）；R=8时Mixed-DAT在SSIM指标上最优（0.848±0.031），而Mixed在PSNR指标上最优（29.56±0.83 dB）。t-SNE可视化显示Mixed-DAT增加了未增强成人、增强成人和新生儿测试数据潜在表征之间的重叠程度。

Conclusion: 对比度感知数据增强与领域对抗训练均能有效改善深度学习MRI重建模型从成人到新生儿的跨领域泛化能力，两者结合使用效果更佳。该研究为提高欠采样新生儿MRI重建对领域漂移的鲁棒性提供了有效策略。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.

</details>


### [128] [EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13580)
*Dachun Kai,Jiayao Lu,Yueyi Zhang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvTexture++，首个专用于视频超分辨率纹理增强的事件驱动框架。它利用事件信号的高频时空细节，通过迭代纹理增强模块逐步恢复纹理，并引入时间纹理对齐模块利用事件连续运动线索增强时序一致性。作为即插即用工具，可提升现有VSR模型性能，在Vid4数据集上最高提升1.55 dB PSNR，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动VSR工作将事件信号用于运动细化和时序对齐，本文则首次将事件信号的关注点从运动细化转向纹理增强，旨在利用事件的高频时空特性提升超分辨率中的纹理恢复质量。

Method: 提出EvTexture++框架，包含：(1) 定制化纹理增强分支；(2) 迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率事件信息进行纹理恢复；(3) 时间纹理对齐模块，利用事件连续时间运动线索估计纹理感知光流，实现帧间纹理对齐，缓解大运动导致的纹理闪烁。整体设计为即插即用模块。

Result: 在五个数据集上达到SOTA性能。集成到现有VSR模型中，在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR提升最高达1.55 dB。
