Conclusion: 事件信号在VSR中的关注点从运动细化转向纹理增强是有效的，EvTexture++通过逐步纹理恢复和时序纹理对齐，显著提升了超分辨率纹理质量与时序一致性。

Abstract: Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.

</details>


### [129] [Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13587)
*Mamoona Javaid,Mubashir Noman,Abdul Hannan,Shah Nawaz,Mustansar Fiaz,Sajid Ghuffar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合空间域和频谱域的级联网络，结合辅助特征增强模块，实现了对杂乱场景中废弃物的高效、精确分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市扩张和人口增长导致废物产量激增，需要自动化废物管理。现有深度学习方法依赖大型骨干网络，效率低下且在杂乱场景中性能下降。

Method: 引入了一个级联分割网络，利用空间域捕捉局部结构依赖，利用频谱域提取全局上下文关系，渐进式融合局部和全局表征；同时设计了辅助特征增强模块（AFEM）以增强目标边界和斑点放大，提升杂乱场景下的分割效果。

Result: 在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste三个数据集上的广泛实验验证了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的网络通过跨域互补表示和特征增强模块，在废弃物分割任务上实现了更优的性能，尤其适用于杂乱场景和自动化废物回收系统。

Abstract: Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

</details>


### [130] [Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13676)
*Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Justin Johnson,Keunhong Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Modality Forcing方法，通过对文本到图像扩散模型（DiT）进行后训练，使其能够联合生成图像与深度图，仅需稀疏深度数据即可实现与SOTA单目深度估计相媲美的性能，且随模型规模和数据量增大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽利用T2I模型的空间先验进行深度预测，但需要密集深度数据和复杂训练流程；旨在探索一种更简单、可扩展的后训练方案，仅使用稀疏深度数据即可实现高质量的联合图像-深度生成与深度预测。

Method: 提出Modality Forcing，为每个模态分配独立的噪声水平，使单个DiT能以任意排列方式实现图像和深度的条件生成与联合生成；采用每模态独立的解码器，支持在稀疏真实世界深度数据上训练。

Result: 训练了370M至3.3B参数的T2I模型，更大模型和更多数据带来更精确的深度；最优模型与SOTA单目深度估计器竞争，相比现有联合图像-深度生成模型AbsRel降低57%。

Conclusion: 图像生成是一种可扩展的空间感知预训练目标，Modality Forcing简单高效地继承了T2I预训练的可扩展性，证明了稀疏深度数据足以训练出强泛化能力的深度预测模型。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/

</details>


### [131] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng,Harry Lee,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterleaveThinker是一个多智能体管道，通过规划器（planner）和批评器（critic）两个智能体，赋予现有图像生成器交错生成（文本-图像序列）能力，并通过冷启动SFT训练和基于GRPO的单步强化学习优化，在交错生成基准上达到接近Nano Banana和GPT-5的性能，同时显著提升基础模型在推理基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成器在单图生成和编辑上表现出色，但由于架构限制，无法实现交错生成（文本-图像序列），而这一能力在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作中至关重要。最新的统一多模态模型（UMMs）在此方面表现有限。

Method: 提出InterleaveThinker多智能体管道：(1) 规划器（planner）组织图像-文本输入序列，指导图像生成器每步执行；(2) 批评器（critic）评估生成输出，识别偏离指令的样本并优化指令以重新生成。构建Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k和Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k进行格式冷启动，再通过Interleave-Critic-RL-13k使用GRPO和单步奖励（accuracy reward + step-wise reward）强化逐步指令修正能力。

Result: InterleaveThinker提升了多种图像生成器的交错生成性能，在交错生成基准上达到与Nano Banana和GPT-5相当的水平。在推理基准上显著增强基础模型，例如在4-step FLUX.2-klein上，WISE和RISE均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: InterleaveThinker作为一种通用且高效的管道，成功赋予了现有图像生成器交错生成能力，且不依赖模型架构改动，同时意外提升了基础模型的推理性能，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

</details>


### [132] [Cascade Classification of Dermoscopic Images of Skin Neoplasms with Controllable Sensitivity and External Clinical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13135)
*Elena S. Kozachok,Sergey S. Seregin,Aleksandr V. Kozachok,Ilya P. Latyshev,Oleg I. Samovarov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了四种深度学习架构（ViT-B/16、Swin-S、ConvNeXt-S、EfficientNetV2-S）在皮肤镜图像分类中的表现，评估了三种分类方案（二分类、单阶段四分类、两阶段级联），并揭示了从公开国际数据集向俄罗斯临床数据集迁移时存在的显著泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在公开数据集上表现良好，但缺乏在真实临床实践（尤其是俄罗斯临床环境）中跨数据集的泛化能力评估，且不同分类方案（二分类、多分类、级联）的比较不充分。

Method: 使用ImageNet预训练权重，在ISIC公开数据集上统一增强训练，评估四种架构在二分类（良/恶性）、单阶段四分类（良性/MEL/SCC/BCC）和两阶段级联（二分类分流+三分类鉴别）三种方案上的表现，并在内部留出样本和两个俄罗斯临床数据集（Melanoscope AI系统、Sechenov大学）上测试。

Result: 内部二分类ROC-AUC达0.952-0.966，但在Sechenov大学数据集下降至0.797-0.893，敏感性降至0.53-0.67，ECE从0.02升至0.27-0.39，存在显著泛化差距。级联方案在大多数架构上提高了宏F1，但仅对ViT-B/16具有统计显著性。二分类阶段ViT-B/16表现显著较差，鉴别阶段无架构具有优势。

Conclusion: 可调节分流阈值的级联方案比标准单阶段分类能更好地控制敏感性，且更符合临床鉴别诊断逻辑。模型部署前必须进行外部临床验证和重校准以应对泛化差距。

Abstract: Purpose. To compare deep learning architectures and classification schemes for dermoscopic images of skin neoplasms and assess their generalization on transfer from open international datasets to independent clinical datasets of Russian practice.
  Methods. Four architectures (ViT-B/16, Swin-S, ConvNeXt-S, EfficientNetV2-S) were compared in three schemes: binary (malignant/benign), single-stage four-class (benign, MEL, SCC, BCC), and a two-stage cascade (binary triage, then three-class differentiation MEL/SCC/BCC). All models used ImageNet-pretrained weights and a single augmentation protocol on aggregated open ISIC Archive data, and were evaluated on an internal held-out sample and two clinical datasets (Melanoscope AI mobile system; Sechenov University).
  Results. Internally the binary stage attains ROC-AUC 0.952-0.966; on Sechenov University it drops to 0.797-0.893, sensitivity to 0.53-0.67, and ECE rises from 0.02 to 0.27-0.39 with underestimation of malignancy, quantifying a generalization gap in ranking and calibration. Paired tests confirm one inter-architecture result on clinical data: the deficit of ViT-B/16 at the binary stage (p<0.05); at the differentiation stage no architecture has a proven advantage. The cascade raises macro F1 over single-stage four-class classification for most architectures, but significantly only for ViT-B/16, by recovering malignant lesions assigned to the dominant benign class. On ISIC MILK10k, direct 11-class classification yields mean-class sensitivity 0.525.
  Conclusion. A tunable triage threshold gives sensitivity control not attainable in standard single-stage (argmax) classification and better reproduces clinical differential-diagnosis logic. The persistent generalization gap mandates external clinical validation and recalibration before deployment.

</details>


### [133] [Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network for Multimodal CT-PET Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13341)
*Gabriel Steele,Alzahra Altalib,Alessandro Perelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双域等变生成对抗网络（DDE-GAN），通过在空间域和频率域联合学习并引入旋转等变性损失，实现了多模态CT-PET图像的高质量合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于GAN的方法仅在空间域操作且忽略几何一致性，导致结构保真度有限，无法充分利用CT-PET图像的频谱信息和旋转对称性。

Method: 1）双域学习：同时在空间域和频率（傅里叶）域进行联合训练，捕获互补的解剖和频谱信息；2）旋转等变损失：在生成器和判别器的损失函数中融入基于CT和PET物理测量特性的旋转等变性约束；3）分层双域训练策略：通过多阶段损失函数强制域内和域间一致性。

Result: 在HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET数据集上，DDE-GAN在合成质量上优于基线模型，验证了双域学习与几何等变性结合能显著提升多模态图像合成的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双域学习与几何等变性的联合使用能有效增强多模态图像合成性能，在PET补全和数据增强等实际应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: We present a Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network (DDE-GAN) for multimodal CT-PET image synthesis. Traditional GAN-based approaches often operate solely in the spatial domain and ignore geometric consistency, resulting in limited structural fidelity. DDE-GAN addresses these challenges by jointly learning from both spatial and frequency (Fourier) domains, capturing complementary anatomical and spectral information. Furthermore, rotational equivariance embedded in the physics of the CT and PET measurements are integrated into the loss of both the generator and discriminator to ensure consistent responses under rotations, improving anatomical accuracy. A hierarchical dual-domain training strategy enforces intra- and inter-domain consistency through multi-stage loss functions. Evaluated on the HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET dataset, DDE-GAN achieves superior synthesis quality over baseline models for CT-PET image synthesis. The results demonstrate that combining dual-domain learning with geometric equivariance substantially enhances multimodal image synthesis accuracy and robustness, enabling practical applications in PET completion and data augmentation.

</details>


### [134] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho,Ryo Hachiuma,Abhishek Badki,Hang Su,Byung-Kwan Lee,Chan Hee Song,Sifei Liu,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Seungryong Kim,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialClaw提出了一种无需训练的框架，采用代码作为动作接口，通过有状态的Python内核和感知几何原语，使VLM代理能逐步执行并灵活组合感知结果，在20个3D/4D空间推理基准上平均准确率59.9%，超过现有方法11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理代理存在两种设计：单次代码执行（在观察到中间结果前就提交完整分析策略）和结构化工具调用接口（缺乏自由组合操作的灵活性）。两者均限制了开放、复杂3D/4D空间推理的能力。

Method: SpatialClaw维护一个有状态的Python内核，预加载输入帧和感知/几何原语，让VLM代理逐步编写可执行代码单元，每一步都基于先前所有输出结果，灵活组合和操作感知结果，适应中间文本/视觉观察和问题需求。

Result: 在20个涵盖静态和动态3D/4D空间推理任务的基准上，平均准确率59.9%，超过最新空间代理11.2个百分点，在6种VLM骨干网络上均有一致提升，无需任何基准或模型特定的适配。

Conclusion: 以代码作为动作接口的有状态执行框架能显著提升VLM在开放空间推理任务上的表现，具备良好的通用性和灵活性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [135] [An Extensible and Lightweight Unified Architecture for Demosaicing Pixel-bin Image Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13136)
*Saurabh Kumar,Nutan Sairam Yenneti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种模块化统一架构，用于对不同像素合并传感器进行去马赛克处理，并附带无学习的CFA识别模块，实现即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素合并传感器成为主流，但其较大的颜色间分离度使去马赛克变得困难；现有多样化的深度学习去马赛克方法需为不同CFA单独训练模型，占用资源且维护成本高。

Method: 提出模块化统一架构，可适应多种像素合并传感器；引入无学习的CFA识别模块，直接检测原始数据的CFA类型。

Result: 在保持可扩展性和轻量化的同时，提供更高的图像质量；CFA识别模块实现即插即用。

Conclusion: 所提统一架构无需为不同CFA分别训练模型，降低了开发和部署成本，兼顾了高性能与实用性。

Abstract: Pixel-bin image sensors are becoming the default choice for smartphone cameras due to their resolution vs light-gathering trade-off. However, their larger inter-color separation compared to the Bayer color filter array (CFA) makes them challenging to demosaic. Furthermore, existing deep learning-based demosaicing methods are CFA-specific, requiring multiple individual models that take up precious onboard resources and demand larger development and maintenance efforts. In this work, we propose a modular unified architecture for demosaicing various pixel-bin sensors that provides higher image quality while being extensible and lightweight. Additionally, to enable plug-and-play operation, we introduce a learning-free CFA-identification module to detect the CFA type of raw data accurately.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Influence Factors on RAG Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12469)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça,Adrien Bécue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过432种配置的全因子实验，系统研究了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在面对投毒攻击时的鲁棒性，发现检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的关键因素，而生成器选择和数据库组成对下游攻击成功率影响最大。投毒脆弱性源于检索、生成和知识库配置的交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部检索内容进行生成，这引入了投毒攻击的脆弱性——对抗性文档可同时操纵检索过程和生成输出。本文旨在系统性地探究RAG系统中哪些组件和配置因素对投毒鲁棒性影响最大。

Method: 采用全因子实验设计，覆盖432种配置，系统分析了数据集、检索器类型、检索深度、数据库组成、分块策略和生成器模型六个因素对检索级和生成级度量指标的影响。

Result: 检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素；密集检索器和基于图的检索器相比BM25更具鲁棒性；更大的检索深度增加检索到投毒片段的概率；跨多个数据库复制投毒内容会放大对抗性影响，而额外清洁源可缓解该影响。

Conclusion: RAG的投毒脆弱性并非由单一组件导致，而是由检索、生成和知识库配置三者之间的交互作用共同决定。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models by grounding responses in retrieved documents from external knowledge sources at inference time. However, this reliance on retrieved content introduces vulnerabilities to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents can manipulate both the retrieval process and the generated outputs. This paper investigates poisoning robustness in RAG through a full factorial experimental study covering 432 configurations. We analyze the impacts of dataset, retriever type, retrieval depth, database composition, chunking strategy, and generator model on retrieval-level and generation-level metrics. The results show that retriever architecture, dataset, and retrieval depth are the strongest factors affecting poisoning exposure, while generator choice and database composition have a major impact on downstream attack success. Dense and graph-based retrievers generally improve robustness relative to BM25, whereas larger retrieval depth increases the likelihood of retrieving poisoned passages. We further show that replicating poisoned content across multiple databases amplifies adversarial influence, while additional clean sources can mitigate it. These findings highlight that poisoning vulnerability in RAG is not attributable to a single component, but instead arises from the interaction of retrieval, generation, and knowledge-base configuration.

</details>


### [137] [From Parameters to Feature Space: Task Arithmetic for Backdoor Mitigation in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12498)
*Zhenqian Zhu,Yamin Hu,Yiya Diao,Weixiang Li,Haodong Li,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出线性特征路径最小化(LFPM)框架，通过引入反后门任务向量并在特征空间中优化，有效消除模型合并中的后门攻击，同时保持干净任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并易受后门攻击，而基于任务算术的防御方法因依赖直接参数空间编辑，在消除后门时严重损害干净任务性能。

Method: 在跨任务线性(CTL)框架下从统一特征空间视角出发，引入反后门任务向量到被污染的合并模型中，并通过梯度累积和损失路径积分机制沿插值路径进行鲁棒后门抑制优化。

Result: 在全微调和参数高效微调(PEFT)两种设置下，LFPM均展现出对后门攻击的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LFPM通过特征空间优化有效弥补了现有参数空间编辑方法的缺陷，为模型合并中的后门防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Model merging (MM) has gained significant attention as a cost-effective approach to integrate multiple task-specific models into a unified model. However, recent work reveals that MM is highly susceptible to backdoor attacks. Existing defenses based on task arithmetic often fail to eliminate backdoors without substantially degrading clean-task performance, owing to their reliance on direct parameter-space editing. To address this gap, we propose Linear Feature Path Minimization (LFPM), a backdoor mitigation framework for model merging, which introduces an anti-backdoor task vector into the backdoored merged model. Unlike prior approaches, LFPM formulates the backdoor robustness of the merged model from a unified feature-space perspective under the Cross-Task Linearity (CTL) framework, which leverages the approximate linearity of features across tasks. This perspective guides the optimization of the anti-backdoor task to suppress backdoors while preserving clean-task performance. Furthermore, we introduce an effective optimization mechanism based on gradient accumulation and loss path-integral, ensuring robust backdoor suppression along the interpolation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LFPM consistently exhibits strong robustness against backdoor attacks in both full fine-tuning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) settings.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: Examining Trigger Leakage in Vision-Language Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12586)
*Jiamin Chang,Salil Kanhere,Piotr Koniusz,Jason,Xue,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉后门攻击中的"触发器泄漏"问题，即与预期触发器视觉或语义相近的输入会无意激活隐藏行为。为此引入邻域泄漏率(NLR)进行量化，发现标准微调会学习到宽激活区域而非精确触发条件，而加入编辑距离为1的硬负样本可有效缩小激活区域、减少泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉后门评估仅关注干净准确率和攻击成功率(ASR)，这些指标能衡量触发器是否生效，但无法衡量攻击是否"精确"——即是否仅在预期时触发隐藏行为。需要量化触发器泄漏问题。

Method: 1) 形式化定义触发器泄漏问题；2) 提出邻域泄漏率(NLR)作为量化指标；3) 通过实验分析图标和文本触发器在不同视觉变换下的泄漏情况；4) 使用文本触发器作为受控探针，研究标准微调学习到的激活区域特性；5) 提出加入编辑距离为1的硬负样本训练策略来缩小激活区域。

Result: 在3%投毒率下，图标和文本触发器对常见视觉变换保持鲁棒，但其邻近变体严重泄漏，NLR分别达到0.996(图标)和0.944(文本)。标准微调学习到宽激活区域，导致邻近字符串也能调用恶意行为。加入硬负样本后显著缩小激活区域、降低泄漏，包括在图像编辑和具身操作工作流中。

Conclusion: 视觉后门攻击在VLA系统中构成系统级威胁，现有评估指标不足以衡量攻击精确性。通过硬负样本训练可有效改进触发器的精确性，减少泄漏带来的安全风险。

Abstract: Vision-Language Agentic Systems (VLAS) connect visual perception to planning, tool use, and physical actions. This means backdoor-type triggers can propagate through both decision pipelines and their connected interfaces, thus making visual backdoors a system-level threat. Current evaluations on such backdoors focus on clean accuracy and attack success rate (ASR), metrics that capture whether a trigger works, but not whether an attack is actually "precise" -- i.e. whether it triggers hidden behaviors only when intended. In this work, we formalize the failure of trigger precision as "trigger leakage": inputs that are visually or semantically close to the intended trigger and therefore inadvertently activate the attacker-specified behavior. To quantify this leakage, we introduce Neighbor Leakage Rate (NLR). Our experiments show that at a 3% poisoning ratio, icon and text triggers remain robust to common visual transformations, but their neighboring variants leak heavily, with NLR reaching 0.996 (icon) and 0.944 (text). Using textual triggers as a controlled probe, we show that standard fine-tuning learns a broad activation region rather than an exact trigger condition, causing neighboring strings to invoke the malicious behavior even when the exact trigger is absent. Adding edit-distance-one hard-negative samples during training substantially narrows this activation region and reduces leakage, including in image-editing and embodied-manipulation workflows, where leaked triggers can propagate into executable programs and action sequences.

</details>


### [139] [Amnesia: A Stealthy Replay Attack on Continual Learning Dreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12655)
*Ahmed Sharshar,Naveen Kumar Kummari,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Amnesia，一种仅控制回放索引选择的持续学习攻击方法，能在满足可审计性约束（类别分布偏差和回放率）的前提下显著降低模型性能，揭示了回放采样中索引控制这一实用但被忽视的安全威胁面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习攻击多修改输入或训练流程（投毒/后门），缺乏显式的可审计约束，不具现实性。回放采样的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究，特别是仅有索引控制权限的内部威胁场景。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击，包含两步：(i) 计算轻量级类别效用（如EMA损失或置信度）倾斜类别分布；(ii) 通过KL（指数倾斜）或TV（平衡质量重分配）优化器将倾斜投影回delta-ball内。窗口调度器确保滚动审计合规。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准和强回放基线中，Amnesia持续降低最终准确率（ACC）并恶化后向迁移（-BWT）。KL变体在多种审计方案下难以检测，TV变体破坏性更强但更易被发现。

Conclusion: 仅索引控制的回放攻击是一种实用且可审计的威胁面，建立了原则性的影响-可见性权衡，揭示了持续学习系统在回放采样环节的安全脆弱性。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) models often use experience replay to reduce catastrophic forgetting, but their robustness to replay sampling interference remains underexplored. Existing CL attacks alter inputs or training pipelines (poisoning/backdoors) and rarely include explicit auditable constraints, limiting realism. Here, auditability means a monitor can verify compliance from sampler-visible telemetry - e.g., logged replay index/label statistics - by checking that the realized replay class histogram stays close to a nominal baseline and that replay rate is unchanged per batch and/or over a rolling window. We study a limited-privilege insider who controls only replay index selection, not pixels, labels, or model parameters, while staying within auditable limits such as queue priorities. We introduce Amnesia, a replay composition attack that maximizes degradation under two budgets: a visibility budget delta bounding the TV/KL divergence from a nominal class histogram p0, and a mass budget f fixing the replay rate. Amnesia has two steps: (i) compute lightweight class utilities, such as EMA loss or confidence, to tilt p0 toward harmful classes; and (ii) project the tilt back into the delta-ball using efficient KL (exponential tilt) or TV (balanced mass redistribution) optimizers. A windowed scheduler enforces rolling audits. Across challenging CL benchmarks and strong replay baselines, Amnesia consistently lowers final accuracy (ACC) and worsens backward transfer (-BWT). The KL variant delivers high impact while remaining largely undetected under multiple audit schemes, including per-batch and rolling-window checks. The TV variant is more damaging but easier to detect, especially under tight per-class constraints. These results expose index-only replay control as a practical, auditable threat surface in CL systems and establish a principled impact-visibility trade-off.

</details>


### [140] [CAPED: Context-Aware Privacy Exposure Defense for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12666)
*Siyu Shen,Fenghao Xu,Wenrui Diao,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPED，一种面向移动GUI代理的上下文感知预上传暴露控制层。在截图发送至远程多模态代理前，选择性暴露任务所需内容并掩盖无关隐私信息。实验表明，该方法将加权种子泄露从0.766降至0.268，同时保持较高任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI代理通过截图与手机交互时，截图会暴露大量与当前任务无关的敏感信息（如联系人、消息、照片等），即"偶然性视觉隐私暴露"问题。现有文本匿名化方法无法覆盖视觉和推理线索，而通用隐私遮蔽会移除GUI代理完成任务所需的证据和控制元素。

Method: CAPED作为手机端保护层，在截图发送至远程多模态代理前：1）提取任务需求；2）利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验；3）解析可见UI元素；4）选择性暴露当前任务所需内容，同时掩盖偶然性隐私内容。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上进行28个种子任务的受控隐私评估中，完整CAPED将成功条件加权的种子泄露从原始截图的0.766降至0.268，同时保持较高的任务效用。更广泛的AndroidWorld运行显示仍存在原型层面的效用成本。

Conclusion: 截图上传应被视为明确的设备-云边界决策，由任务驱动的选择性暴露而非全有或全无的屏幕共享来管控。实验结果支持这一核心主张。

Abstract: Screenshot-based mobile GUI agents can operate ordinary smartphone apps through the same visual interface as a human user, but this capability also turns every screen observation into a privacy boundary. During normal task execution, screenshots may expose contacts, messages, photos, files, recommendations, health cues, and other sensitive context that is unrelated to the user's request. We call this problem incidental visual privacy exposure. It is difficult to address with existing defenses: text anonymization misses many visual and inferential cues, while generic privacy masking can remove the evidence and controls that a GUI agent needs to complete the task.
  This paper presents CAPED, a context-aware pre-upload exposure control layer for mobile GUI agents. CAPED is designed as a phone-side protection layer: before screenshots are released to a remote multimodal agent, it extracts task requirements, uses screen context as a privacy prior, parses visible UI elements, and selectively exposes only content needed for the current task while masking incidental private content. We evaluate CAPED on AndroidWorld for broad task utility and with a controlled 28-task seeded privacy evaluation used as a measurement instrument for trajectory-level incidental leakage. In this seeded evaluation, Full CAPED reduces success-conditioned weighted seeded leakage from 0.766 under raw screenshots to 0.268 while preserving high task utility. A broader AndroidWorld run shows a remaining prototype-level utility cost, but the results support the central claim that screenshot upload should be treated as an explicit device--cloud boundary decision, governed by task-driven selective exposure rather than all-or-nothing screen sharing.

</details>


### [141] [SMSR: Certified Defence Against Runtime Memory Poisoning in Persistent LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12703)
*Tarun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多会话记忆投毒（MSMP）攻击的防御机制——带签名记忆与平滑检索（SMSR），通过HMAC-SHA256写入时认证和随机记忆消融+基于裁决的多数投票，首次为该场景提供了可证明的鲁棒性保证，将攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%（未签名）和8.0%（单次注入认证攻击）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）智能体越来越多地使用跨用户会话持久化记忆，这引入了新的攻击面：攻击者仅通过正常交互即可注入精心构造的记忆，在后续被检索时操纵智能体对其他用户的响应，而无需修改模型权重或代码。现有防御（如RobustRAG、ReliabilityRAG）假设固定知识库，启发式过滤器可被流畅的企业风格文本绕过，尚无方案能提供可证明的防御。

Method: 提出SMSR防御框架，包含两个组件：(1) 写入时添加HMAC-SHA256来源认证，阻断未签名注入；(2) 查询时应用随机记忆消融（randomised memory ablation）配合基于裁决的多数投票（verdict-based majority voting），限制经过认证的对手的影响。理论方面，证明了无来源的检索时过滤器无法防御自适应注入，推导了组件2的超几何认证（hypergeometric certificate），并形式化了"一致少数效应"（Consistent Minority Effect）。

Result: 在15个企业场景（3,150次重复试验）中，组件1将未签名变体的攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%。对于单次注入的认证对手，组件2将成功率控制在8.0%（95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450），低于认证的最坏情况。在端到端纯查询攻击（智能体自行写入投毒而非预植入）中，SMSR将成功率从65.3%降至5.3%（n=150，置信区间不重叠）。干净查询的实用率为90%（组件1）和85%（合并）。

Conclusion: SMSR是首个针对多会话记忆投毒攻击提供可证明鲁棒性边界的防御方案，通过写入时签名与查询时随机消融+裁决投票的双重机制，有效阻断未签名注入并限制认证攻击者的影响力，同时保持较高的干净查询效用。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) agents increasingly run with persistent memory that accumulates across user sessions. This creates a new attack surface: an adversary interacting only through normal channels can inject crafted memories that, once retrieved, steer the agent's responses for future users, without touching model weights or code. We call this Multi-Session Memory Poisoning (MSMP) and show that no existing defence certifies against it; static-corpus defences (RobustRAG, ReliabilityRAG) assume a fixed knowledge base, and heuristic filters are bypassed by fluent enterprise-style text. We present Signed Memory with Smoothed Retrieval (SMSR), the first defence with a certified robustness bound for this setting. Component 1 adds HMAC-SHA256 provenance at write time, blocking unsigned injection. Component 2 applies randomised memory ablation with verdict-based majority voting at query time, bounding the influence of authenticated adversaries. We prove that no provenance-free retrieval-time filter can certify against adaptive injection, derive a hypergeometric certificate for Component 2, and formalise the Consistent Minority Effect, whereby a consistent adversarial answer wins string-based voting as a numerical minority while verdict-based voting removes it. Across 15 enterprise scenarios (3,150 repeated trials), Component 1 cuts attack success from 93-100% to 0% for all unsigned variants. For an authenticated adversary with a single injection, Component 2 holds success to 8.0% (95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450), below the certified worst case. In an end-to-end query-only attack where the agent itself writes the poison rather than it being pre-seeded, SMSR reduces success from 65.3% to 5.3% (n=150, non-overlapping CIs) on a live agent stack. Clean-query utility is 90% (Component 1) and 85% (combined).

</details>


### [142] [PI-Hunter: Automated Red-Teaming for Exposing and Localizing Prompt Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12737)
*Pengfei He,Lesly Miculicich,Vishesh Sharma,Ash Fox,George Lee,Jiliang Tang,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PI-Hunter是一个自动化代理审计框架，通过构建真实场景感知的测试用例并迭代演化，主动暴露LLM代理系统中潜在提示注入漏洞，在漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖上显著优于现有红队方法，且能绕过现有防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要聚焦于推理时阻断恶意内容，红队方法主要优化攻击成功率，导致开发者对潜在提示注入如何在代理系统中产生和传播的能见度有限。

Method: 构建真实场景感知的测试用例，通过反馈驱动的探索迭代演化，诱导代理检索并揭示外部环境中嵌入的潜在恶意指令。

Result: 在多个基准、代理架构、攻击和防御场景下，PI-Hunter在漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖上显著优于强自动化红队基线，且对现有提示注入防御依然有效。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的自动化审计框架，用于主动暴露LLM代理系统中的提示注入漏洞，填补了开发者对漏洞传播可见性不足的空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into agentic systems that interact with external tools and environments, introducing new security risks such as indirect prompt injection attacks through untrusted external sources. Existing defenses mainly focus on blocking malicious content at inference time, and current red-teaming methods primarily optimize attack success. As a result, developers have limited visibility into how latent prompt injections emerge and propagate through agents. We propose PI-Hunter, an automated agentic auditing framework for proactive vulnerability exposure in LLM agents. PI-Hunter constructs realistic source-aware test cases and iteratively evolves them through feedback-driven exploration to induce agents to retrieve and reveal latent malicious instructions embedded within external environments. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, agent architectures, attacks, and defenses demonstrate that PI-Hunter substantially improves vulnerability exposure and attack-surface coverage over strong automated red-teaming baselines, while remaining effective under existing prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [143] [Semantic Identification of IoT Devices from Behavioral Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12793)
*Samuel Witt,Hassan Habibi Gharakheili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用制造商使用说明（MUD）配置文件进行物联网设备识别，通过ACE级别的语义表示与匹配，在运行时流量变化条件下（如未见过的ACE、漂移主机名、稀疏重叠）相比传统精确匹配方法具有更强的鲁棒性和识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网设备识别方法依赖底层通信观测数据（数据包或流记录），其流量模式会因部署环境、软件版本和用户交互而变化，导致识别鲁棒性不足。本文探索利用MUD配置文件这一高级语义描述来克服上述局限。

Method: 使用28个公开MUD配置文件（含1,023个ACE实例），构建ACE级别的语义表示（从紧凑行为文本中提取），分析其几何特性；在受控运行时变化条件和真实物联网流量（80万+流记录）中评估语义ACE匹配与精确ACE匹配的性能对比。

Result: ACE级别表示比整体配置文件嵌入更有效保留设备级别行为区分性，经白化校准后仍有效。精确ACE匹配在MUD覆盖率高时表现良好，但稀疏或无重叠时性能急剧下降；语义ACE匹配在早期观察阶段、候选排序保持正确设备以及稀疏重叠场景下均表现更优。

Conclusion: 语义ACE匹配为物联网设备识别提供了一种对运行时变化更鲁棒的解决方案，特别在精确匹配信号不足的早期及稀疏重叠场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Accurate identification of IoT devices is important for security management and policy enforcement. Existing approaches typically learn device signatures from packets or flow records. These methods operate on low-level communication observations whose traffic patterns may vary across deployments, software versions, and user interactions. This paper studies device identification using Manufacturer Usage Description (MUD) profiles. MUD profiles describe device behavior using Access Control Entries (ACEs), where each ACE represents a behavioral primitive consisting of protocol, endpoint, direction, and port semantics derived from device communication policy. Our contributions are threefold. First, using 28 publicly available MUD profiles containing 1,023 ACE instances, we construct ACE-level semantic representations from compact behavioral text and analyze their geometric properties. ACE-level representations preserve device-level behavioral distinctions more effectively than whole-profile embeddings and remain effective after whitening calibration. Second, we evaluate semantic ACE matching under controlled runtime variations, including unseen ACEs, drifted hostnames, and partial runtime observation. Exact ACE matching performs well when the overlap with the canonical MUD profile remains high, but degrades sharply when the overlap becomes sparse or disappears. In contrast, semantic ACE matching preserves useful identification evidence across these conditions. Third, we evaluate the same approach on real IoT traffic traces comprising more than 800,000 observed flows. Exact overlap remains the strongest signal when stable overlap exists, while semantic ACE matching provides stronger identification evidence during the early stages of observation, frequently retains the correct device among the highest-ranked candidates, and remains effective under sparse-overlap runtime traffic.

</details>


### [144] [A Privacy-Preserving Framework Using Remote Data Science for Inter-Institutional Student Retention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12845)
*John Fields,K M Sajjadul Islam,Ruchitha Thota,Victor Chen,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究基于PySyft平台开发了远程数据科学框架，采用半气隙架构实现三所大学在无需直接访问敏感学生数据的情况下协作构建学生保留预测模型，验证了隐私保护机器学习在跨机构教育场景中的技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在遵守FERPA等隐私法规的前提下，实现跨机构协作预测学生保留率的可行性，解决教育机构间因数据敏感性和隐私限制而难以共享数据构建高质量预测模型的问题。

Method: 基于PySyft开发远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用半气隙架构（高侧和低侧服务器）；评估三种合成数据生成方法；提出Data-Type-Aware Templates新型合成数据方法；利用一所小型私立大学的历史数据（N=720）进行跨机构协作验证。

Result: 跨机构分类性能一致（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），严格符合FERPA合规要求；Data-Type-Aware Templates在隐私保护优先于分布保真度方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于RDS的PPML在跨机构教育场景中技术可行，可替代联邦学习用于小规模合作；为教育领域敏感数据协作分析提供了实用方案。

Abstract: This study explores privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) techniques using the PySyft platform to enable collaborative prediction of student retention between institutions. We developed a remote data science (RDS) framework with a semi-air-gapped architecture consisting of high-side and low-side servers, allowing researchers from three universities to build predictive models on sensitive student data without direct data access. Using historical data from a small private university (N=720), we evaluated three synthetic data generation approaches and validated the framework through inter-institutional collaboration. The results demonstrate consistent classification performance across institutions (Macro F1: 0.690--0.695) while maintaining strict Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) compliance. We also propose Data-Type-Aware Templates, a novel synthetic data method that prioritizes privacy over distributional fidelity. Our findings confirm that RDS-based PPML is technically feasible for educational settings and offers a practical alternative to federated learning for small-scale inter-institutional collaborations. The code is available at https://github.com/jtfields/NAIRR240195-Privacy-Preserving-Machine-Learning.

</details>


### [145] [LNTest: A Testbed for Evaluating Bitcoin Lightning Network-Based Botnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12887)
*Thomas Bakaysa,Ahmet Kurt,Abdul-Salem Beibitkhan,Jesus Maria Romo Diaz de Leon,Tag Kalat,Joshua Kramer,Estela Rodriguez,Abraham Watkins,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LNTest是首个针对基于闪电网络(LN)的僵尸网络的可复用测试平台，通过真实环境实验发现：D-LNBot的自组网协议实际生成的是由桥接节点连接的聚类链而非均匀链；命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)而非此前声称的O(m log n)；三种覆盖拓扑对拆除攻击表现出不同的脆弱性模式，其中自主形成的聚类链最为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LNBot和D-LNBot设计仅停留在仿真验证的概念验证阶段，缺乏真实环境下的拓扑形成机制、传播复杂度和抗拆除能力的系统性评估。

Method: 基于Core Lightning节点、Docker容器和Bitcoin Core regtest链构建LNTest测试平台，支持确定性链、自主发现和用户提供图三种覆盖拓扑模式，进行多组对比实验。

Result: (1) D-LNBot自组网协议实际生成聚类链，桥接节点是关键脆弱点；(2) 命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)线性增长，邻居连接度提升无增益；(3) 均匀度链抗定向拆除但惧随机失效，无标度拓扑相反，自主聚类链在两种攻击下均脆弱。

Conclusion: LNTest作为开源可复现测试平台，揭示了LN僵尸网络在真实环境下的性能瓶颈和安全脆弱性，为相关防御研究提供了实验基础。

Abstract: Bitcoin's Lightning Network (LN) can be exploited as a covert, low-cost command-and-control (C&C) channel for botnets, as demonstrated by the LNBot and D-LNBot designs. However, both remain proof-of-concept prototypes evaluated only through simulation, leaving key questions about real-world topology formation, propagation complexity, and resilience to takedowns unanswered. We present LNTest, the first reusable testbed for LN-based botnets, built from Core Lightning nodes containerized with Docker over a shared Bitcoin Core regtest chain. LNTest supports three overlay topology modes (a deterministic chain, autonomous peer discovery, and user-supplied graphs), enabling controlled experiments across different botnet structures. Using LNTest, we report three main findings. First, D-LNBot's autonomous formation protocol does not produce the uniform chain from its design; instead, it creates a clustered chain in which cliques are linked by bridge nodes whose removal fragments the network. Second, command propagation scales linearly with botnet size ($Θ(n)$), not the $O(m \log n)$ previously claimed, and gains nothing from higher neighbor connectivity. Third, the overlay topology determines the effectiveness of takedown strategies: uniform-degree chains resist targeted removal but fragment under random failure, scale-free topologies show the opposite pattern, and the autonomous clustered chain is fragile under both, making it the most vulnerable of the three. LNTest is released as open source, with a script that reproduces all our experiments, to support reproducible research on LN-based botnet defenses.

</details>


### [146] [MAStrike: Shapley-Guided Collusive Red-Teaming on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12918)
*Chejian Xu,Zhaorun Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Freddy Lecue,Avni Kothari,Sarah Tan,Wenbo Guo,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAStrike是一个针对层次化多智能体系统的闭环红队测试框架，通过首次提出的智能体级Shapley值分析量化每个智能体对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献，识别脆弱联盟并生成协调的角色感知对抗攻击，实验表明其在多个前沿模型上显著优于启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化多智能体系统在金融、软件工程等高危场景中快速部署，但其安全风险因角色分工而高度分散，现有红队测试方法仅依赖启发式选择和孤立的单消息扰动，无法回答哪些智能体对系统安全最负责、以及被攻破的智能体如何协调绕过防御等问题。

Method: 提出MAStrike闭环框架，包括：(1) 首个智能体级Shapley值分析，量化每个智能体在任务分布下的边际安全贡献；(2) 基于归因识别脆弱智能体联盟，生成协调的角色感知对抗操作；(3) 通过结构化因果诊断迭代优化攻击，将失败案例归因于阻止攻击的未妥协智能体；(4) 构建涵盖多种层次化拓扑和领域的MAS红队测试基准。

Result: 在多个前沿模型构建的MAS上，MAStrike显著优于启发式基线方法。分析揭示了非平凡的Shapley值分布和智能体间高阶交互结构，发现了以往单智能体或模板方法忽略的漏洞和协调模式。

Conclusion: MAStrike通过闭环红队测试框架和Shapley值归因分析，有效识别了层次化MAS中的关键安全弱点和智能体间协调攻击模式，为多智能体系统的安全评估提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Hierarchical multi-agent systems (MAS) are rapidly being deployed in high-stakes workflows across domains such as finance and software engineering. In these systems, safety and security are inherently distributed across role-specialized agents, significantly expanding the attack surface, particularly under coordinated adversarial behaviors such as privilege escalation and cross-agent collusion. Existing red-teaming approaches for MAS remain limited: they rely on heuristic selection of target agents and perturb isolated message streams, leaving critical questions unanswered as which agents are most responsible for system safety, and how compromised agents can coordinate to bypass defenses. We propose MAStrike, a closed-loop framework for collusive red-teaming in hierarchical MAS. We propose the first agent-level Shapley value analysis for MAS, quantifying each agent's marginal contribution to system robustness under task-specific distributions. GGuided by this attribution, MAStrike identifies vulnerable agent coalitions and generates coordinated, role-aware adversarial manipulations. These attacks are iteratively refined through structured causal diagnosis, attributing failure cases to uncompromised agents that block adversarial attempts. We further build a comprehensive MAS red-teaming benchmark and controllable environments spanning diverse hierarchical topologies and domains, including finance, software engineering, and CRM. Extensive experiments across MAS built on multiple frontier models show that MAStrike substantially outperforms heuristic baselines. Our analysis further uncovers non-trivial Shapley value distributions and higher-order interaction structures among agents, revealing critical vulnerabilities and coordination patterns that are overlooked by prior single-agent or template-based methods.

</details>


### [147] [ViPER: Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12949)
*Fatima Qaiser,Bisma Tahir,Muhammad Abid Mughal,Nauman Shamim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ViPER是一种基于视觉的打包感知编码器，采用LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络和双头架构，联合学习恶意软件分类和打包检测，通过打包感知门控机制和频率加权损失函数，在20万Windows PE字节图像数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能（平衡准确率0.8521，ROC-AUC 0.9260，AUPR 0.9279，打包检测AUC 0.9949）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可视化（将二进制字节映射为灰度图像）的恶意软件检测方法在面对可执行文件打包时存在严重失效问题：打包产生的高熵图像会掩盖模型依赖的结构模式。由于打包在良性软件中也普遍存在（如压缩或拷贝保护），打包状态本身不能作为恶意性的可靠指标，而现有方法未能在统一监督框架下解决这一挑战。

Method: ViPER采用LoRA适配的ViT-B/14视觉Transformer作为骨干网络，设计双头架构同时学习恶意软件分类和打包检测任务；提出打包感知门控机制，根据推断的打包状态调整恶意软件预测的决策边界；采用频率加权损失和分层采样策略应对训练中的打包标签倾斜问题。

Result: 在20万Windows PE字节图像数据集上，ViPER取得平衡准确率0.8521、ROC-AUC 0.9260、AUPR 0.9279，在所有主要指标上优于代表性基线方法；打包检测AUC达到0.9949。

Conclusion: ViPER通过联合学习打包检测和恶意软件分类的视觉框架，有效解决了可执行文件打包对基于图像的恶意软件检测造成的性能退化问题，验证了打包感知机制在此场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Visualization-based malware detection maps raw binary bytes to grayscale images and applies learned visual classifiers, providing an evasion-resistant and disassembly-free alternative to conventional analysis pipelines. However, executable packing remains a critical failure mode: packed binaries produce high-entropy images that obscure the structural patterns these models rely on. Because packing is also prevalent in benign software (e.g., for compression or copy protection), packing state alone is not a reliable indicator of maliciousness, and existing approaches do not address this challenge within a unified supervised framework. We present ViPER, a Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust malware detection. ViPER builds on a LoRA-adapted ViT-B/14 backbone with a dual-head architecture that jointly learns malware classification and packing detection. A packing-aware gating mechanism conditions malware predictions on the inferred packing state, enabling distinct decision boundaries for packed and unpacked inputs. To address packing label skew during training, we employ frequency-weighted losses with stratified sampling over joint class-packing strata. Evaluated on 200,000 Windows PE byteplot images, ViPER achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.8521, ROC-AUC of 0.9260, and AUPR of 0.9279, outperforming representative state-of-the-art baselines across all primary metrics, while attaining a packing detection AUC of 0.9949.

</details>


### [148] [SoK: The Constant Time Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13000)
*Billy Bob Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化了针对密码学实现的时序攻击防御中的"常数时间"编程模型，识别了模型保护范围与规范假设之间的持续差距，并提出了一套发现密码学原语边界外时序漏洞的攻击方法论。应用该方法，发现了私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了信息泄漏。有趣的是，尽管BoringSSL有更严格的安全模型，其单次观测信号强度却比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界和工业界对"常数时间"的理解存在差异，现有防御模型未能覆盖密码学原语边界之外的时序泄漏来源，缺乏系统化的方法论来发现这些边界外的漏洞。

Method: 系统化了常数时间模型及其演化过程，识别了模型保护范围与规范假设之间的差距，提炼了一套主动发现密码学原语边界外时序漏洞的攻击方法论。

Result: 定位了一个与私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中都确认了泄漏；发现BoringSSL单次观测信号强度比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 现有的常数时间防御模型存在覆盖范围不足的问题，密码学实现的安全性不仅取决于代码层面的常数时间特性，还依赖于更高层次的规范假设；更严格的安全模型不一定带来更强的实际安全性。

Abstract: Constant time programming patterns is the primary defense against timing attacks on cryptographic implementations, yet what "constant time" means varies across academia and industry. This work systematizes constant time models and their evolution, identifies a recurring gap between what models protect and what specifications assume, and distills an offensive methodology for discovering timing vulnerabilities that originate outside the cryptographic primitive boundary. Applying this methodology, we locate a specification-level vulnerability related to private key loading, and confirm the leak in both OpenSSL and BoringSSL. Counterintuitively, BoringSSL's per-observation signal is several orders of magnitude stronger than OpenSSL's, despite an explicitly stricter threat model.

</details>


### [149] [DIG: Oracle-Guided Directed Input Generation for One-Day Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13037)
*Andrew Bao,Haochen Zeng,Peng Chen,Stephen McCamant,Pen-Chung Yew*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DIG提出了一种利用补丁信息生成漏洞PoC的方法，通过LLM分析补丁合成oracle，结合agent推理约束求解和有向模糊测试的分支距离反馈，在138个真实CVE上触发80个漏洞，比最好基线提升40%，并发现6个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单日漏洞因补丁部署延迟或不完整而构成重大风险，现有有向模糊测试方法未能显式识别和有效求解必要约束，而智能体方法在长程推理中容易出现目标漂移，亟需一种能够利用补丁信息显式提取触发条件并高效求解的PoC生成方法。

Method: DIG利用单日漏洞的关键特性——补丁常揭示触发的必要前置条件，使用LLM分析补丁并合成oracle来显式化这些条件；高层次上，通过agent推理并求解约束以满足oracle进行生成器演化；低层次上，将oracle植入目标程序并利用分支距离反馈指导有向模糊测试的随机变异。

Result: 在138个真实CVE上，DIG触发80个漏洞，优于2个最先进智能体方法和10个模糊测试工具；比最佳基线提升40%（57 vs. 80 CVE）；独占触发9个现有技术无法触发的漏洞；在92.9%的案例中比平均速度更快，48.8%案例获得100倍以上加速，最大加速达3664倍；额外发现6个零日漏洞。

Conclusion: DIG通过补丁驱动的oracle合成与双层次约束求解机制，显著提升了单日漏洞PoC生成的效率与覆盖范围，并在实践中证明了其发现零日漏洞的能力。

Abstract: One-day vulnerabilities pose significant risks due to delayed or incomplete patch adoption. Generating proof-of-concept (PoC) inputs is therefore essential for assessing real-world impact. The key challenge is identifying necessary constraints for triggering the vulnerability and solving them effectively. Existing directed fuzzing approaches prioritize inputs toward target locations, but neither explicitly identify necessary constraints nor solve them effectively, relying instead on target-distance feedback and random mutation. Agentic approaches show strong potential through code reasoning and structured input generation, but goal drift in long-horizon reasoning limits their effectiveness.
  DIG addresses this challenge by exploiting a key property of one-day vulnerabilities: patches often reveal necessary preconditions for triggering. DIG uses an LLM to analyze the patch and synthesize an oracle making these conditions explicit. The oracle supports effective PoC generation at two levels. At the high level, DIG performs oracle-guided generator evolution, where an agent infers and solves constraints to satisfy the oracle. At the low level, DIG instruments the oracle into the target program and uses branch-distance feedback to guide random mutation in directed fuzzing. Evaluation shows DIG outperforms 2 state-of-the-art agents and 10 fuzzers across 138 real-world CVEs. DIG triggers 80 vulnerabilities, surpassing prior results and outperforming the best baseline by 40% (57 vs. 80 CVEs). Notably, DIG exclusively triggers 9 vulnerabilities no existing technique can trigger. Compared to the average of other tools, DIG triggers vulnerabilities faster in 92.9% of cases, achieving over 100x speedup in 48.8% of cases, with a maximum speedup of 3,664x. Beyond one-day PoC generation, DIG uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in widely deployed libraries, enabling zero-day discovery.

</details>


### [150] [The Emergence of Autonomous Penetration Capabilities in Large Language Model-Powered AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13079)
*Jiaqi Luo,Jiarun Dai,Zhile Chen,Jia Xu,Weibing Wang,Yawen Duan,Brian Tse,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个由两级目标服务器（共300台）和通用代理框架组成的自主渗透评估框架，对19个开源和闭源LLM进行评估，发现当前模型的渗透成功率在10.7%到69.3%之间，且自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主渗透评估存在方法不透明、场景过于简化、LLM获得过多先验知识和任务引导等问题，无法准确衡量AI系统在真实高影响力网络攻击场景中自主执行渗透这一核心能力。

Method: 构建包含两级目标服务器的评估框架：Tier-1（一个安全服务）和Tier-2（三个安全服务），共300台目标服务器；采用通用代理架构并配备通用网络安全工具，无目标特定先验知识；评估19个开源和闭源LLM。

Result: 当前模型渗透成功率为10.7%至69.3%；自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而持续改进。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统的自主渗透能力虽有进展但仍有较大提升空间，该框架为更准确地评估该核心能力提供了标准化方法。

Abstract: Nowadays, the autonomous execution of cyberattacks capable of causing substantial real-world harm is widely regarded as one of the critical red lines that frontier AI systems must not cross. Within this broader red-line scenario, autonomous penetration represents a core enabling capability and subtask: the ability of LLM-powered AI systems to independently conduct adversarial operations against a target server without human intervention, identify and exploit vulnerabilities, and obtain unauthorized access or control. A growing body of work has sought to assess the autonomous penetration capabilities of AI systems. However, existing evaluations often employ opaque methodologies, rely on unrealistic or overly simplified penetration-testing scenarios, or provide LLMs with excessive prior knowledge and task-specific guidance, and cannot accurately capture the extent to which modern AI systems can autonomously perform this core capability within broader high-impact cyberattack scenarios.
  To address these limitations, we construct a new autonomous penetration evaluation framework consisting of two components: target servers and agent scaffolding. Specifically, on the target-server side, we design two levels of target environments based on the number of secure services without known vulnerabilities deployed alongside a vulnerable service: Tier~1 (one secure service) and Tier~2 (three secure services), resulting in a total of 300 target servers. Meanwhile, the agent scaffolding adopts a general-purpose agent architecture equipped with a set of general-purpose cybersecurity tools, without any target-specific prior knowledge. We evaluate 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs, and find that current models achieve penetration success rates ranging from 10.7% to 69.3%. Moreover, we observe that autonomous penetration capability continues to improve alongside advances in overall model capability.

</details>


### [151] [The Invisible Ink of the Android Malware World: A Longitudinal Study on the Usage of Covert Communication Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13107)
*Zeya Umayya,Manan Aggarwal,Manan Chugh,Mann Nariya,Yogesh Kaushik,Sambuddho Chakravarty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Android恶意软件生态系统中隐蔽通道（CC）的使用进行了纵向研究，分析了2009年至2025年间350万款恶意APK，发现CC使用率从2012年的0.30%激增至2025年的50%，并识别出511个恶意家族和59个明确验证的CC IP地址。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代理、VPN和Tor等工具既被隐私社区和受审查地区用户用于对抗审查，也被恶意软件和僵尸网络利用来隐藏与外部命令控制服务器的通信，但此前缺乏关于恶意软件如何使用隐蔽通道（CC）逃避检测的纵向研究。

Method: 开发了一个多阶段分析管道，结合静态和动态分析来研究系统和网络层面特征；对350万款Android恶意软件（2009年至2025年7月）语料库进行了分析；使用精心设计的静态验证规则识别使用CC的APK，并通过动态分析验证CC IP地址。

Result: 静态分析发现288K APK使用了CC，涵盖511个恶意家族，CC使用率从2012年的0.30%指数增长至2025年的50%；动态分析识别出85个国家的19,308个唯一IP地址，其中17个国家的59个IP地址明确验证存在CC；纵向研究发现部分恶意软件采用多个CC，有的周期性切换（一个家族在2019-2025年间切换了40次）。

Conclusion: CC在Android恶意软件生态系统中使用呈指数级增长，使用模式日趋复杂，需要持续监测和更先进的检测方法。

Abstract: Proxies, VPNs and Tor have long helped the privacy community and users in censored regions to fight censorship. However, the same tools can be maliciously exploited by malware and botnets to conceal their communication to external command and control servers. Despite being a critical concern fueled by the proliferation of malware based attacks, no longitudinal studies have analyzed how malware applications use covert channels (CC) to evade detection.
  We fill this gap by performing the first study of the usage of covert channels in the Android malware ecosystem. To that end, we develop a multistage pipeline that combines static and dynamic analysis to investigate both system and network-level features. We applied this pipeline on a corpus of 3.5M Android malware spanning 2009 to July 2025. Our carefully crafted static validation rules uncovered 288K APKs that used CCs spanning 511 malware families and CC usage growing exponentially from 0.30\% (2012) to 50\% (2025). Overall, in dynamic analysis, we identified 19,308 unique IP addresses being contacted in 85 countries, out of which we were able to explicitly validate the presence of CCs for 59 IP addresses across 17 countries. Further, we performed a longitudinal dataset study spanning over 16 years for CC based malware and found that CC usage has evolved, \textit{e.g.,} some malware adopted by using more than one CCs; others switched between them periodically (one family switched CC usage 40 times from 2019 to 2025).

</details>


### [152] [Who Pays the Price? Stakeholder-Centric Prompt Injection Benchmarking for Real-world Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13385)
*Zihao Wang,Yiming Li,Yutong Wu,Zheyu Liu,Kangjie Chen,Fok Kar Wai,Pin-Yu Chen,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing,Bo Li,Dacheng Tao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 StakeBench，一个以利益相关者为中心的基准测试，用于系统性地分类和归因 LLM 驱动的 Web 代理在真实环境中面临的提示注入攻击风险，揭示了现有代理无法可靠抵抗任何攻击目标，且失败模式呈现异质性（如隐蔽寄生、错位干扰、复合失败），强调了需要利益相关者感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准采用攻击中心视角，关注注入的技术可行性，忽略了危害在不同利益相关者之间的差异化分布：同一个攻击对不同受害方会产生不对称后果，且同一攻击模式对不同目标的效力显著不同。

Method: 构建利益相关者中心基准，区分受影响实体（用户、卖家、平台等），将攻击分解为具体目标，采用结果级和过程级互补指标进行评估。

Result: 当前代理无法可靠抵抗任何攻击目标；失败模式呈现异质性，包括隐蔽寄生（攻击成功但未干扰用户委托任务）、错位干扰（任务被干扰但攻击未成功）和复合失败（攻击目标与任务完整性同时被破坏）。

Conclusion: 传统评估方式忽略了上述模式差异，凸显了在真实部署中对 LLM 代理进行利益相关者感知评估的必要性。

Abstract: Web agents driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they operate over untrusted web content and execute actions with direct consequences. This makes them vulnerable to prompt-injection attacks, in which seemingly benign content embeds adversarial instructions that manipulate agent behaviour. Existing security benchmarks adopt an \textit{attack-centric} perspective, focusing on the technical feasibility of injections while overlooking the nuanced distribution of resulting harms. In practice, however, prompt-injection risk is victim-dependent: a single exploit can produce asymmetric consequences for different stakeholders, and the same attack pattern may exhibit substantially different effectiveness depending on whom it targets. To capture these properties, we introduce \textbf{\sysname}, a \textit{stakeholder-centric} benchmark to systematically categorize and attribute harm in real-world web agent systems. It distinguishes between affected entities (e.g., user, seller, platform), decomposes the attacks into concrete objectives, and evaluates each case with complementary outcome- and process-level metrics. Our results reveal substantial and heterogeneous vulnerabilities: not a single attack objective is reliably resisted by current agents, and failures distribute across qualitatively distinct modes ranging from \emph{stealthy parasitism} (attack succeeds without disrupting the user's delegated task) to \emph{misaligned disruption} (task disrupted without attack success) and \emph{compounded failure} (both adversarial objective and task integrity simultaneously violated). These patterns are missed by conventional evaluation, highlighting the need for stakeholder-aware assessment of LLM-based agents in real-world deployments. Benchmark is available at https://github.com/StakeBench/SBC.

</details>


### [153] [An Assessment Framework for Application-Level Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13425)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个七维组件化评估框架，用于系统衡量应用层面的密码敏捷性，并评估了六种主流密码API，发现三个普遍存在的关键缺陷（意图驱动的密钥创建、策略驱动的算法选择、密钥算法转换操作），解释了后量子密码迁移仍是一个软件工程问题的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码迁移要求全面替换算法，但现有密码API在设计时未充分考虑敏捷性，且缺乏系统性评估密码敏捷性的方法。"密码敏捷性"这一术语涵盖多种独立能力（替换算法、策略选择、实现替换），缺乏结构化分解限制了系统评估和敏捷API的开发。

Method: 提出了一个组件化评估框架，沿七个正交维度刻画应用级密码敏捷性：三个耦合维度（应用代码对算法和提供者的了解程度）、一个横切解耦机制、一个治理权威维度、两个敏捷性使能维度（实际迁移能力）。该框架是非线性的，可捕获非层级化的敏捷性画像。使用该框架评估了六种代表性API（PKCS#11、OpenSSL 3.0、JCA、Google Tink、AWS KMS、HashiCorp Vault Transit）。

Result: 六种API均存在三个独立且普遍的缺陷：不支持意图驱动的密钥创建、不支持策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）、不支持对现有密钥进行算法转换的一等操作。这三个缺陷各自足以阻碍敏捷迁移。

Conclusion: 当前密码API的这三个系统性缺陷解释了为何后量子过渡尽管已有数十年的API发展，仍然是一个软件工程问题。该框架为评估和设计具有密码敏捷性的系统提供了结构化方法。

Abstract: The impending post-quantum transition to new cryptography will require complete replacement of algorithms within all software. The cryptographic APIs used today make this transition challenging because they were not designed with agility as a concern. There is no method for systematically assessing cryptographic agility as an overall ability. In addition to this, the term itself refers to multiple independent capabilities. Specifically, it includes replacing algorithms, selecting by policy, and substituting implementations. This lack of structured decomposition limits both the evaluation of systems and the development of cryptographically agile APIs.
  We introduce a component-based assessment framework that characterizes application-level cryptographic agility along seven orthogonal dimensions: three coupling dimensions that measure what the application code knows about algorithms and providers, a cross-cutting decoupling mechanism, a governance authority dimension, and two agility enablers that measure actual migration capability. The framework is non-linear and captures non-hierarchical profiles: a system may achieve high operation decoupling yet low creation decoupling, or strong versioning without externalized configuration.
  We evaluate six representative APIs (PKCS#11, OpenSSL~3.0, JCA, Google Tink, AWS KMS, and HashiCorp Vault Transit) against the framework, revealing three pervasive and independent gaps: no system supports intent-based key creation, none provides policy-driven algorithm selection (as distinct from access control), and none offers dedicated/first-class operations for algorithm transformation of existing keys. These gaps are individually sufficient to prevent agile migration, explaining why the post-quantum transition remains a software engineering problem despite decades of API progress.

</details>


### [154] [Intent-Based Cryptographic API Design for Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13445)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出当前加密API缺乏加密敏捷性，难以支持向抗量子密码的迁移。作者提出一套基于五大架构特征的设计原则，并通过Protocol Buffers模式实现了一个支持策略选择、密钥演化与治理的加密敏捷API框架，使密码更新成为无需修改应用代码的运维过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织向抗量子密码迁移时面临巨大挑战，现有加密API针对特定算法设计，缺乏策略选择支持，无法平滑迁移现有密钥，导致算法过渡成为一个软件工程难题。

Method: 从抽象性、稳定性、时间灵活性、分离性和可扩展性五大架构特征推导出加密敏捷API设计原则；通过Protocol Buffers API设计模式实现这些原则，包括基于作用域的意图词汇表、抽象策略API、稳定密钥标识符与密钥演化操作。

Result: 提出了一套可实际实现的加密敏捷API框架：支持密钥创建与算法身份解耦、算法透明替换、策略无关的治理接口、密钥轮换/变换/迁移等演化操作，同时保留原始密钥身份与演化历史。

Conclusion: 采用该方法，密码更新可转化为运维操作，无需重写应用代码，从而有效应对抗量子密码迁移等算法过渡挑战。

Abstract: As organizations move toward post-quantum cryptography, they face the major challenge of updating cryptographic algorithms across large, complex software portfolios. However, most cryptographic APIs in use today were designed around specific algorithms. These APIs expect explicit use of specific algorithms, provide little or no support for policy-based algorithm selection, and offer no straightforward way to migrate existing keys to newer algorithms. This makes the transition to post-quantum cryptography challenging. The companion assessment framework identifies the barriers to cryptographic agility and explains why algorithm transition is largely a software engineering problem.
  To address the limitations of current cryptographic APIs, we identify the principles necessary to design a cryptographically agile API. The design principles are derived from five fundamental architectural characteristics (Abstraction, Stability, Temporal Flexibility, Separation, and Extensibility). We also show how the design principles can be implemented using several examples of Protocol Buffers API design patterns. In particular, we present an intent vocabulary that is based on scopes which allows for decoupling key creation from algorithm identities. It also supports transparent substitutions of algorithms in the applicable scope. Cryptographic governance is enabled by an abstract policy API that does not prescribe the policy format. Keys are represented by stable identifiers and support key evolution operations (rotation, transformation, migration), facilitating migration between algorithms and providers while tracking both the original key identity and its evolution history. With this approach, updating cryptography becomes an operational process without the need to rewrite application code.

</details>


### [155] [Differentially Private Hierarchical Heavy Hitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13563)
*Ari Biswas,Graham Cormode,Yaron Kanza,Divesh Srivastava,Zhengyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了差分隐私保护下的分层频繁项（HHH）发布问题，分别在非流式和流式设置下提出了解决方案。非流式设置中，相对误差与层次高度和频繁项数量无关；流式设置中，尽管近似函数具有高全局敏感性，但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层频繁项（HHH）是数据流挖掘中的重要问题，但现有研究未考虑数据隐私保护。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究在差分隐私约束下如何有效发布HHH结果。

Method: 分别针对非流式和流式两种场景设计差分隐私HHH发布方法。非流式场景下，证明了前缀残差计数的相对误差与层次高度和频繁项数量无关。流式场景下，分析了近似函数的高全局灵敏度问题，并证明了频率估计绝对误差与可用空间无关。

Result: 非流式设置：相对误差独立于层次高度和频繁项数量；流式设置：绝对误差独立于可用空间。

Conclusion: 首次在差分隐私框架下研究HHH发布问题，并证明了即使在流式设置下，也能以与空间无关的误差实现隐私保护。

Abstract: The task of finding _Hierarchical_ Heavy Hitters (HHH) was introduced by Cormode et al. [VLDB 2003] as a generalisation of the heavy hitter problem. While finding HHH in data streams has been studied extensively, the question of releasing HHH when the underlying data is private remains unexplored. In this paper, we study differentially private HHH release in both the streaming and non-streaming setting. In the non-streaming setting, we show the surprising result that the relative error in estimating the residual count for any prefix is independent of the height of the hierarchy and the number of heavy hitters in the stream. Meanwhile, in the streaming setting, although the exact version of HHH has low global sensitivity (as counting queries are 1-sensitive), the approximation functions due to streaming have high global sensitivity, linear in the available space. Despite this obstacle, we show that the absolute error for estimating frequencies in the steaming setting is independent of the available space.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond the IT Checklist: Engineering a Reasonable Standard of Care for Cyber Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13612)
*Matthew E. Jablonski,Linton Wells,Kathryn B. Laskey,F. Brett Berlin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文批判了美国当前以安全合规为中心的赛博政策在赛博-物理系统中的不足，通过对292份关键基础设施政策文档（2000-2025年）的编码分析，发现义务集中在"预期"阶段且强调行政合规，而"承受"和"恢复"阶段严重依赖与物理风险脱节的IT控制目录。作者识别出三大脱节问题，并提出了以危险特定可追溯性、结构化保障案例和赛博韧性工程为基础的现代化注意标准，同时建议联邦政策配套激励措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有美国赛博政策以安全为中心，将文档控制与事件报告视为安全的代理指标，这种做法在赛博-物理系统中不充分，因为数字故障可能造成动能伤害。作者希望揭示这种政策框架的根本缺陷并提出改革方向。

Method: 构建并编码包含292份关键基础设施政策文档（时间跨度2000-2025年）的语料库，考察"合理注意"如何在NIST SP 800-160 Vol.2韧性生命周期各阶段中操作化，绘制义务分布图谱。

Result: 义务集中在"预期"阶段并强调行政合规；"承受"和"恢复"阶段依赖对IT控制目录的委托引用，与基于物理的危害适配性差；识别出三大脱节：委托标准校准失当、恢复被定义为通知而非工程化导航、不同部门适应性要求不均衡。

Conclusion: 建议采用以危险特定可追溯性、结构化保障案例和赛博韧性工程为基础的现代化注意标准，并要求联邦政策配套针对性激励措施，使关键基础设施的韧性架构成为可行的商业决策而非无资金支持的期望。

Abstract: Current U.S. cyber policy, centered on security, often treats documentation of controls and incident reports as a proxy for safety in the built environment. This paper argues that such an approach is inadequate for cyber-physical systems, where digital failures can produce kinetic harm. We construct and code a corpus of critical infrastructure policy documents (N=292, 2000-2025) to examine how "reasonable care" is operationalized across the NIST SP 800-160 Vol.~2 resilience lifecycle. The resulting maps show that obligations are concentrated in the Anticipate phase and emphasize administrative compliance, while Withstand and Recover phases rely heavily on delegated references to IT-focused control catalogs that are poorly aligned with physics-based hazards. We identify three major disconnects: miscalibrated delegated standards, recovery defined as notification rather than engineered navigation, and uneven adaptation requirements across sectors. We then propose a modernized standard of care anchored in hazard-specific traceability, structured assurance cases, and cyber resiliency engineering. Finally, we recommend that federal policy pair these engineering obligations with targeted incentives so that resilient architectures for critical infrastructure become a viable business decision rather than an unfunded expectation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Identifiability Without Gaussianity: Symbolic World Models and Near-Infinite Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12471)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出物理基础符号架构（PGSA），证明其能在所有物理系统（无论潜在分布如何）中实现精确线性可辨识性和近乎无限的时序一致性，突破了Klindt等人证明的JEPA在高斯过程上的理论极限。研究表明，统计世界模型无法在非高斯系统中达到该性质，而符号化地扎根于世界因果动力学是实现近乎无限时序一致性的充分必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klindt等人证明了JEPA仅在高斯平稳过程中能实现线性可辨识性，这意味着对于非高斯物理系统，统计世界模型的表示误差会随时间单调增长。作者旨在突破这一理论限制，寻找能实现无界时序一致性的通用架构。

Method: 提出物理基础符号架构（PGSA），在理论上证明其三个核心性质：精确线性可辨识性（所有物理体系）、每步误差受限于数值精度、近乎无限的时序一致性。定理的核心代数部分在Lean 4 + Mathlib4 v4.31.0中形式化验证。

Result: PGSA在所有物理体系中实现精确线性可辨识性；每步误差仅受数值精度限制；可实现近乎无限的时序一致性。统计世界模型在任何非高斯系统中均无法达到该性质。四个定理的核心代数部分已完成形式化验证。

Conclusion: 符号化地扎根于世界因果动力学是在非高斯体系中实现近乎无限时序一致性的充分必要条件；Klindt等人发现的"高斯边界"本质上是统计对齐机制的产物，而非世界模型的固有属性。

Abstract: Klindt, LeCun, and Balestriero (arXiv:2605.26379) proved that Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) achieve linear identifiability, the linear recovery of the world's true latent variables, if and only if the world's latent dynamics follow a Gaussian, stationary process. This Gaussian boundary implies a fundamental limit on temporal consistency: for any non-Gaussian physical system, the representation error of a statistical World Model grows monotonically with time. We prove that this limit is an artifact of the statistical alignment mechanism, not a property of World Models in general. We introduce the Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA) and prove three results: (1) a PGSA achieves exact linear identifiability for all physical regimes, regardless of the latent distribution; (2) the per-step error of a PGSA is bounded by numerical precision alone; and (3) as a direct consequence, a PGSA maintains temporal consistency for an unbounded number of transitions, a property we term near-infinite temporal consistency. We further prove that statistical World Models cannot achieve this property for any non-Gaussian system, regardless of model capacity or the volume of training data. The algebraic cores of four of the theorems are formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib4 v4.31.0 (zero sorry placeholders); the Klindt et al. converse is taken as an external premise. The contrast establishes that symbolic grounding in the causal generator of the world's dynamics is the sufficient condition and, in non-Gaussian regimes, the only condition for near-infinite temporal consistency.

</details>


### [158] [Epistemic Uncertainty Is Not the Reducible Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12646)
*Robin Young*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了预测不确定性标准定义（认知不确定性可通过收集更多数据消除）与标准度量（互信息项）在外延上不一致，并提出了三分法分类：偶然不确定性、样本可缩减认知不确定性和机制可缩减认知不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类法将认知不确定性定义为可通过收集更多数据消除的部分，而标准度量将其等同于互信息项。作者发现这两个定义在外延上不一致，即存在构造使得互信息度量将所有不确定性归为认知类，但实际上无论收集多少训练数据都无法减少该不确定性。

Method: 通过显式构造反例证明定义与度量不一致；提出三分法（偶然、样本可缩减认知、机制可缩减认知不确定性）；推导观测价值的精确恒等式；分析集成不一致性（ensemble disagreement）的行为；设计有限样本下的证伪检验和种子扫描实验进行验证。

Result: 1）标准定义与度量在外延上不一致；2）可缩减性是(不确定性，采集类)对的属性，而非不确定性本身的属性；3）分布内数据从不减少机制不可缩减不确定性，且通常会增大它；4）集成不一致性追踪的是训练过程而非认知项，在一致训练下会坍缩到零，在插值下等于超参数缩放的初始化噪声。

Conclusion: 认知不确定性的标准定义与度量存在根本性不一致，需要重新审视不确定性量化的理论基础，集成不一致性等常用估计量可能并不反映真正的认知不确定性。

Abstract: The standard taxonomy of predictive uncertainty defines epistemic uncertainty as the part removable by collecting more data, while the standard measure identifies it with a mutual-information term. We prove the definition and the measure are extensionally inconsistent. On an explicit construction, the measure assigns all uncertainty to the epistemic class, yet no quantity of training data reduces it. Reducibility is instead a property of the pair (uncertainty, acquisition class), and the dichotomy resolves into three parts: aleatoric, sample-reducible epistemic, and mechanism-reducible epistemic uncertainty. An exact identity for the value of an observation shows that in-distribution data never reduces mechanism-irreducible uncertainty and generically increases it. Ensemble disagreement, the deployed epistemic estimate, tracks the training procedure rather than the epistemic term. It collapses to zero beneath a positive truth under consistent training, and equals hyperparameter-scaled initialization noise under interpolation. A finite-sample falsification test and seed-swept experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [159] [Prediction-Powered Causal Inference by Automatic Debiased Machine Learning and Semi-Supervised Riesz Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12892)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出预测驱动因果推断（PPCI）框架，在半监督场景下利用未标记辅助回归变量提升因果与结构参数的估计效率，推导了有效影响函数与效率界，并基于去偏机器学习（DML）框架构建了两种（EE-DML-PPCI和TMLE-DML-PPCI）渐近方差达到效率界的估计方法，同时开发了半监督广义Riesz回归用于Riesz表示估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半监督场景下，除了标记数据（包含结果和回归变量）外，还存在未标记的辅助回归变量。现有方法仅使用标记数据构造估计量，无法利用未标记数据中蕴含的信息来降低因果和结构参数估计的渐近方差。

Method: 首先推导了有效影响函数和效率界，证明辅助回归变量能够达到比仅用标记数据更小的渐近方差。然后将有效影响函数与去偏机器学习（DML）框架结合，提出两种方法：基于估计方程的EE-DML-PPCI和基于目标学习的TMLE-DML-PPCI。对于Riesz表示估计，开发了半监督广义Riesz回归，并提供了收敛速度保证。

Result: 推导了半监督场景下因果和结构参数估计的有效影响函数与效率界；所提DML-PPCI方法的渐近方差达到了推导的效率界；半监督广义Riesz回归方法具有收敛速度保证。

Conclusion: 半监督辅助回归变量可以显著提升因果推断的效率；所提出的DML-PPCI框架是有效且可行的，为半监督因果推断提供了理论基础和方法学工具。

Abstract: This study investigates semiparametric efficient estimation of causal and structural parameters in a semi-supervised setting. In our setting, unlabeled auxiliary regressors are available in addition to labeled observations consisting of outcomes and regressors. Our goal is to construct estimators of causal and structural parameters whose asymptotic variances are smaller than those of estimators constructed using only labeled data. We refer to this framework as prediction-powered causal inference (PPCI). We first derive the efficient influence function and the efficiency bound, which imply that the use of auxiliary regressors can attain a smaller asymptotic variance than the efficiency bound attainable from labeled observations alone. Then, by combining the efficient influence function with the debiased machine learning (DML) framework, we propose methods that we call DML-PPCI. If we construct an estimating-equation estimator, we refer to the method as EE-DML-PPCI; if we construct a targeted-learning estimator, we refer to the method as TMLE-DML-PPCI. The asymptotic variances of both estimators match our derived efficiency bound. In the construction of the estimators, estimation of the efficient influence function plays an important role. In our study, the efficient influence function is also a Neyman orthogonal score, which depends on the Riesz representer and the regression function. For Riesz representer estimation, we develop semi-supervised generalized Riesz regression with convergence rate guarantees.

</details>


### [160] [Robust State-Conditional Feature-Weighted Jump Models for Temporal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13146)
*Federico P. Cortese,Alessio Farcomeni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒的特征加权跳跃模型用于时间依赖聚类，通过Tukey双权损失函数实现鲁棒性，通过惩罚项实现时间平滑过渡，允许特征权重在不同状态间变化。模拟和实证应用表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间依赖聚类方法对异常值敏感，且无法有效识别不同时间状态下各特征的相关性差异，因此需要一种既能抵抗异常值干扰、又能自适应调整特征权重的鲁棒聚类模型。

Method: 采用Tukey双权损失函数替代传统平方损失以增强鲁棒性；引入惩罚项鼓励特征权重在时间上平滑过渡；设置额外参数控制特征权重在不同状态间的变异性，使每个状态可分配不同的特征重要性。

Result: 模拟实验显示该方法能准确恢复真实聚类序列并可靠识别相关特征，尤其在存在异常值时显著优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在时间依赖聚类中兼顾了鲁棒性和特征选择能力，并通过科索沃冲突伤亡数据和12个欧洲国家宏观经济数据的实证应用验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a robust feature-weighted jump model for time-dependent clustering. A penalty is used to encourage smoothness of transitions over time, while robustness is achieved through the use of a Tukey's biweight loss function. An additional parameter controls the variability of feature weights across states, allowing the model to assign state-specific relevance to each feature. We illustrate in simulation how the method accurately recovers the true cluster sequence and reliably identifies relevant features, outperforming competing approaches, particularly in the presence of outliers. We conclude with two empirical applications, one on the number of conflict-related homicides in Kosovo in the period 1998-2000, and another on macroeconomic performance of twelve European countries in the period 1949-2024.

</details>


### [161] [ProtoX-AD: Self-Explainable Time Series Anomaly Detection and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13277)
*Aitor Sánchez-Ferrera,Elisabeth Wetzer,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Michael Kampffmeyer,Robert Jenssen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ProtoX-AD是一个基于原型自解释框架的自监督时间序列异常检测方法，在保持检测性能的同时，通过原型学习提供可解释性，揭示异常特征模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督TSAD方法缺乏可解释性，无法提供被标记异常的特征信息，限制了其在实际应用中的可信度。

Method: 提出ProtoX-AD框架，学习变换感知的潜在表示和可解释原型，通过分类误差检测异常，同时利用原型提供异常模式的语义解释。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，ProtoX-AD达到与黑盒方法相当的检测性能，并提供比现有可解释基线更一致、语义更有意义的解释。

Conclusion: ProtoX-AD证明了在自监督TSAD中实现检测性能与可解释性兼得是可行的，且变换设计对检测和可解释性有系统性影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in time series anomaly detection (TSAD) have highlighted the effectiveness of self-supervised classification-based approaches. These methods apply transformations to normal training samples, training a classifier to recognize transformation-specific patterns that help identify anomalies through increased classification errors. Despite their strong performance, a significant challenge is their lack of explainability, as they provide limited insight into the characteristics of flagged anomalies. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoX-AD, a prototype-based self-explainable framework for self-supervised TSAD. ProtoX-AD learns transformation-aware latent representations alongside interpretable prototypes, enabling both accurate anomaly detection and the identification of distinct anomalous profiles through prototype-based explanations. Additionally, it allows for systematic analysis of how transformation design impacts detection performance and explainability. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ProtoX-AD achieves detection performance comparable to its black-box counterparts while offering more consistent and semantically meaningful explanations than existing explainable baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aitorzan3/ProtoX-AD.

</details>


### [162] [Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13295)
*Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification Cristian Buoncompagni,Stefano Pellegrino,Giulia Vannucci,Roberta Siciliano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的概率机器学习框架——同时潜在预算树（Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees, SLBT），用于在存在分层因子（如时间、空间或人口统计学变量）作为控制变量或潜在混杂因素时构建分类树。该方法通过模型驱动的分裂规则，将子节点解释为同时混合模型的潜在成分，并采用最小二乘法进行参数估计，支持交互式可视化、剪枝和决策树选择，最终应用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症疾病进展中的性别差异研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可解释人工智能时代，单一决策树因其易于解释而受到重视。然而，当存在分层因子（如时间、空间或人口统计学变量）作为控制变量或潜在混杂因素时，标准的树生长过程无法优化条件分裂规则。因此，需要一种新的树模型来应对这一问题。

Method: 提出基于模型的分类树分裂规则，子节点被解释为同时混合模型（如同时潜在预算模型及其约束版本）的潜在成分。混合参数驱动不同组的观测分配到子节点，潜在预算参数更新控制变量各水平的响应类分布。参数采用最小二乘法进行估计，并结合神经网络视角；提供交互式可视化、路径解释、视觉剪枝和决策树选择；提出处理不平衡响应类分布的措施。

Result: 该方法成功应用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）疾病进展中的性别差异研究。同时，SLBT算法库已在GitHub上公开发布。

Conclusion: SLBT为存在分层因子（混杂变量）的分类问题提供了一种可解释、模型驱动的树结构方法，兼具统计建模的可解释性与树状结构的直观性，在生物医学等领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: In the era of Explainable Artificial Intelligence, there is a renewed focus on single trees for their ease of interpretation. This paper introduces Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees, a probabilistic machine learning framework for classification trees in the presence of a stratification factor such as a temporal, spatial, or demographic variable, acting as a control variable or potential confounder.
