Standard tree growth procedures are not designed to optimize a conditional split rule. A model-based split rule is proposed in which child nodes are interpreted as latent components of a simultaneous mixture model, such as the Simultaneous Latent Budget Model and its constrained versions, fitted to the parent node. Mixing parameters drive the observations, differently for each group, to the child nodes whereas latent budgets parameters update the response classes profile of each level of the control variable. Parameters are estimated by least squares considering a neural network perspective of the model. An informative tree structure can be interactively visualized with interpretation aids on the node and the paths, including visual pruning and decision tree selection procedure. Suitable measures are proposed to handle an unbalanced response class distribution. The proposed methodology is applied to investigate gender-related differences in disease progression of Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis. The SLBT library with the various tree-based algorithms is available in the linked GitHub repository.

</details>


### [163] [Majority-of-Three is Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13614)
*Divit Rawal,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文给出简短证明：三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC学习框架中是最优的。这一结果简化了之前的投票学习算法（如Hanneke算法和Larsen的Bagging分析），证明最简单的投票方案即可达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在可实现PAC学习框架中寻找结构最简单、概率分析最简洁的最优学习算法，简化之前复杂的投票学习方案（如Hanneke的算法和Larsen的Bagging分析）。

Method: 通过概率分析证明三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC框架下是最优的，对比并简化了之前的投票学习方法。

Result: 证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC设置中是最优学习者，且证明简短，算法结构更简单。

Conclusion: 最简单的多数投票方案即可在可实现PAC学习中达到最优性能，简化了该领域的算法设计和理论分析。

Abstract: We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Phase transition of Schott's statistic for high-dimensional heavy-tailed data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12943)
*Hantao Chen,Guangming Pan,Cheng Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schott统计量在α正则变化总体下的渐近分布，发现了相变现象：轻尾(α>3)时呈现α-自由渐近分布，重尾(α<3)时呈现新的渐近正态分布，并提出了适用于未知位置参数和所有α>0的标准化检验统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Schott统计量的渐近理论对尾部分布参数α的依赖关系尚不明确，尤其是在高维框架下重尾与轻尾情况的行为差异未被系统刻画。

Method: 基于α正则变化假设，分别对α>3（轻尾）和α<3（重尾）两种情形建立渐近理论，推导了渐近分布的显式表达式，并构造了相合方差估计量。

Result: 发现相变阈值α=3：α>3时无需严格约束p/n比即可得到α-自由渐近分布；α<3时得到方差显式依赖于α的新渐近正态分布；提出了适用于所有α>0且未知位置参数的标准化检验统计量。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了Schott统计量的高维渐近理论，揭示了重尾程度对检验行为的关键影响，为实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Consider Schott's statistic (Schott, 2005) defined as the squared Frobenius norm of the sample correlation matrix for data from $α$-regularly varying populations. We investigate its asymptotic distribution in a general framework characterized by data dimension p, sample size n, and regularly varying coefficients $α$. In particular, we identify a phase transition phenomenon in the asymptotic behavior. For light-tailed populations ($α> 3$), we revisit the $α$-free asymptotic distribution but relax the constraint on the ratio of $p/n$. For heavy-tailed populations ($α< 3$), we derive a new asymptotic normal distribution whose variance explicitly depends on $α$. We also propose a consistent estimator for the asymptotic variance such that the standardized Schott's test statistic remains applicable for unknown location parameters and all $α> 0$.

</details>


### [165] [Characterizing metric-space-valued processes: separating classes and weak invariance principles for measure-theoretic inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13084)
*Anne van Delft*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究非Hilbert空间中随机过程的推断问题，通过利用球性质（ball property）建立测度论推断框架，提出Lp基替代方法规避小概率极限分布不存在的问题，并建立U-过程的极限理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法依赖于空间的Hilbertian等距嵌入能力，但当度量空间无法等距嵌入Hilbert空间时会产生几何畸变。本文旨在突破这一局限，为更广泛的度量空间（满足球性质）中的随机过程提供统一的推断方法论。

Method: 证明Hilbertian等距嵌入空间是满足球性质的度量空间的严格子类；利用Borel概率测度由球上的值唯一确定这一分离类性质；建立时变随机测度族的一致收敛性和非平稳随机场的弱不变原理；提出Lp基替代度量以规避小概率快速衰减导致的极限分布问题；对可Hilbertian嵌入空间建立退化与非退化多参数U-过程的极限理论。

Result: 明确了空间拓扑、几何与时变依赖结构之间的复杂相互作用；揭示了依赖性和几何复杂度如何影响样本路径正则性；证明了局部差异检验在动态参数区间下保持渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于球性质的测度论推断框架有效拓展了随机过程分析的适用范围；Lp基方法成功规避了传统supremum基度量在不利条件下的失效问题；所建立的极限理论为实际推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This article investigates stochastic processes taking values in metric spaces that lack a topological vector space structure, a regime characterized by intricate interplay between topological, geometric, and temporal dependence structures. It is formally established that spaces admitting an isometric Hilbertian embedding constitute a strict subclass within the much broader class of metric spaces possessing the ball property. While traditional kernel methods are susceptible to geometric distortion when the underlying space cannot be isometrically embedded into a Hilbert space, we bypass such limitations by exploiting a fundamental structural property inherent to this broader class; namely, that Borel probability measures are uniquely determined by their values on balls. These separating classes provide the foundation for the subsequently introduced measure-theoretic inference methodology. We derive uniform convergence of a family of time-dependent random measures, alongside weak invariance principles for the corresponding nonstationary random fields. This framework explicitly exposes how dependence and geometric complexity influence sample path regularity. Furthermore, because the rapid decay of small-ball probabilities can prohibit the existence of limiting distributions for supremum-based discrepancy measures, we develop $L^p$-based alternatives. By directly leveraging the introduced convergence results, this approach circumvents the need for higher-order $U$-process formulations. Finally, for spaces that do admit an isometric Hilbertian embedding, and where $U$-processes naturally arise, we establish limit theory for both degenerate and nondegenerate multi-parameter $U$-processes, and demonstrate that local discrepancy tests maintain asymptotic stability under dynamic parameter regimes.

</details>


### [166] [Consistency of variational approximations under bounded Kullback--Leibler divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13230)
*Hien Duy Nguyen,Jacob Westerhout,Thomas Guilmeau,Julyan Arbel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究变分近似是否继承后验一致性，证明当变分分布与目标后验之间的KL散度一致有界时，变分序列也具有一致性，并给出了易于验证的对数矩条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分方法是贝叶斯推断中广泛使用的近似工具，但其近似结果是否保持后验一致性（即随着数据增多是否收敛到真实参数）这一理论基础尚不清晰，需要严格的理论保证。

Method: 在一般度量空间上，证明若变分近似序列与目标后验序列之间的KL散度一致有界，且目标后验序列是紧的，则变分近似序列也是紧的。进一步，若目标后验弱收敛到真实参数的狄拉克测度，则变分近似也一致收敛。同时给出对数矩条件来验证有界性条件，并应用于光滑广义后验分布。

Result: 建立了变分近似继承后验一致性的充分条件：变分分布与目标后验的KL散度一致有界。该条件可通过简单的对数矩条件验证，适用于光滑广义后验。

Conclusion: 为变分贝叶斯方法的理论可靠性提供了严谨的渐进一致性保证，为实际应用中选择变分近似族提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Variational methods are widely used to approximate posterior distributions in Bayesian inference when exact computation is infeasible. We study when such approximations inherit posterior consistency. Our first result shows that, on a general metric space, a uniform bound on the Kullback--Leibler divergence from the approximating measures to a tight sequence of target measures forces the approximating sequence to be tight. It follows that if the target posteriors converge weakly to a Dirac mass at the true parameter, then any variational sequence with bounded Kullback--Leibler divergence to the targets is also consistent. We also give simple logarithmic-moment conditions that verify this boundedness condition, and illustrate them for smooth generalised posterior distributions.

</details>


### [167] [Generalization Bounds for Transformer-Based Next-Token Prediction in a Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13280)
*Insung Kong,Niklas Dexheimer,Johannes Schmidt-Hieber*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM预训练，提出了一种基于扩展log-bilinear语言模型的文本数据分布，并推导了深度Transformer架构的泛化界，揭示了与网络架构、词汇量、文档数量和文档长度的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM预训练的统计学理解不足，需要分析Transformer架构在能体现文本数据关键特征的数据分布上的表现。

Method: 基于自然语言处理中log-bilinear语言模型的扩展，构建文本数据分布，并在此数据生成过程下推导深度Transformer的泛化界。

Result: 推导出深度Transformer架构的泛化界，明确了其对网络架构、词汇量、文档数量和文档长度的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解LLM预训练中Transformer的统计特性提供了理论分析基础。

Abstract: A refined statistical understanding of LLM pre-training requires the analysis of the transformer architecture for data distributions that encapsulate key characteristics of text data. To address this, we propose a text data distribution based on an extension of the log-bilinear language model from the natural language processing literature. For this data generating process, we derive generalization bounds for deep transformer architectures, highlighting the dependence on the network architecture, the vocabulary size, the number of documents and the document length.

</details>


### [168] [Asymptotic regimes for maximum likelihood estimation in the Ewens--Pitman model: When the strength parameter matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13554)
*Filippo Ascolani,Mario Beraha,Stefano Favaro*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Ewens-Pitman随机分割模型中MLE的大样本渐近行为，发现四种不同机制，并提出了通过允许θ随样本量增长的缩放Ewens-Pitman模型来突破无限可交换性约束的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献隐含地将研究限制在无限可交换性所施加的两种渐近机制上，而忽略了参数θ在渐近行为中的关键作用。作者希望揭示更丰富的渐近行为，并解决经典框架无法拟合实际数据中某些频率谱的问题。

Method: 在温和的数据生成机制假设下，分析了Ewens-Pitman模型参数(α,θ)的MLE大样本渐近行为。通过区分频率谱的四种极限行为来划分渐近机制。引入缩放Ewens-Pitman模型，允许θ随样本量n增长，从而突破无限可交换性带来的结构约束。

Result: 1) 发现四种不同的渐近机制，区别于以往仅关注两种机制；2) 证明了θ在渐近行为中可发挥关键作用；3) 揭示了无限可交换性强制了不同块数量与频率谱之间的刚性结构关系；4) 缩放Ewens-Pitman模型能够克服这种局限性。

Conclusion: 缩放Ewens-Pitman模型提供了更大的灵活性，能够拟合经典框架无法捕获的真实数据频率谱，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study the large sample asymptotic behaviour of the Maximum Likelihood Estimator of the discount and strength parameters $(α,θ)$ in the Ewens--Pitman model for random partitions, under mild assumptions on the data-generating mechanism. We show that four distinct regimes arise, depending on the limiting behaviour of the frequency spectrum. In particular, in contrast with previous work, we find that $θ$ may play a crucial role asymptotically. We further show that the existing literature implicitly focuses on only two of these regimes, and we relate this restriction to the constraints imposed by infinite exchangeability. Under the latter, indeed, the number of distinct blocks and the frequency spectrum are necessarily tied by a rigid structural relation. We prove that this lack of flexibility can be overcome through what we call the scaled Ewens--Pitman model, in which $θ$ is allowed to grow with the sample size $n$. Finally, we provide empirical evidence from real-world data showing that such extensions are needed to capture frequency spectra that fall outside the classical Ewens--Pitman framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用威布尔分布（极值理论中的两参数族）作为Transformer中逐元素权重幅度分布的统一诊断框架。通过分析12个模型条目（7个架构家族），发现前馈网络和注意力输出投影（传输类）的威布尔形状参数k集中在[1.186, 1.204]区间（跨家族变异系数仅0.51%）；注意力输入投影（选择类）偏离威布尔族；尺度参数λ随训练增长并与√(η/λ_wd)呈强相关（r=0.94）。两个参数携带独立信息：k标记功能类别，λ标记训练进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合统计量（如范数、方差）无法解析Transformer训练过程中各层、各组件权重分布的细粒度演化差异，需要一个架构无关且具有物理意义的诊断框架来区分不同功能模块的权重分布行为。

Method: 采用威布尔分布拟合每层每个权重矩阵的幅度分布，通过中间80%分位数概率图拟合提取形状参数k和尺度参数λ；在12个模型（Pythia、OLMo-1/2、LLaMA-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5/3）的每个检查点上逐组件独立分析。

Result: 1) FFN和注意力输出投影（传输类）终端k值高度稳定（中位数[1.186,1.204]）；2) 注意力输入投影（选择类）偏离威布尔分布，偏离程度受Q/K存储方式影响；3) λ随训练增长，与√(η/λ_wd)呈0.94相关性。

Conclusion: 威布尔分布的两个参数解耦了功能类别（k）与训练进度（λ）的信息，为理解Transformer各组件不同的训练动态提供了可解释的诊断工具。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [170] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了物理信息神经网络（PINN）与标准临床基线方法（非线性最小二乘NLS）在化疗药代动力学（PK）问题上的表现。在可解析的线性二室模型中，PINN与NLS性能相当同时能输出组织浓度曲线；在Michaelis-Menten非线性模型中，NLS因模型误设而失效，而PINN能诚实反映结构不可识别性，且仅需少量稀疏组织观测便可大幅改善参数可识别性。核心主张是PINN提供了一种统一框架：在经典问题上匹配教科书级估计器，暴露后者隐藏的结构可识别性问题，并能将异质测量统一纳入单一损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物领域的部分观测问题（如化疗药代动力学）中，已知动力学方程但部分隔室（如组织浓度）无法测量，传统方法（如基于解析解的NLS）受限于模型封闭形式的假设，无法处理非线性扩展模型（如Michaelis-Menten饱和消除），且无法揭示模型的结构可识别性问题。PINN作为能够直接整合物理方程与数据的工具，为解决这类问题提供了新思路。

Method: 在两种PK问题上对比三种方法：PINN、标准临床基线NLS（基于解析的双指数血浆解的非线性最小二乘法）、以及纯数据驱动的MLP。线性问题使用标准二室模型；非线性问题使用Michaelis-Menten扩展模型。评估指标包括参数恢复精度、组织浓度预测误差，以及模型对结构可识别性的表现。

Result: 线性二室模型：NLS近最优，PINN与之匹配（仅差小常数因子），且单次训练即可输出组织曲线；数据MLP在组织上误差约10倍。Michaelis-Menten模型：NLS因双指数解析解不存在而返回无意义参数；PINN诚实收敛到k12→0的盆地，揭示模型非可识别。加入两稀疏组织观测后：5次实验PINN恢复k21误差<1%，Vmax、Km在一个标准差内，k12从0.02恢复至0.82（仍低于真值约2σ）。

Conclusion: PINN并非在所有场景下优于NLS，而是提供了一种统一方法论：在经典可解析问题上匹配教科书级估计器，在非线性扩展中暴露传统方法隐藏的结构可识别性问题，并能灵活吸收异质测量数据。这一框架为部分观测生物系统的建模与推理提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [171] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了因果知识在何种条件下能够显著提升监督域适应的有限样本性能。在线性回归框架下，通过理论推导匹配的上下界，证明了有限样本增益由候选预测器之间的目标风险间隔和源估计误差共同决定：当间隔足够大时，自适应聚合方法可匹配最优候选预测器并避免负迁移；当间隔过小时，任何算法都无法获得更快的有限样本率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果域泛化工作展示了共享因果结构可诱导跨域不变预测器，但这类群体级因果不变性在有限样本场景下能否带来实际收益尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究因果知识（完整或部分）在何种条件下能可证明地提升监督域适应性能。

Method: 以线性回归为切入点，将完整的或部分的因果知识建模为一系列不变或可能不变的候选特征子集，每个子集产生一个基于源数据训练的候选预测器。推导了匹配的上下界，证明有限样本增益受候选预测器间的目标风险间隔与源估计误差共同控制。当间隔相对于目标样本量足够大时，采用自适应聚合方法可匹配最优候选预测器；间隔过小时则无算法可可靠利用候选集合加速有限样本率。进一步将间隔与线性结构因果模型中的偏移幅度建立联系。

Result: 提供了匹配的上下界理论结果，刻画了因果知识在监督域适应中带来有限样本增益的充分必要条件。在真实世界因果基准上验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 因果知识在监督域适应中的有效性取决于目标风险间隔与源估计误差的相对大小。该工作为理解因果先验知识在有限样本域适应中的价值提供了理论框架，并为实际应用中选择合适的因果知识利用策略提供了指导。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [172] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在部分观测线性动力学系统的数据上通过经验风险最小化训练的两层线性自回归模型，能够自然地学习近似卡尔曼滤波，其学习到的隐层表示与最优卡尔曼滤波产生的状态估计在相似变换下等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在语言、视频等序列数据中表现出色，但其如何以及为何学习到潜在表示的理论机制尚不明确，本文旨在从理论上揭示这一过程。

Method: 作者从三个核心洞察展开：1) 证明卡尔曼滤波可以被自回归模型以有界截断误差近似；2) 尽管优化问题非凸，两层模型的优化景观是良性的（所有驻点要么是严格鞍点，要么是全局最小值）；3) 提供预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的有限样本保证。

Result: 理论结果表明，自回归模型学习到的隐层表示与卡尔曼滤波的状态估计在相似变换下等价，并给出了有限样本下的预测误差、参数估计误差和状态恢复的理论保证。

Conclusion: 自回归模型能够隐式地学习到部分观测线性动力学系统的最优滤波机制，为理解此类模型的表示学习能力提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [173] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种主流概率物理系统预测方法——生成模型（扩散/流匹配）与CRPS训练集成模型——在匹配模型规模和计算预算下的可靠性，发现CRPS集成在不确定性校准、推理速度上更优，且隐空间训练对其性能影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前两类概率预测方法（生成模型与CRPS集成）虽均有良好预测精度，但其不确定性预测的可靠性缺乏系统性评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在匹配模型大小和计算预算的条件下，构建统一评估框架，对多种二维时空物理系统进行单步预测和自回归 rollout 测试，通过预测区间的经验覆盖率评估可靠性，同时考虑准确性和计算效率。

Result: CRPS训练集成在单步预测和自回归 rollout 中均表现出更可靠的校准性能；生成模型在隐空间训练时覆盖能力较弱，在原始空间训练时覆盖能力与CRPS集成相当但推理延迟大；隐空间训练对CRPS集成性能退化不明显；两类方法均具有良好预测精度。

Conclusion: CRPS训练集成在不确定性校准和推理效率方面优于生成模型，是更实用的概率物理系统预测方案；同时开源AutoCast和AutoSim以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [174] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jeffrey guidance，一种基于Jeffrey条件规则的扩散模型控制框架，通过最小化扰动联合分布来实现对生成分布的精确控制，在CIFAR-10、FFHQ和CelebA-HQ上分别实现了FID降低和公平性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能通过引导灵活控制生成，但现有方法在复杂场景下目标分布往往仅通过采样规则或启发式能量函数隐式定义，缺乏统一的原理性框架。

Method: 提出Jeffrey guidance，利用Jeffrey条件规则更新边际分布以逼近预设目标分布，同时保持条件结构并最小化对联合分布的扰动。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上使用Inception嵌入作为目标时显著降低FID；在CelebA-HQ上通过强制属性独立性实现了公平性引导。

Conclusion: Jeffrey guidance为扩散模型提供了一种超越标准引导的统一且原理性的控制框架，可应用于分布匹配、公平性等多种场景。

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>


### [175] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的投机解码方案，将LLM中的块验证机制适配到连续扩散模型，实现了无需训练的Free Drafter方法，在无额外训练和可忽略开销的情况下，相比现有投机方法获得最高6.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法很难适配连续扩散模型：连续空间中的残差分布采样计算效率低，现有方法要么使用计算低效的采样技术，要么依赖替代方案，无法高效实现原始投机采样机制。

Method: 提出一种新的连续扩散模型投机采样方案，高效实现了原始投机采样机制；将LLM中的块验证（block verification）适配到扩散模型，形式上证明其能提高草稿接受率；形式化分析了无需训练的启发式自投机草案生成器Free Drafter。

Result: Free Drafter在无需额外训练且仅有可忽略额外开销的情况下，相比现有投机方法获得最高6.3%的加速。

Conclusion: 本文成功将投机采样中的块验证机制从LLM领域适配到扩散模型，并提出了一种高效且无需训练的Free Drafter方法，验证了块验证能提升草稿接受率，为扩散模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [176] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"可模拟过程"框架，证明当学习器可以访问近似数据生成过程的模拟器时，即使面对任意复杂和依赖的数据过程，也能获得与经典独立数据场景相同的学习保证（基于VC维的误差界）。进一步展示了条件采样的统计和计算优势，并提出了一个能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程下任意VC类的单一算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论中的泛化结果大多依赖于数据独立性假设，而强依赖数据场景下的理论结果非常有限。为弥合这一差距，本文探索了在数据生成过程任意复杂且高度依赖时，是否仍能恢复经典学习保证。

Method: 引入"可模拟过程"框架，假设学习器可访问一个近似数据分布的模拟器，通过该模拟器进行学习分析。利用VC维理论建立误差界，并引入有界多项式时间Kolmogorov复杂度来衡量过程复杂性。

Result: 1) 在可模拟过程框架下，恢复与经典独立数据场景相同的VC维相关误差界；2) 条件采样具有严格的统计和计算优势；3) 提出单一算法能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程下的任意VC类，遗憾界由时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓展了经典PAC学习模型，将学习理论从独立性假设推广到更广泛的依赖数据场景，为强依赖过程的学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [177] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-FBD通过将ResNet分解为六个功能块并维护颜色变体仓库，实现了联邦学习中的块级隔离、隐私保护和即时机器遗忘，以微小精度代价换取强大的安全与隐私保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如FedAvg）将客户端视为黑箱，无法隔离恶意参与者、审计客户端影响或实现参与者退出后的被遗忘权。

Method: 将ResNet骨架分解为stem、四个残差组和分类头六个功能块，维护N个颜色变体仓库，每个变体由独立追踪且带贡献者戳记的块组装而成。

Result: 在MedMNIST-2D、PathMNIST(224x224)和CIFAR-10上，Fed-FBD在充足数据集上牺牲0.3%-3.1%的IID精度换取上述保障；在Dirichlet alpha=1.0三个数据集上保持与FedAvg相差0.8%-4.0%；所有六种对抗攻击仅影响被投毒客户端自身的块，干净颜色AUC漂移不超过±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD以可接受的精度代价实现了架构级的块隔离、设计层面的隐私保护和亚秒级免重训练机器遗忘，弥补了FedAvg在安全与隐私方面的关键缺失。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

</details>


### [178] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大型语言模型生成的代码存在功能性记忆问题，即模型能复现训练数据中的功能逻辑，即使文本上不相似，现有基于文本重叠的审计指标无法检测到这种现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注训练数据与模型输出之间的文本重叠，但代码具有功能等价而文本不相似的特点，因此需要研究模型是否能记忆并复现训练数据中的功能逻辑。

Method: 构建反事实实验：对比中期训练模型（暴露于目标代码）与预训练参考模型（未暴露），用Python函数签名提示两个模型，分别测量文本相似度和功能相似度（LLM-as-a-judge和基于执行的方法）。

Result: 发现了明确的功能性记忆证据，表明模型能够复现训练数据中的功能逻辑，即使文本上存在差异。

Conclusion: 需要开发超越文本重叠的审计指标来检测模型的功能性记忆现象。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [179] [PolicyGuard: Towards Test-time and Step-level Adversary Defense for Reinforcement Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12896)
*Junfeng Guo Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolicyGuard，一种基于高斯过程后验方差的自适应伪轨迹方法，用于强化学习智能体在测试时进行逐时间步的后门攻击检测，在多种RL游戏中实现了先进检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后门防御方法要么需要访问智能体内部参数，要么仅在模型或轨迹层面操作，或仅限于特定攻击类型，缺乏通用的逐时间步检测方法。

Method: PolicyGuard利用高斯过程(GP)后验方差，并引入自适应伪轨迹技术，使得不确定性计算能够作用于单个时间步，实现测试时逐时间步的后门检测。

Result: 在7个RL游戏上的实验表明，PolicyGuard在大多数场景下达到最先进检测性能，针对扰动型攻击平均AUROC为0.856，针对对手智能体攻击为0.859。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard是一种有效的测试时逐时间步后门防御方法，同时提供了GP后验方差有效性的理论依据，为RL安全防护提供了新思路。

Abstract: While real-world applications of reinforcement learning (RL) are becoming increasingly popular, the security of RL systems deserve more attention and exploration. In particular, recent work has revealed that RL agents are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a victim agent behaves normally under standard conditions but executes malicious actions when a specific trigger is activated. Existing backdoor defenses for RL either require access to the agent's internal parameters, operate only at the model or trajectory level, or are limited to specific attack types. To ensure the security of RL agents, we propose \texttt{PolicyGuard}, a \textit{test-time step-level} backdoor defense which leverages Gaussian Process (GP) posterior variance and adapts pseudo trajectories to enable uncertainty computation for individual time step. Besides, we also provide theoretical foundations to explain the efficacy of GP posterior variance. Extensive experiments across seven RL games demonstrate that PolicyGuard achieves state-of-the-art detection performance in most cases, with average AUROC of 0.856 for perturbation-based attacks and 0.859 for adversary-agent attacks.

</details>


### [180] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将幻觉检测重新定义为快速变化检测问题，提出了基于马尔可夫模型的序贯分析方法，发现因果循环标注器（CUSUM变体）在匹配虚警率下检测延迟为11-13个token，远优于线性基线（31个token），并通过Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理揭示了与理论下界（约1.3个token）之间存在数量级差距的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的token级幻觉检测器用AUC等分类指标评估，但流式监控更关注检测延迟（幻觉发生到报警间的token数）。传统分类指标掩盖了延迟结构，需要更适合的序贯分析框架。

Method: 将幻觉起始检测建模为快速变化检测问题；用RAGTruth验证一阶马尔可夫潜在状态模型；应用Lorden下界；将因果循环标注器解释为CUSUM（学习增量）；通过受控分解分离per-token分数和时序累积的贡献；使用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理分析差距。

Result: Lorden下界：虚警率0.01时理论检测延迟约1.3个token。因果循环标注器（CUSUM变体）检测延迟11-13个token，线性per-token基线为31个token。优势主要来自更好的per-token分数，而非时序累积。学习分数仅实现特征携带散度的1/4.5，剩余差距为有限时域效应。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了检测延迟结构，序贯分析使其可测量；现有方法距离理论最优仍有显著差距，主要受限于per-token分数的信息利用效率。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [181] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann Attention），通过伊辛模型引入可学习的成对耦合项，使注意力机制能够显式建模位置间的协同或对抗性交互，在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上优于标准softmax注意力，并展示了量子退火在训练中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度计算相关性，softmax归一化虽引入位置间竞争，但未显式参数化注意力决策之间的可学习交互，限制了模型直接建模注意力机制内部的合作或对抗性共注意力结构的能力。

Method: 将注意力模式建模为伊辛模型驱动的能量函数，在标准数据相关的局部场（local fields）基础上增加可学习的成对耦合项（pairwise couplings），通过能量最小化或采样得到注意力分布，训练可采用精确玻尔兹曼计算或非绝热量子退火。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务中，玻尔兹曼注意力在标准Transformer架构下一致优于softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而更显著；四路消融实验证实改进来源于可学习的成对耦合项；量子退火可达到与精确计算相当的竞争性能。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强，伊辛公式化为量子计算方法（如量子退火）在注意力训练中的应用开辟了自然路径。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [182] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决RL-based LLM路由中异构任务下的奖励信号模糊和优化偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL-based LLM路由方法在异构任务中难以提供信息丰富且可比较的学习信号：多目标聚合为单一标量奖励导致信用分配模糊和优化冲突；奖励信号在不同实例间差异大，引入优化偏差，偏向简单样本而非信息性样本。

Method: 1) 层次化奖励分解机制，结合组件优势估计；2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每数据集归一化校准优化变异性。

Result: 在7个数据集上，ReCal一致提升了路由性能和训练稳定性，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ReCal通过奖励校准有效解决了异构任务下的学习信号问题，提升了LLM路由的优化质量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [183] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使未经微调的LLM能直接利用其推理能力进行时间序列分析，在编辑、描述和问答三项任务上优于原始序列表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将原始数值序列输入LLM或微调预训练模型，存在模态不匹配问题，导致长序列性能下降且计算开销大。需要一种有效的表示方法弥合时间序列与LLM文本模态之间的鸿沟。

Method: T2SP将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化的符号程序形式表示。该方法无需训练，通过将时间结构提取从模型转移到表示层面，使现成的LLM能够直接利用其推理能力处理时间序列。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三项推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示一致地提升了性能，减少了推理时间，并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP提供了时间序列与LLM之间的有效接口，通过将时间序列表示为与LLM原生训练数据对齐的程序格式，弥合了模态差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [184] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数）方法，通过将查询点与支撑点的平方距离作为核心，解决了传统Christoffel函数在高维数据中矩阵规模指数增长的可扩展性问题，在ADBench基准上超越14个SOTA基线，实现了理论稳健且普适的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Christoffel函数异常检测方法因需要求逆一个随数据维度指数级增长的矩阵，导致即使在中低维数据集上也无法实际应用，亟需解决其可扩展性瓶颈，同时保留其关键的on-off支撑二象性行为和精确支撑形状捕捉等理论特性。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），基于查询点与支撑点之间的平方距离构建，避免了高维矩阵求逆操作，从而将Christoffel函数从多变量形式降为单变量形式，保持原有理论性质的同时大幅降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）指标上一致优于14个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: UCF解决了Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，为异常检测工具箱增添了一种稳健、有理论根基且普适的方法，拓展了基于多项式优化的异常检测在更高维度数据上的实用前景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [185] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出推测性回滚修正（SRC），一种面向可重置智能体环境的分支级模仿学习框架。通过固定水平分支审查机制，让学生智能体先执行短推测段，教师仅在局部进展中断时定位首次有害偏离，并回滚至有用前缀；成功轨迹经硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量多样性存档。在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条验证通过轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例，在恢复效果与查询次数之间取得了更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式Web智能体的模仿学习面临专家干预时机的两难困境：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复；过早或过度干预则使智能体过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一路径的局部最优。需要一种能平衡恢复能力与专家查询效率的干预框架。

Method: SRC框架包含固定水平分支审查（学生执行短推测段后教师审查）、首次有害偏离定位与回滚（保留有用前缀）、硬验证器过滤成功轨迹、轻量级质量多样性存档存储多样化成功方案，以及基于局部修正和验证通过轨迹的下一步动作监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity基准上，SRC收集了977条验证通过轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例；固定水平分支审查在恢复效果与查询次数之间优于逐状态审查，同时保留了多样化的验证通过解决方案。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级回滚机制有效解决了交互式Web智能体模仿学习中专家干预时机的权衡问题，既能纠正早期错误避免状态不可恢复，又能保留有用前缀和多样化成功轨迹，为可重置智能体环境提供了一种高效且实用的训练范式。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [186] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对卡车车队的基于状态的预测性维护（PdM）提出了一种新方法，该方法假设被监测组件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，通过选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式，利用机器学习分类模型进行预测。实验表明，该方法在Scania Component X数据集上相比现有SOTA方法降低了成本，并通过AutoML简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的基于状态的预测性维护在近年来受到广泛关注，但其实际实施面临三大挑战：车辆产生的大量数据、通过传感器数据检测故障的固有复杂性，以及在解决方案实施中寻找成本效益平衡的困难。现有方法尚不能很好地解决这些问题。

Method: 提出一种基于单调非递减时间序列假设的PdM方法：仅从时间序列中选择最新观测值，将其转换为表格格式，然后使用适用于表格数据的机器学习分类模型进行故障预测，并借助AutoML简化建模流程。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提出的方法相比当前最先进的（SOTA）方法降低了维护成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于单调非递减时间序列假设的PdM方法能有效降低成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模过程，为卡车车队的预测性维护提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [187] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中相位变化的量化敏感层，仅对这些层使用8-bit激活精度，其余保持4-bit，解决了W4A4KV4量化中激活值剧烈波动导致的量化不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法通过变换（正交旋转、仿射缩放）缓解大规模激活值问题，但忽略了残差流跨层动态特性——大规模激活值以相位模式在网络深度上出现和消失，导致残差变化巨大，静态变换无法解决动态量化不稳定。

Method: 提出Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标量化残差流动态行为；基于此设计DynamicPTQ，动态识别量化敏感层，对敏感层分配8-bit激活精度，其余层保持W4A4KV4量化；可集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等基线方法中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐量提升，内存开销适中。

Conclusion: 证明了通过识别残差流相位动态进行混合精度量化是迈向鲁棒低比特LLM推理的实用路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [188] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示具有兼容性（即模型更新后表示可互换使用），并提出结合交叉熵损失与对比度损失的凸组合方法来捕捉高阶依赖关系，在无需重新处理图库图像的条件下实现了模型更新和替换时的持续检索服务，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习兼容表示以支持模型更新后特征表示的互换使用，确保检索服务无需重新处理图库图像即可无缝运行。

Method: 使用d-Simplex固定分类器学习平稳表示，通过交叉熵损失与对比度损失的凸组合训练模型，在保持兼容性约束的同时捕捉高阶统计依赖关系。

Result: 平稳表示在模型顺序微调和替换场景下，无需重新处理图库图像即可实现不间断检索服务，性能达到SOTA。

Conclusion: d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示满足兼容性的形式化定义，结合对比度损失可更好捕捉高阶依赖，为实际学习场景中的模型更新与替换提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [189] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对循环神经网络（RNN）的抽象-精化验证框架，通过分区处理激活函数交叉零区间来减少非线性松弛误差，并引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略来控制组合复杂度，在CIFAR10和MNIST基准上显著提升了验证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证具有挑战性，因为非线性松弛引入的近似误差会通过循环连接传播并随时间累积，导致可扩展的线性边界传播方法过于保守，尤其在预激活区间穿越零点时无法认证实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 1) 抽象-精化框架：对穿越零点的预激活区间进行分区，在每个精化分支上使ReLU激活精确化，并为tanh和sigmoid等平滑激活函数构造更紧的线性包络；2) SHAP引导的时间步选择策略：按隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献度排序，仅精化时序上最关键的时间步，以控制长序列中分区的组合爆炸成本。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST笔画基准上，该方法相比纯抽象基线在验证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧度上取得了一致的改进，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型之间清晰的计算开销权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的抽象-精化框架有效缓解了RNN验证中的松弛误差累积问题，SHAP引导的时间步选择在保持可扩展性的同时提升了验证精度，但不同激活函数下的运行时开销存在显著差异。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [190] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Net-Ev²，一个联合事件语义和网络拓扑的生成式模拟器，通过结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程，实现网络事件演化的高保真模拟，并引入多模态基准Net-Ev²-6.5M和新评估指标JL-MMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少现实世界中的试错成本是决策制定的核心目标，现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性与非结构化语义，也难以捕捉拓扑结构在网络事件演化中的作用。

Method: Net-Ev²包含两阶段：结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程（通过U-Net-like图下采样和上采样实现去噪），推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。

Result: 在四个大规模道路网络上的实验表明，Net-Ev²达到了最先进的性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²能有效模拟扰动事件在网络中的传播影响，兼顾结构化属性和语义信息，并保持网络拓扑保真度。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [191] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出μVLA，通过在预训练VLA骨干中仅添加少量可学习记忆token并使用时序截断反向传播（TBPTT）端到端训练，实现了最简形式的循环机制。在MIKASA-Robo上，成功率从0.42提升至0.84；在LIBERO上达96.2%不退化。研究标定了最小循环机制在部分可观测下的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA模型同时引入循环、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层级记忆或任务特定架构变更，导致循环本身的作用被其他机制混淆，缺乏对循环机制在VLA中独立贡献的受控隔离研究。

Method: 在Transformer中仅插入少量跨时间步携带的可学习记忆token，通过自注意力更新，使用截断反向传播（TBPTT）端到端训练，无辅助损失和架构改动，以记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K和更新规则（跨步梯度或解耦EMA）为唯一变量。

Result: MIKASA-Robo上训练任务成功率从0.42升至0.84，保留任务达0.23（基线0.07）；LIBERO上达96.2%无退化。

Conclusion: 标定了最小化骨干内循环的能力边界：在简单记忆需求场景下足够，在需要复杂记忆结构时仍接近基线，需额外记忆结构。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [192] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将基于机器学习的驾驶行为模型替代传统规则模型用于交通微观仿真，以提升事故频率预测的准确性。通过在英国利兹五个真实信号交叉口的实验，发现基于ML的模型生成的冲突数据结合极值理论能有效预测真实事故，而规则模型因缺乏校准无法实现有意义的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微观仿真采用简化规则模型，虽能合理复现交通流，但难以生成真实冲突动态，限制事故预测精度。ML行为模型可从大规模轨迹数据中直接学习人类驾驶行为，有望提升仿真真实性与事故预测能力。

Method: 对英国利兹五个真实信号交叉口进行微观仿真，分别使用标准规则模型和先进ML模型；采用二维Time-to-Collision指标识别仿真冲突，并基于极值理论预测事故频率。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突数据得出的事故预测与真实事故数据一致，而规则模型无法产生有意义预测；直接使用ML模型生成的仿真事故预测真实事故效果较差，表明ML模型能真实再现冲突但尚无法生成真实事故。

Conclusion: 基于ML的行为模型在无需位置特定校准的情况下，能通过仿真冲突改善事故预测，为ML在交通微观仿真中的应用指明了方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [193] [Viral Proteins Reveal Geometry of Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12609)
*Arthur Bigot,Harmon Bhasin,Core Francisco Park,Eugene Shakhnovich,Dianzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡的数据上训练，导致对低代表性序列（如病毒蛋白）的表示存在"自然性"主轴，该轴从细胞蛋白到病毒蛋白再到随机序列排列；缩放模型会不均匀地压缩这一轴，但嵌入仍保留病毒特异性信号，可线性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡训练数据下如何表征低代表性（underrepresented）的生物序列，以病毒蛋白为案例，理解模型对"非自然"或罕见序列的表示结构。

Method: 以病毒蛋白为案例，在ESM模型家族中分析嵌入空间结构，使用掩码重建困惑度（masked reconstruction perplexity）作为度量，研究序列从细胞蛋白到病毒蛋白再到随机序列的排列规律，并分析模型缩放对不同病毒家族的影响。

Result: 嵌入空间中存在一个主导的"自然性"主轴，与困惑度对齐；模型缩放不均匀地压缩该轴；尽管有这种压缩，病毒蛋白的嵌入仍然保留特异性信号，可在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外实现线性可分。

Conclusion: 蛋白质语言模型的表示由一种广义的"自然性"概念结构化，同时保留不同生物组特有的信息，说明模型在全局结构与局部特异性之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>


### [194] [The Mathematics of AI Winters: The mathematical Taxonomy of Paradigm Fragility in AI Winter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12610)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对前两次AI寒冬的补充性解释：除了工程失败和商业失望之外，主导范式还遇到了形式上的数学瓶颈，包括表示限制、优化困难、计算复杂性、统计可学习性和高维逼近问题。文章综合分析了感知机不可能结果、神经网络训练的计算复杂性、非参数估计的极小化极大速率、梯度消失分析以及统计学习理论，并将这些障碍与后来的突破联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对前两次AI寒冬的传统解释（工程失败、商业失望、期望过高）不够充分，需要从形式化数学瓶颈的角度提供补充性分析，以更全面地理解AI发展历程中的停滞与突破。

Method: 综合性分析方法（非档案式），整合了感知机不可能性结果、计算复杂性理论、非参数估计极小化极大速率、梯度消失分析以及统计学习理论等多个数学框架，论证这些形式化瓶颈与AI寒冬中核心失望事件之间的对齐关系。

Result: 证明了早期AI中的多次失望与数学上精密的瓶颈相一致，具体包括：Minsky-Papert的感知机限制、Blum-Rivest的神经网络训练计算复杂性、Stone的高维非参数估计极小化极大速率、Hochreiter和Bengio等的梯度消失分析，以及Vapnik-Chervonenkis/Valiant/Blumer等的统计学习理论。

Conclusion: 形式化的数学瓶颈确实与前两次AI寒冬中的核心挫折相对应；后来的突破只是缓解（而非消除）了这些障碍，这一视角有助于更深入地理解AI发展的内在规律。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>


### [195] [Evaluation of AutoML Frameworks for IDS under Imbalanced Data Conditions of the NSL-KDD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12611)
*Wiliane Carolina Silva,Evandro César Vilas Boas,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了9种开源AutoML框架在严重多类别不平衡的NSL-KDD网络入侵检测数据集上的性能，发现集成学习与不平衡感知优化能提升少数类检测能力，PyCaret以66% macro-F1最佳，而仅依赖准确率优化不足以处理稀有攻击类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常通过二分类或移除少数类来简化网络入侵检测中的类别不平衡问题，缺乏对真实多类别不平衡场景下AutoML框架性能的系统评估。

Method: 在NSL-KDD数据集上保留原始五类分布（含R2L、U2R等高度少数类），在统一可重复的实验协议下分析了9种AutoML框架的架构设计、集成策略、验证流程、超参数优化和不平衡处理机制。

Result: PyCaret取得66% macro-F1最佳性能，AutoGluon为55%；含集成学习与不平衡感知优化的框架更好区分少数类；缺乏原生平衡支持的框架在少数类检测上显著退化；仅以准确率为导向的优化不足以应对高度不平衡的入侵检测场景。

Conclusion: 建立了AutoML入侵检测在不平衡多分类场景下的标准化基准，指出现有架构局限，强调需将不平衡感知优化、重采样和分层评估策略原生集成到自动化学习流程中。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of severe class imbalance on the performance of automated machine learning (AutoML) frameworks for multiclass network intrusion detection using the NSL-KDD dataset. Unlike previous studies that simplify the problem through binary classification or minority-class removal, we preserve the original five-class distribution, including highly underrepresented attacks such as R2L and U2R, enabling a realistic evaluation of imbalance-sensitive learning behavior. Nine open-source AutoML frameworks were analyzed under a unified and reproducible experimental protocol, considering differences in architectural design, ensemble strategies, validation procedures, hyperparameter optimization, and imbalance-handling mechanisms. The results demonstrate that frameworks incorporating ensemble learning and imbalance-aware optimization achieve better minority-class discrimination. PyCaret obtained the best overall performance, reaching 66\% macro-F1, followed by AutoGluon with 55\%, whereas frameworks lacking native balancing support exhibited significant degradation in minority-class detection capability. The analysis further shows that accuracy-oriented optimization alone is insufficient for highly imbalanced IDS scenarios, since high-weighted metrics may coexist with poor generalization on rare attack categories. As a contribution, this work establishes a standardized benchmark for AutoML-based intrusion detection under severe multiclass imbalance, highlighting current architectural limitations and the need for native integration of imbalance-aware optimization, resampling, and stratified evaluation strategies into automated learning pipelines. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [196] [Towards Provably Fair Machine Learning: Bayesian Approaches For Consistent and Transparent Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12615)
*Owen O'Neill,Fintan Costello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出公平贝叶斯分类器，通过强制在每一个子组上同时满足确定性和统计一致性（贝叶斯最优目标分布）来实现精确预测，并在无法做出一致确定性预测时选择弃权。实验表明该方法在三个基准数据集上实现了零一致性错误，同时超过了基线准确率和多校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML分类器在高风险领域中对细粒度交叉子组的预测质量不一致，正则化加剧了小群体的预测偏差，影响了人口统计少数群体。需要一种原则性的方法来解决子组级别的统计一致性问题。

Method: 定义了两个一致性要求：确定性（相同个体得到相同预测）和统计一致性（在显著性水平α下无法拒绝子组预测来自贝叶斯最优目标分布的假设）。由此推导出公平贝叶斯分类器，对所有组和子组同时强制满足这两个要求，并在无法做出一致确定性预测时弃权。

Result: 在Adult、COMPAS和Bank Marketing三个基准数据集上，标准分类器对大量子组产生统计不一致的预测。公平贝叶斯分类器实现了零一致性错误，同时超过了基线准确率和多校准性能。

Conclusion: 统计一致性为预测质量提供了原则性基础，对算法公平性有直接影响。通过在最细粒度上强制贝叶斯一致性，该分类器证明了带有原则性弃权的穷举子组公平性在实践中是可以实现的。

Abstract: ML classifiers deployed in high-stakes domains produce predictions whose quality varies systematically across subgroups. For granular subgroups defined by intersections of multiple features, predictions are often inconsistent with the observed data: the model's outputs contradict the evidence available for that subgroup. This problem is exacerbated by regularisation, which improves aggregate performance by collapsing small subgroups into larger groups, disproportionately affecting demographic minorities. We define two requirements for consistent prediction: determinism (identical individuals receive identical predictions) and statistical consistency (we cannot reject, at significance level alpha, the hypothesis that the predictions for a subgroup were drawn from the Bayesian optimal target distribution inferred for that subgroup). From these requirements we derive the Fair Bayesian classifier, which enforces both across every group and subgroup simultaneously and abstains whenever no consistent deterministic prediction is possible. On three benchmark datasets (Adult, COMPAS, and Bank Marketing), standard classifiers produce statistically inconsistent predictions for a substantial proportion of subgroups. Our classifier achieves zero consistency error by construction while exceeding baseline accuracy and multicalibration on every dataset tested. Statistical consistency provides a principled foundation for prediction quality with direct implications for algorithmic fairness. Minority demographics are disproportionately concentrated in small subgroups, precisely where frequentist inference is least reliable; addressing this inference problem is therefore a necessary step toward fair ML. By enforcing Bayesian consistency at the finest resolution the data supports, the our classifier demonstrates that exhaustive subgroup fairness with principled abstention is achievable in practice.

</details>


### [197] [Bag of Dims: Training-Free Mechanistic Interpretability via Dimension-Level Sign Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12629)
*Varun Reddy Nalagatla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Bag of Dims"框架，证明Transformer隐藏状态的标准基（standard basis）本身就是一种无需训练、架构通用的特征基。每个隐藏维度通过符号编码语义、通过幅值编码置信度，作为独立的二元寄存器工作。通过四个渐进实验在三个模型族上验证了该框架：符号模式本身具有预测能力、符号模式组织成语义特征、这些特征在注意力投影中同样存在、静态FFN权重分析与特征关联。最终实现全无监督发现1500个特征，100%产出率，99%稀疏度，跨维度互信息仅0.0014比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer隐藏状态的标准基是否本身就能作为可解释的特征基，而无需额外的训练、优化或降维技术。

Method: 通过四个渐进实验验证：1) 符号模式预测能力实验（用单位幅值替换所有幅值测试LM头精度、纯汉明打分实验）；2) 单token类型缓存实验（无上下文，通过维度符号一致性发现语义类别）；3) 注意力投影中的特征发现；4) 静态FFN权重分析与神经元投票关联。

Result: 符号模式在无LM头纯汉明打分下达到80-90% top-4096精度；无训练发现175个类别，平均AUC 0.80；有训练探针仅提升+0.018 AUC且收敛到轴对齐权重；175个类别在K和V投影中均可发现；20%特征与单个写神经元关联>0.70；无监督发现1500个特征，100%产出，99%稀疏度，跨维度互信息0.0014比特。

Conclusion: Transformer隐藏状态的标准基足以用于特征读取，贯穿整个计算通路，无需训练、优化或大量GPU计算，仅需每个词汇token单次前向传播。

Abstract: We show that the standard basis of transformer hidden states already provides a training-free, architecture-general feature basis. Individual dimensions encode semantic content via their signs and confidence via their magnitudes, functioning as independent binary registers. We validate this Bag of Dims framework across three model families (Qwen 3.5-4B, Gemma 3-4B, Mistral 7B) through four progressive experiments. Sign patterns alone carry predictive content: replacing all magnitudes with unity achieves 72-93% top-5 next-token accuracy through the LM head, and pure Hamming scoring without any decoder reaches 80-90% top-4096. These sign patterns organize into semantic features: using a single-token type cache (one forward pass per vocabulary token, no context), we discover 175 categories via per-dimension sign consistency (mean AUC 0.80) from 50 anchors with zero training. A trained probe adds only +0.018 AUC and converges to axis-aligned weights, confirming negligible cross-dimension structure. This structure extends to attention: all 175 categories remain discoverable in K and V projections. On the write side, static FFN weight inspection links 20% of features to individual writer neurons (>0.70 agreement; random controls: 0%), with top-200 neuron coalitions achieving >0.70 agreement on 99.9% of prototypes via majority vote. Fully unsupervised discovery (random seeds, no labels) scales to 1500 features at 100% yield and 99% sparsity across all three models, with pairwise MI of 0.0014 bits confirming low inter-dimension coupling. These results establish that the standard basis already suffices for feature reading throughout the transformer compute pathway, requiring no training, no optimization, and no GPU-days beyond a single forward pass per vocabulary token.

</details>


### [198] [Keep Policy Gradient in Charge: Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12634)
*Tianyu Ding,Jianhong Xin,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGCD通过蒸馏进行信用分配而非作为竞争性演员损失，利用动态采样的成功/失败兄弟轨迹对比，结合外部LLM生成步骤级信用参考，在保持工具使用能力的同时提升GRPO在AppWorld和τ³-airline上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野工具使用强化学习从结果验证中学习，但其轨迹级优势被广播到大量推理、API和回答令牌上。直接令牌级自蒸馏会静默破坏工具使用能力，因为它重复教师行为却不知道验证器奖励哪些动作，导致有用技能和有害捷径被同时放大。

Method: SGCD包含四个关键组件：1) 动态采样产生混合成功与失败的兄弟轨迹；2) 外部LLM总结对比生成训练专用步骤级信用参考；3) 密集师生散度驱动信用重新分配；4) 有界分离信用权重重塑GRPO令牌优势。部署的学生无需外部LLM、兄弟证据或预言机。

Result: 在AppWorld上，TGC从42.9提升到45.6（test_normal），从24.7提升到27.0（test_challenge）；在τ³-airline上，pass@1从0.583提升到0.602。

Conclusion: SGCD通过蒸馏实现更密集的信用分配信号，避免了直接自蒸馏对工具使用能力的破坏，在多个基准上超过匹配的GRPO比较器。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use reinforcement learning can learn from outcome verification, but its
  trajectory-level advantage is broadcast across many reasoning, API, and answer tokens.
  Self-distillation promises a denser signal by reusing a policy's own rollouts or a privileged
  teacher. We show, however, that direct token-level self-distillation can silently destroy tool use:
  it rehearses teacher behavior without knowing which actions the verifier rewards, so useful skills
  and harmful shortcuts are amplified together. We introduce Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation
  (SGCD), which uses distillation for credit assignment rather than as a competing actor loss.
  Dynamic sampling produces mixed successful and failed sibling rollouts; an external LLM summarizes
  their contrast into a training-only stepwise credit reference; dense teacher/student divergence
  drives credit reassignment; and bounded detached credit weights reshape GRPO token advantages. The
  deployed student sees no external LLM, sibling evidence, or oracle. Across AppWorld and
  $τ^3$-airline, SGCD improves over matched GRPO comparators: AppWorld TGC $42.9 \to 45.6$ on
  test_normal and $24.7 \to 27.0$ on test_challenge, and $τ^3$-airline pass@1 $0.583 \to 0.602$.

</details>
